import { useEffect, useId, useState } from "react";
import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import { isDpiValueAllowed } from "../api";
import type { AudioNode, AudioSnapshot, AvailabilityCode, Battery, Device, DpiCapability, HubApi, ProviderKind, Snapshot } from "../types";

export type RunMutation = (key: string, operation: () => Promise<Snapshot>) => Promise<boolean>;
export interface MutationControls {
  api: HubApi;
  runMutation: RunMutation;
  pending: ReadonlySet<string>;
}

export const providerNames: Record<ProviderKind, string> = {
  openrazer: "OpenRazer", ratbag: "ratbagd", upower: "UPower",
  headsetcontrol: "HeadsetControl", pipewire: "PipeWire", usb: "USB", app: "Applicazione",
};

export const availabilityNames: Record<AvailabilityCode, string> = {
  available: "Disponibile", absent: "Non presente", unsupported: "Non supportato",
  providerMissing: "Provider assente", permissionDenied: "Permesso negato", unknown: "Da verificare",
  unsupportedPlatform: "Piattaforma non supportata",
};

export function deviceStatus(device: Device): { label: string; tone: string } {
  if (device.availability !== "available") return { label: availabilityNames[device.availability], tone: "muted" };
  if (device.support === "partial") return { label: "Supporto parziale", tone: "partial" };
  if (device.support === "unsupported") return { label: "Hardware non supportato", tone: "muted" };
  return { label: "Hardware disponibile", tone: "available" };
}

export function hasPending(pending: ReadonlySet<string>, prefix: string): boolean {
  return [...pending].some((key) => key.startsWith(prefix));
}

export function Toggle({ label, checked, disabled = false, description, onChange }: {
  label: string; checked: boolean; disabled?: boolean; description?: string | null; onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <label className={`toggle-row${disabled ? " is-disabled" : ""}`}>
      <span className="toggle-copy"><span>{label}</span>{description && <small id={`${id}-reason`}>{description}</small>}</span>
      <span className="toggle-switch">
        <input type="checkbox" role="switch" aria-label={label} checked={checked} disabled={disabled}
          aria-describedby={description ? `${id}-reason` : undefined} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.checked)} />
        <span className="toggle-track" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

function rangeStyle(value: number, min: number, max: number): CSSProperties {
  return { "--range-fill": `${max > min ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value - min) / (max - min) * 100)) : 0}%` } as CSSProperties;
}

export function DpiControl({ capability, value, label = "DPI desiderati", disabled = false, reason, onChange }: {
  capability: DpiCapability | null; value: number | null; label?: string; disabled?: boolean;
  reason?: string | null; onChange: (value: number) => void;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  const values = capability?.values;
  const known = values && values.kind !== "unknown" && (values.kind === "discrete" ? values.values.length > 0 : values.step > 0 && values.max >= values.min);
  const writable = known && capability?.writable && !disabled;
  const explanation = reason || capability?.reason || (!known ? "Le capabilities DPI non sono disponibili. Nessun intervallo viene presunto." : !capability?.writable ? "DPI in sola lettura per questo provider." : null);
  const valid = values !== undefined && isDpiValueAllowed(values, value);
  const valueLabel = value === null ? "Non letto" : `${value} DPI`;

  if (!known || !values) {
    return <div className="dpi-control"><span className="field-label">{label}</span><strong className="read-only-value">{valueLabel}</strong><p className="control-reason">{explanation}</p></div>;
  }

  const min = values.kind === "discrete" ? 0 : values.min;
  const max = values.kind === "discrete" ? values.values.length - 1 : values.max;
  const position = valid && value !== null ? values.kind === "discrete" ? values.values.indexOf(value) : value : null;

  return (
    <div className="dpi-control">
      <div className="field-heading"><label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label><output htmlFor={id}>{valueLabel}</output></div>
      {position !== null ? (
        <>
          <input id={id} className="range-input" type="range" min={min} max={max} step={values.kind === "discrete" ? 1 : values.step}
            value={position} aria-valuetext={`${value} DPI`} disabled={!writable}
            aria-describedby={explanation ? `${id}-reason` : `${id}-bounds`} style={rangeStyle(position, min, max)}
            onChange={(event) => {
              const next = Number(event.target.value);
              onChange(values.kind === "discrete" ? values.values[next] : next);
            }} />
          <div className="range-bounds" id={`${id}-bounds`}><span>{values.kind === "discrete" ? values.values[0] : min} DPI</span><span>{values.kind === "discrete" ? values.values[values.values.length - 1] : max} DPI</span></div>
          {values.kind === "discrete" && <p className="control-hint">Posizioni discrete: {values.values.join(" / ")} DPI</p>}
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="invalid-dpi">
          <p id={`${id}-invalid`} className="control-reason">{value === null ? "Nessuna lettura confermata." : "Valore letto fuori dai valori ammessi."} Scegli un DPI valido prima di applicare.</p>
          {writable && (values.kind === "discrete" ? (
            <select id={id} aria-label={`Scegli ${label}`} aria-describedby={`${id}-invalid`} value="" onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))}>
              <option value="" disabled>Scegli un valore valido</option>
              {values.values.map((dpi) => <option key={dpi} value={dpi}>{dpi} DPI</option>)}
            </select>
          ) : (
            <input id={id} type="number" aria-label={`Scegli ${label}`} aria-describedby={`${id}-invalid`} min={values.min} max={values.max} step={values.step}
              placeholder={`${values.min} - ${values.max}`} onChange={(event) => {
                const next = event.target.value === "" ? null : Number(event.target.value);
                if (isDpiValueAllowed(values, next) && next !== null) onChange(next);
              }} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {explanation && <p id={`${id}-reason`} className="control-reason">{explanation}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function OutputSelector({ outputs, value, onChange, disabled = false, label = "Uscita audio" }: {
  outputs: AudioNode[]; value: string; onChange: (value: string) => void; disabled?: boolean; label?: string;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className="select-field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <select id={id} value={value} disabled={disabled || outputs.length === 0} aria-describedby={outputs.length === 0 ? `${id}-empty` : undefined} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}>
        {!outputs.some((output) => output.nodeName === value) && <option value="">{outputs.length ? "Scegli un'uscita" : "Nessuna uscita rilevata"}</option>}
        {[false, true].map((virtual) => {
          const nodes = outputs.filter((output) => output.isVirtual === virtual);
          return nodes.length > 0 && <optgroup key={String(virtual)} label={virtual ? "Uscite virtuali" : "Uscite fisiche"}>
            {nodes.map((output) => <option key={output.nodeName} value={output.nodeName}>{output.description || output.nodeName}{virtual ? " [virtuale]" : " [fisica]"}</option>)}
          </optgroup>;
        })}
      </select>
      {outputs.length === 0 && <small className="control-reason" id={`${id}-empty`}>Il backend non espone uscite audio.</small>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function NodeControls({ node, input = false, available, api, pending, runMutation }: MutationControls & {
  node: AudioNode; input?: boolean; available: boolean;
}) {
  const id = useId();
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(node.volume);
  useEffect(() => { setDraft(node.volume); }, [node.nodeName, node.volume]);
  const busy = hasPending(pending, "audio/");
  const readable = node.volume !== null && Number.isFinite(node.volume) && node.volume >= 0;
  const percent = draft === null ? null : Math.round(draft * 100);
  const actualPercent = node.volume === null ? null : Math.round(node.volume * 100);
  const canApply = readable && draft !== null && draft >= 0 && draft <= 1 && draft !== node.volume;
  const reason = !available ? "Il servizio audio non e' disponibile." : !readable ? "Volume non leggibile: il nodo rimane in sola lettura." : busy ? "Operazione audio in corso." : null;
  const label = input ? "Guadagno microfono" : "Volume uscita";
  return (
    <div className="node-controls">
      <div className="field-heading"><label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label><output htmlFor={id}>{percent === null ? "Non disponibile" : `${percent}%`}</output></div>
      <input id={id} className="range-input" type="range" min={0} max={100} step={1}
        value={Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent ?? 0))} disabled={!available || !readable || busy}
        aria-valuetext={percent === null ? "Volume non disponibile" : `${percent}%`} aria-describedby={`${id}-readback`}
        style={rangeStyle(percent ?? 0, 0, 100)} onChange={(event) => setDraft(Number(event.target.value) / 100)} />
      <div className="range-bounds"><span>0%</span><span>100%</span></div>
      <p className="control-hint" id={`${id}-readback`}>Lettura confermata: {actualPercent === null ? "non disponibile" : `${actualPercent}%`}.{reason ? ` ${reason}` : ""}{actualPercent !== null && actualPercent > 100 ? " Scegli un valore entro 100% prima di applicare." : ""}</p>
      <button className="button button-secondary button-small" disabled={!available || busy || !canApply} onClick={() => {
        if (draft !== null) void runMutation(`audio/volume/${node.nodeName}`, () => api.setVolume(node.nodeName, draft));
      }}>{busy ? "Attendi..." : input ? "Applica guadagno" : "Applica volume"}</button>
      <Toggle label={input ? "Silenzia microfono" : "Silenzia uscita"} checked={node.muted === true}
        disabled={!available || busy || node.muted === null}
        description={node.muted === null ? "Stato mute non leggibile." : !available ? "Servizio audio non disponibile." : null}
        onChange={(muted) => { void runMutation(`audio/mute/${node.nodeName}`, () => api.setMute(node.nodeName, muted)); }} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function SurroundControl({ audio, api, pending, runMutation }: MutationControls & { audio: AudioSnapshot }) {
  const busy = hasPending(pending, "audio/");
  const transitioning = audio.surroundState === "starting" || audio.surroundState === "stopping";
  const on = audio.surroundState === "on";
  const disabled = busy || transitioning || (!on && (!audio.available || !audio.canEnableSurround));
  const stateLabels = { off: "Disattivato", starting: "Attivazione in corso", on: "Attivo", stopping: "Disattivazione in corso", error: "Non attivo: errore" };
  const reason = transitioning || busy ? "Attendi la lettura confermata dal backend." : audio.surroundReason || (!audio.available ? "PipeWire non disponibile." : !audio.canEnableSurround && !on ? "Il backend non consente l'attivazione per questo target." : null);
  return (
    <div className={`surround-control${on ? " surround-on" : ""}`}>
      <div className="surround-heading"><span className="spatial-number" aria-hidden="true">7<span>.1</span></span><span><strong>Surround binaurale</strong><small className={on ? "accent-text" : ""} role="status">{stateLabels[audio.surroundState]}</small></span></div>
      <Toggle label="Surround 7.1" checked={on} disabled={disabled} description={reason || "Ingresso a 8 canali, uscita stereo con HRTF."}
        onChange={(enabled) => { void runMutation("audio/surround", () => api.setSurround(enabled)); }} />
    </div>
  );
}

function batteryTime(timestamp: number | null): string {
  if (timestamp === null || !Number.isFinite(timestamp)) return "non disponibile";
  return new Date(timestamp * 1000).toLocaleString("it-IT", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
}

export function BatteryReadout({ battery, compact = false }: { battery: Battery | null; compact?: boolean }) {
  if (battery === null) return <div className={`battery-readout${compact ? " compact" : ""}`}><span className="battery-empty">Batteria non esposta</span>{!compact && <p className="control-hint">Nessuna misura associata con certezza a questo dispositivo.</p>}</div>;
  const percentage = battery.percentage !== null && Number.isFinite(battery.percentage) && battery.percentage >= 0 && battery.percentage <= 100 ? battery.percentage : null;
  const levels = { unknown: "Non disponibile", critical: "Critico", low: "Basso", normal: "Normale", high: "Alto", full: "Pieno" };
  const level = battery.level !== null ? levels[battery.level] : "Non disponibile";
  return (
    <div className={`battery-readout${compact ? " compact" : ""}`}>
      <div className="battery-heading"><strong>{percentage !== null ? `${Math.round(percentage)}%` : level}</strong>{percentage === null && battery.level && battery.level !== "unknown" && <span className="tiny-label">Livello approssimativo</span>}{battery.stale && <span className="status-pill partial">Dato precedente</span>}</div>
      {percentage !== null && !compact && <meter className="battery-meter" min={0} max={100} value={percentage} aria-label="Percentuale batteria" />}
      {battery.charging === true && <small>In carica</small>}
      <small className="battery-source">Fonte: {providerNames[battery.source]}{compact ? ` / ${battery.stale ? "precedente" : "letto"} ${batteryTime(battery.observedAt)}` : ""}</small>
      {!compact && <dl className="metadata-list"><div><dt>Osservato</dt><dd>{batteryTime(battery.observedAt)}</dd></div><div><dt>Aggiornato dalla fonte</dt><dd>{batteryTime(battery.sourceUpdatedAt)}</dd></div></dl>}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useId } from "react";
import type { Device, DeviceKind } from "../types";
import { BatteryReadout, deviceStatus, providerNames } from "./Controls";

export function DeviceIllustration({ kind, className = "" }: { kind: DeviceKind; className?: string }) {
  const id = useId().replaceAll(":", "");
  const mouse = kind === "mouse";
  return (
    <svg className={`device-illustration ${className}`} viewBox="0 0 240 200" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
      <defs>
        <linearGradient id={`shell-${id}`} x1="70" y1="20" x2="174" y2="175" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><stop stopColor="#343434" /><stop offset="0.5" stopColor="#202020" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#151515" /></linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id={`edge-${id}`} x1="75" y1="30" x2="160" y2="170" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><stop stopColor="#b0b0b0" /><stop offset="0.5" stopColor="#555" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#363636" /></linearGradient>
        <radialGradient id={`floor-${id}`}><stop stopColor="#44d62c" stopOpacity="0.12" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#44d62c" stopOpacity="0" /></radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <ellipse cx="120" cy="179" rx="84" ry="13" fill={`url(#floor-${id})`} />
      <path d="M30 158H49M191 158H210M120 9V16" stroke="#414141" strokeWidth="1" />
      {mouse ? (
        <g transform="rotate(-12 120 103)">
          <path d="M121 24C91 24 78 46 73 79L67 127C64 158 86 179 120 179C153 179 177 159 173 127L167 79C162 45 150 24 121 24Z" fill={`url(#shell-${id})`} stroke={`url(#edge-${id})`} strokeWidth="1.5" />
          <path d="M120 27V89M78 80C91 84 103 86 120 87C136 87 151 83 165 79M120 89V100" stroke="#696969" strokeWidth="1.1" />
          <path d="M86 53C80 70 78 94 77 108M154 51C159 67 162 92 163 111" stroke="#ddd" strokeOpacity="0.25" />

          <circle cx="120" cy="122" r="3" stroke="#444" />
        </g>
      ) : kind === "headset" ? (

          <rect x="158" y="90" width="38" height="65" rx="15" transform="rotate(8 158 90)" fill={`url(#shell-${id})`} stroke={`url(#edge-${id})`} strokeWidth="1.5" />

          <path d="M52 145C41 168 62 181 90 172" stroke="#777" strokeWidth="3" strokeLinecap="round" />
          <rect x="85" y="167" width="14" height="7" rx="3.5" transform="rotate(-14 85 167)" fill="#404040" stroke="#44d62c" strokeWidth="1" />
        </g>
      ) : (
        <g><rect x="79" y="54" width="82" height="99" rx="13" fill={`url(#shell-${id})`} stroke={`url(#edge-${id})`} /><path d="M106 54V37H134V54" stroke="#777" strokeWidth="2" /><circle cx="120" cy="100" r="16" stroke="#444" /><circle cx="120" cy="100" r="5" fill="#44d62c" /><path d="M97 132H143" stroke="#555" /></g>

export function DeviceCard({ device, selected, onOpen }: { device: Device; selected: boolean; onOpen: (device: Device) => void }) {
  const status = deviceStatus(device);
  const actionable = device.kind === "mouse" || device.kind === "headset";
  const brand = device.brand === "razer" ? "Razer" : device.brand === "logitech" ? "Logitech" : "Periferica";
  return (
    <article className={`device-card${selected ? " selected" : ""}`} data-testid="device-card">
      <button type="button" className="device-card-button" aria-label={`Apri controlli di ${device.name}`} disabled={!actionable} onClick={() => onOpen(device)}>
        <div className="device-card-top"><span className="tiny-label">{brand} / {device.kind === "mouse" ? "Mouse" : device.kind === "headset" ? "Cuffie" : "Ricevitore"}</span><span className={`status-dot ${status.tone}`} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
        <DeviceIllustration kind={device.kind} />
        <div className="device-card-copy"><h3>{device.name}</h3><span className={`status-pill ${status.tone}`}>{status.label}</span><p>{providerNames[device.provider]}{device.identity.transport ? ` / ${device.identity.transport}` : ""}</p></div>
        <div className="device-card-bottom"><BatteryReadout battery={device.values.battery} compact /><span className="card-arrow" aria-hidden="true">{actionable ? ">" : ""}</span></div>
      </button>
    </article>
  );
}

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createDemoApi, createHubApi, createTauriApi, isDpiValueAllowed } from "../api";
import type { AudioProfile, Snapshot } from "../types";

const ipc = vi.hoisted(() => ({ invoke: vi.fn(), isTauri: vi.fn(), listen: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("@tauri-apps/api/core", () => ({ invoke: ipc.invoke, isTauri: ipc.isTauri }));
vi.mock("@tauri-apps/api/event", () => ({ listen: ipc.listen }));

beforeEach(() => {
  ipc.invoke.mockReset();
  ipc.isTauri.mockReset().mockReturnValue(true);
  ipc.listen.mockReset().mockResolvedValue(() => {});
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
});

describe("contratti IPC Tauri", () => {
  it("invoca i comandi esatti con gli argomenti camelCase, senza path HRTF dal frontend", async () => {
    const snapshot = await createDemoApi().getSnapshot();
    ipc.invoke.mockResolvedValue(snapshot);
    const api = createTauriApi();
    const profile = snapshot.profiles[0];
    await api.getSnapshot();
    await api.refreshDevices();
    await api.setDpi("mouse-1", 800, 1600);
    await api.setPollingRate("mouse-1", 250);
    await api.setDefaultOutput("alsa_output.test");
    await api.setVolume("alsa_input.test", 0.42);
    await api.setMute("alsa_input.test", true);
    await api.setSurround(true);
    await api.importHrtf();
    await api.saveAudioProfile(profile);
    await api.applyAudioProfile(profile.id);
    ipc.invoke.mockResolvedValueOnce(undefined);
    await api.exitApp();
    expect(ipc.invoke.mock.calls).toEqual([
      ["get_snapshot"], ["refresh_devices"], ["set_dpi", { deviceId: "mouse-1", x: 800, y: 1600 }],
      ["set_polling_rate", { deviceId: "mouse-1", rate: 250 }], ["set_default_output", { nodeName: "alsa_output.test" }],
      ["set_volume", { nodeName: "alsa_input.test", volume: 0.42 }], ["set_mute", { nodeName: "alsa_input.test", muted: true }],
      ["set_surround", { enabled: true }], ["import_hrtf"], ["save_audio_profile", { profile }],
      ["apply_audio_profile", { profileId: profile.id }], ["exit_app"],
    ]);
  });

  it("sottoscrive snapshot-changed e restituisce il cleanup Tauri", async () => {
    const stop = vi.fn();
    ipc.listen.mockResolvedValue(stop);
    const listener = vi.fn();
    const unsubscribe = await createTauriApi().subscribe(listener);
    const snapshot = await createDemoApi(0).getSnapshot();
    expect(ipc.listen).toHaveBeenCalledWith("snapshot-changed", expect.any(Function));
    ipc.listen.mock.calls[0][1]({ event: "snapshot-changed", id: 1, payload: snapshot });
    expect(listener).toHaveBeenCalledWith(snapshot);
    unsubscribe();
    expect(stop).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it("nel browser comunica unsupportedPlatform senza invocare IPC o attivare demo", async () => {
    ipc.isTauri.mockReturnValue(false);
    const api = createTauriApi();
    const snapshot = await api.getSnapshot();
    expect(snapshot).toMatchObject({ platform: "unsupportedPlatform", demo: false, devices: [], audio: { available: false } });
    expect(snapshot.diagnostics[0].message).toContain("backend Tauri");
    await expect(api.setDpi("mouse", 800, 800)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "unsupportedPlatform" });
    await api.subscribe(() => {});
    expect(ipc.invoke).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(ipc.listen).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("conserva l'errore del backend e non ripiega su dati demo", async () => {
    const failure = { code: "providerMissing", message: "OpenRazer non disponibile.", detail: "Session bus non raggiungibile." };
    ipc.invoke.mockRejectedValue(failure);
    const api = createTauriApi();
    await expect(api.getSnapshot()).rejects.toEqual(failure);
    expect(api.demo).toBe(false);
  });

  it("la demo richiede esattamente VITE_DEMO=true e accetta query 0/1/3", async () => {
    ipc.isTauri.mockReturnValue(false);
    vi.stubEnv("VITE_DEMO", "1");
    expect((await createHubApi().getSnapshot()).demo).toBe(false);
    vi.stubEnv("VITE_DEMO", "true");
    for (const count of [0, 1, 3]) {
      window.history.replaceState(null, "", `/?devices=${count}`);
      const snapshot = await createHubApi().getSnapshot();
      expect(snapshot.demo).toBe(true);
      expect(snapshot.devices).toHaveLength(count);
    }
    window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?devices=2");
    expect((await createHubApi().getSnapshot()).devices).toHaveLength(3);
  });
});

describe("adapter demo esplicito", () => {
  it("fornisce istanze dinamiche univoche e non inventa batterie Logitech/cuffie", async () => {
    const snapshot = await createDemoApi(6).getSnapshot();
    expect(snapshot.devices).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(new Set(snapshot.devices.map((device) => device.id)).size).toBe(6);
    expect(new Set(snapshot.devices.map((device) => device.identity.serial)).size).toBe(6);
    for (const device of snapshot.devices.filter((entry) => entry.kind === "headset" || entry.brand === "logitech")) expect(device.values.battery).toBeNull();
    expect(snapshot.devices[0].values.battery?.charging).toBe(false);
    expect(snapshot.devices[0].values.battery?.observedAt).toBeLessThanOrEqual(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
    expect(snapshot.devices[0].values.battery?.observedAt).toBeGreaterThan(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - 5);
    expect(snapshot.devices[1]).toMatchObject({ provider: "usb", support: "partial", capabilities: { dpi: null, polling: null } });
  });

  it("valida range e liste DPI, assi e liste polling senza accettare frequenze universali", async () => {
    const api = createDemoApi();
    const initial = await api.getSnapshot();
    const viper = initial.devices[0];
    const g305 = initial.devices[2];
    await expect(api.setDpi(viper.id, 850, 850)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "demoValidation" });
    await expect(api.setPollingRate(viper.id, 250)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "demoValidation" });
    await expect(api.setDpi(g305.id, 1600, 3200)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "demoValidation" });
    await expect(api.setDpi(g305.id, 1500, 1500)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "demoValidation" });
    expect((await api.getSnapshot()).revision).toBe(initial.revision);
    const dpiResult = await api.setDpi(viper.id, 1200, 1600);
    expect(dpiResult.devices[0].values.dpi).toEqual({ x: 1200, y: 1600 });
    const rateResult = await api.setPollingRate(g305.id, 250);
    expect(rateResult.devices[2].values.pollingRate).toBe(250);
    expect(rateResult.revision).toBe(initial.revision + 2);
  });

  it("emette snapshot indipendenti e valida volume/mute sul nodo reale della demo", async () => {
    const api = createDemoApi(0);
    const events: Snapshot[] = [];
    const unsubscribe = await api.subscribe((snapshot) => events.push(snapshot));
    const initial = await api.getSnapshot();
    const microphone = initial.audio.inputs[0];
    await api.setVolume(microphone.nodeName, 0.35);
    await api.setMute(microphone.nodeName, true);
    expect(events).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(events[0].audio.inputs[0].muted).toBe(false);
    expect(events[1].audio.inputs[0]).toMatchObject({ volume: 0.35, muted: true });
    await expect(api.setVolume(microphone.nodeName, 1.5)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "demoValidation" });
    await expect(api.setMute("nodo-assente", true)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "demoValidation" });
    unsubscribe();
    await api.refreshDevices();
    expect(events).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("salvare un profilo non lo applica, e il volume 7.1 non aumenta il volume fisico", async () => {
    const api = createDemoApi();
    const initial = await api.getSnapshot();
    const target = initial.audio.outputs.find((node) => node.nodeName === initial.audio.selectedOutput)!;
    const profile: AudioProfile = { ...initial.profiles[0], id: "f20b029d-71cb-4c2c-a357-49a3068d8a8e", name: "Spaziale", volume: 0.95, surroundRequested: true };
    const saved = await api.saveAudioProfile(profile);
    expect(saved.audio.surroundState).toBe("off");
    expect(saved.audio.outputs.find((node) => node.nodeName === target.nodeName)?.volume).toBe(target.volume);
    const applied = await api.applyAudioProfile(profile.id);
    expect(applied.audio.surroundState).toBe("on");
    expect(applied.audio.outputs.find((node) => node.nodeName === target.nodeName)?.volume).toBe(target.volume);
    expect(applied.audio.outputs.find((node) => node.nodeName === applied.audio.defaultOutput)).toMatchObject({ isVirtual: true, volume: 0.95 });
    const off = await api.setSurround(false);
    expect(off.audio.outputs.every((node) => !node.isVirtual)).toBe(true);
    expect(off.audio.defaultOutput).toBe(target.nodeName);
  });

  it("riconosce solo DPI effettivamente ammessi, inclusi valori fuori elenco", () => {
    expect(isDpiValueAllowed({ kind: "discrete", values: [400, 900, 1800] }, 900)).toBe(true);
    expect(isDpiValueAllowed({ kind: "discrete", values: [400, 900, 1800] }, 800)).toBe(false);
    expect(isDpiValueAllowed({ kind: "range", min: 100, max: 30000, step: 100 }, 30000)).toBe(true);
    expect(isDpiValueAllowed({ kind: "range", min: 100, max: 30000, step: 100 }, 30001)).toBe(false);
    expect(isDpiValueAllowed({ kind: "unknown" }, 800)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("importare una HRTF disattiva il filtro e non riattiva effetti o cambia il volume fisico", async () => {
    const api = createDemoApi();
    const on = await api.setSurround(true);
    const target = on.audio.outputs.find((node) => node.nodeName === on.audio.selectedOutput)!;
    const imported = await api.importHrtf();
    expect(imported.audio.surroundState).toBe("off");
    expect(imported.audio.outputs.every((node) => !node.isVirtual)).toBe(true);
    expect(imported.audio.selectedOutput).toBe(target.nodeName);
    expect(imported.audio.outputs.find((node) => node.nodeName === target.nodeName)?.volume).toBe(target.volume);
    expect(imported.audio.hrtf?.reference).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}\.sofa$/);
    const previousProfile = await api.applyAudioProfile(imported.profiles[0].id);
    expect(previousProfile.audio.hrtf?.reference).toBe("system");
  });
});

import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import App from "../App";
import { createDemoApi, createTauriApi } from "../api";
import type { HubApi, Snapshot } from "../types";

function clone(snapshot: Snapshot): Snapshot { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(snapshot)) as Snapshot; }

function deferred<T>() {
  let resolve!: (value: T) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<T>((done) => { resolve = done; });
  return { promise, resolve };
}

function controlledApi(initial: Snapshot) {
  let current = clone(initial);
  const listeners = new Set<(snapshot: Snapshot) => void>();
  const api: HubApi = {
    ...createDemoApi(), demo: initial.demo,
    getSnapshot: vi.fn(async () => clone(current)),
    subscribe: vi.fn(async (listener: (snapshot: Snapshot) => void) => {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => { listeners.delete(listener); };
    }),
  };
  return { api, emit(next: Snapshot) { current = clone(next); for (const listener of listeners) listener(clone(next)); } };
}

async function dashboard(api: HubApi) {
  render(<App api={api} />);
  await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /Il tuo setup/ });
}

describe("revisioni, hotplug e lifecycle", () => {
  it("ignora eventi e risposte mutazione piu vecchi della snapshot confermata", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const initial = await createDemoApi(1).getSnapshot();

    act(() => emit(latest));
    const older = clone(initial);
    older.revision = 8;
    older.devices[0].values.dpi = { x: 3200, y: 3200 };
    act(() => emit(older));
    await act(async () => { write.resolve(older); await write.promise; });

  });

  it("non sovrascrive un evento recente con una lettura iniziale tardiva", async () => {

  });

  it("unplug del mouse selezionato passa al successivo senza valori della bozza precedente", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const initial = await createDemoApi().getSnapshot();
    const { api, emit } = controlledApi(initial);
    await dashboard(api);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("tab", { name: /Mouse/ }));
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "DPI desiderati" }), { target: { value: "1200" } });
    const next = clone(initial);
    next.revision = 2;
    next.devices = next.devices.filter((device) => device.id !== "demo-viper-1");
    act(() => emit(next));
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Logitech G305" })).toBeVisible();
    expect(screen.getByRole("slider", { name: "DPI desiderati" })).toHaveValue("2");
    next.revision = 3;
    next.devices = [];
    act(() => emit(next));
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Nessun mouse rilevato" })).toBeVisible();
  });

  it("StrictMode pulisce anche una sottoscrizione risolta dopo l'unmount", async () => {
    const api = createDemoApi(0);
    const first = deferred<() => void>();
    const second = deferred<() => void>();
    const stopFirst = vi.fn();
    const stopSecond = vi.fn();
    const subscribe = vi.spyOn(api, "subscribe").mockReturnValueOnce(first.promise).mockReturnValueOnce(second.promise);
    const { unmount } = render(<StrictMode><App api={api} /></StrictMode>);
    await waitFor(() => expect(subscribe).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2));
    await act(async () => { first.resolve(stopFirst); second.resolve(stopSecond); await Promise.all([first.promise, second.promise]); });
    expect(stopFirst).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(stopSecond).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    unmount();
    expect(stopSecond).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { isDpiValueAllowed } from "../api";
import type { Device } from "../types";
import { BatteryReadout, DpiControl, Toggle, deviceStatus, hasPending, providerNames } from "../components/Controls";
import type { MutationControls } from "../components/Controls";
import { DeviceIllustration } from "../components/DeviceCard";

export function MouseControls({ device, compact = false, api, pending, runMutation }: MutationControls & { device: Device; compact?: boolean }) {
  const [draftDpi, setDraftDpi] = useState(device.values.dpi);
  const [linked, setLinked] = useState(true);
  const [draftRate, setDraftRate] = useState(device.values.pollingRate);
  useEffect(() => { setDraftDpi(device.values.dpi); }, [device.id, device.values.dpi?.x, device.values.dpi?.y]);
  useEffect(() => { setDraftRate(device.values.pollingRate); }, [device.id, device.values.pollingRate]);
  useEffect(() => { setLinked(true); }, [device.id]);
  const dpi = device.capabilities.dpi;
  const polling = device.capabilities.polling;
  const busy = hasPending(pending, `mouse/${device.id}/`);
  const hardwareAvailable = device.availability === "available" && device.support !== "unsupported";
  const canWriteDpi = hardwareAvailable && dpi?.writable === true;
  const canWritePolling = hardwareAvailable && polling?.writable === true;
  const availabilityReason = !hardwareAvailable ? `${deviceStatus(device).label}. I controlli restano in sola lettura.` : null;
  const validDpi = dpi && draftDpi && isDpiValueAllowed(dpi.values, draftDpi.x) && isDpiValueAllowed(dpi.values, draftDpi.y) && (dpi.separateAxes || draftDpi.x === draftDpi.y);
  const dpiChanged = draftDpi?.x !== device.values.dpi?.x || draftDpi?.y !== device.values.dpi?.y;
  const rateChanged = draftRate !== device.values.pollingRate;
  const validRate = draftRate !== null && polling?.values.includes(draftRate);
  const dpiReason = busy ? "Scrittura del mouse in corso. L'audio rimane indipendente." : availabilityReason;
  const confirmed = device.values.dpi;

  function changeDpi(axis: "x" | "y", value: number) {
    if (linked || !dpi?.separateAxes || compact) setDraftDpi({ x: value, y: value });
    else setDraftDpi((current) => ({ x: axis === "x" ? value : current?.x ?? value, y: axis === "y" ? value : current?.y ?? value }));
  }

  const pollingControls = (
    <>
      {!compact && <div className="section-heading"><div><span className="eyebrow">02 / Comunicazione</span><h2>Polling</h2></div><span className="unit-tag">Hz</span></div>}
      <p className="readback-line">Lettura confermata: <strong>{device.values.pollingRate === null ? "non disponibile" : `${device.values.pollingRate} Hz`}</strong></p>
      {polling && polling.values.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="rate-options" role="group" aria-label="Frequenza polling desiderata">
          {polling.values.map((rate) => <button key={rate} type="button" aria-pressed={draftRate === rate} disabled={busy || !canWritePolling} onClick={() => setDraftRate(rate)}>{rate}<small>Hz</small></button>)}
        </div>
      ) : <p className="control-reason">Nessun elenco di frequenze supportate. Nessun intervallo universale viene presunto.</p>}
      {device.values.pollingRate !== null && polling && !polling.values.includes(device.values.pollingRate) && <p className="control-reason">La frequenza letta non e' nell'elenco del provider. Scegli una frequenza valida prima di applicare.</p>}
      {(availabilityReason || polling?.reason || !polling?.writable) && <p className="control-reason">{availabilityReason || polling?.reason || "Polling in sola lettura."}</p>}
      <button className="button button-secondary button-small" disabled={busy || !canWritePolling || !validRate || !rateChanged}
        onClick={() => { if (draftRate !== null) void runMutation(`mouse/${device.id}/polling`, () => api.setPollingRate(device.id, draftRate)); }}>
        {pending.has(`mouse/${device.id}/polling`) ? "Applicazione polling..." : "Applica polling"}
      </button>
    </>
  );

  return (
    <div className={`mouse-controls${compact ? " compact-controls" : ""}`}>
      {!compact && <div className="section-heading"><div><span className="eyebrow">01 / Sensore</span><h2>Sensibilita</h2></div><span className="unit-tag">DPI</span></div>}
      <p className="readback-line">Lettura confermata: <strong>{confirmed ? confirmed.x === confirmed.y ? `${confirmed.x} DPI` : `X ${confirmed.x} / Y ${confirmed.y} DPI` : "non disponibile"}</strong></p>
      {!compact && <p className="control-hint">DPI hardware, non velocita del puntatore di sistema. Le modifiche restano in bozza fino ad Applica.</p>}
      {dpi?.separateAxes && !compact ? (
        <Toggle label="Collega assi X/Y" checked={linked} disabled={busy || !canWriteDpi}
          description={linked ? "Una modifica imposta lo stesso DPI su entrambi gli assi." : "Il provider supporta impostazioni separate."}
          onChange={(checked) => {
            setLinked(checked);
            if (checked && draftDpi !== null && isDpiValueAllowed(dpi.values, draftDpi.x)) setDraftDpi({ x: draftDpi.x, y: draftDpi.x });
          }} />
      ) : dpi && <p className="axis-note">{dpi.separateAxes ? "Controllo rapido con assi collegati." : "Assi collegati dal provider."}</p>}
      <DpiControl capability={dpi} label={!linked && !compact ? "DPI X desiderati" : "DPI desiderati"} value={draftDpi?.x ?? null}
        disabled={busy || !hardwareAvailable} reason={dpiReason} onChange={(value) => changeDpi("x", value)} />
      {!linked && dpi?.separateAxes && !compact && <DpiControl capability={dpi} label="DPI Y desiderati" value={draftDpi?.y ?? null}
        disabled={busy || !hardwareAvailable} reason={dpiReason} onChange={(value) => changeDpi("y", value)} />}
      <div className="action-row">
        <button className={`button ${compact ? "button-secondary button-small" : "button-primary"}`} disabled={busy || !canWriteDpi || !validDpi || !dpiChanged}
          onClick={() => { if (draftDpi) void runMutation(`mouse/${device.id}/dpi`, () => api.setDpi(device.id, draftDpi.x, draftDpi.y)); }}>
          {pending.has(`mouse/${device.id}/dpi`) ? "Applicazione DPI..." : "Applica DPI"}
        </button>
        {!compact && <button className="text-button" disabled={busy || !dpiChanged} onClick={() => setDraftDpi(device.values.dpi)}>Ripristina bozza</button>}
      </div>
      {compact ? <details className="quick-details"><summary>Polling <span>{device.values.pollingRate === null ? "Non letto" : `${device.values.pollingRate} Hz`}</span></summary><div className="quick-details-content">{pollingControls}</div></details> : <><div className="control-divider" />{pollingControls}</>}
    </div>
  );
}

        </aside>

  );

import { invoke, isTauri } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
import type { AppError, AudioNode, AudioProfile, Device, DpiValues, HubApi, Snapshot } from "./types";

const browserMessage = "Backend non disponibile: i controlli hardware richiedono Peripheral Hub desktop su Linux. Questo browser non dispone del backend Tauri.";

function unsupportedSnapshot(): Snapshot {
  return {
    revision: 0,
    platform: "unsupportedPlatform",
    demo: false,
    devices: [],
    audio: {
      available: false, outputs: [], inputs: [], defaultOutput: null,
      configuredDefaultOutput: null, selectedOutput: null, defaultInput: null,
      surroundState: "off", canEnableSurround: false, surroundReason: browserMessage, hrtf: null,
    },
    profiles: [],
    diagnostics: [{ id: "browser", provider: "app", code: "unsupportedPlatform", severity: "warning", message: browserMessage, detail: null }],
  };
}

export function asAppError(error: unknown): AppError {
  if (typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "message" in error && typeof error.message === "string") {
    return {
      code: "code" in error && typeof error.code === "string" ? error.code : "backendError",
      message: error.message,
      detail: "detail" in error && typeof error.detail === "string" ? error.detail : null,
    };
  }
  return { code: "backendError", message: "Operazione non riuscita.", detail: typeof error === "string" ? error : null };
}

export function isDpiValueAllowed(values: DpiValues, value: number | null): boolean {
  if (value === null || !Number.isInteger(value) || value <= 0) return false;
  if (values.kind === "discrete") return values.values.includes(value);
  if (values.kind === "range") {
    return values.step > 0 && value >= values.min && value <= values.max && (value - values.min) % values.step === 0;
  }
  return false;
}

export function createTauriApi(): HubApi {
  async function command<T = Snapshot>(name: string, args?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {
    if (!isTauri()) throw { code: "unsupportedPlatform", message: browserMessage, detail: null } satisfies AppError;
    try {
      return args === undefined ? await invoke<T>(name) : await invoke<T>(name, args);
    } catch (error) {
      throw asAppError(error);
    }
  }

  return {
    demo: false,
    getSnapshot: () => isTauri() ? command("get_snapshot") : Promise.resolve(unsupportedSnapshot()),
    refreshDevices: () => isTauri() ? command("refresh_devices") : Promise.resolve(unsupportedSnapshot()),
    setDpi: (deviceId, x, y) => command("set_dpi", { deviceId, x, y }),
    setPollingRate: (deviceId, rate) => command("set_polling_rate", { deviceId, rate }),
    setDefaultOutput: (nodeName) => command("set_default_output", { nodeName }),
    setVolume: (nodeName, volume) => command("set_volume", { nodeName, volume }),
    setMute: (nodeName, muted) => command("set_mute", { nodeName, muted }),
    setSurround: (enabled) => command("set_surround", { enabled }),
    importHrtf: () => command("import_hrtf"),
    saveAudioProfile: (profile) => command("save_audio_profile", { profile }),
    applyAudioProfile: (profileId) => command("apply_audio_profile", { profileId }),
    exitApp: () => command<void>("exit_app"),
    async subscribe(listener) {
      if (!isTauri()) return () => {};
      return listen<Snapshot>("snapshot-changed", (event) => listener(event.payload));
    },
  };
}

function clone<T>(value: T): T {
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T;
}

function demoError(message: string): never {
  throw { code: "demoValidation", message, detail: "Validazione dei dati simulati; nessun comando hardware eseguito." } satisfies AppError;
}

        dpi: { values: { kind: "discrete", values: [400, 800, 1600, 3200, 6400, 12000] }, writable: true, separateAxes: false, reason: null },

  const devices = Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => {

    const instance = Math.floor(index / samples.length) + 1;
    sample.id += `-${instance}`;
    if (instance > 1) sample.name += ` (${instance})`;
    sample.identity.serial = `DEMO-${index + 1}`;

    description: "Audio integrato - analogico stereo", mediaClass: "Audio/Sink", channels: ["FL", "FR"],
    volume: 0.62, muted: false, isVirtual: false, deviceSerial: null, deviceBusPath: null,
    vendorId: null, productId: null, transport: "Analogico (demo)",

  const headsetNodes = devices.filter((device) => device.kind === "headset").map((device, index) => ({

    deviceBusPath: device.identity.physicalPath, vendorId: device.identity.vendorId, productId: device.identity.productId,

  const microphone: AudioNode = {

    diagnostics: [
      { id: "demo-openrazer", provider: "openrazer", code: "available", severity: "info", message: "OpenRazer simulato", detail: "Nessuna connessione DBus e nessuna lettura hardware." },
      { id: "demo-ratbag", provider: "ratbag", code: "available", severity: "info", message: "ratbagd simulato", detail: "Liste e readback sintetici, non una verifica del supporto reale." },
      { id: "demo-pipewire", provider: "pipewire", code: "available", severity: "info", message: "PipeWire simulato", detail: "Nessun flusso audio o filtro HRTF viene creato." },

      { id: "demo-headsetcontrol", provider: "headsetcontrol", code: "unsupported", severity: "info", message: "Batteria cuffie non esposta", detail: "La cuffia demo e' identificata solo via USB." },
    ],
  };
  const listeners = new Set<(snapshot: Snapshot) => void>();
  const hrtfs = new Map([["system", clone(state.audio.hrtf!)]]);
  const virtualName = "peripheral-hub.demo.surround-7.1";

  function commit(change: (next: Snapshot) => void): Snapshot {
    const next = clone(state);
    change(next);
    next.revision += 1;
    state = next;
    for (const listener of listeners) listener(clone(state));
    return clone(state);
  }

  function mouse(next: Snapshot, id: string): Device {
    const device = next.devices.find((entry) => entry.id === id);
    if (!device || device.kind !== "mouse" || device.availability !== "available") demoError("Mouse non disponibile.");
    return device;
  }

  function node(next: Snapshot, nodeName: string): AudioNode {
    const found = [...next.audio.outputs, ...next.audio.inputs].find((entry) => entry.nodeName === nodeName);
    if (!found) demoError("Nodo audio non disponibile.");
    return found;
  }

  function surround(next: Snapshot, enabled: boolean): void {
    if (enabled) {
      const target = next.audio.outputs.find((entry) => entry.nodeName === next.audio.selectedOutput && !entry.isVirtual);
      if (!next.audio.canEnableSurround || !target || target.channels.length !== 2 || !next.audio.hrtf?.readable) demoError("Surround non disponibile per il target o la HRTF selezionati.");
      if (!next.audio.outputs.some((entry) => entry.nodeName === virtualName)) {
        next.audio.outputs.push({ ...clone(baseNode), id: 501, objectSerial: 1501, nodeName: virtualName, description: "Peripheral Hub - ingresso 7.1 (simulato)", channels: ["FL", "FR", "FC", "LFE", "RL", "RR", "SL", "SR"], isVirtual: true, transport: "Virtuale (demo)" });
      }
      next.audio.surroundState = "on";
      next.audio.defaultOutput = virtualName;
      next.audio.configuredDefaultOutput = virtualName;
    } else {
      next.audio.outputs = next.audio.outputs.filter((entry) => entry.nodeName !== virtualName);
      next.audio.surroundState = "off";
      next.audio.defaultOutput = next.audio.outputs.find((entry) => entry.nodeName === next.audio.selectedOutput)?.nodeName ?? next.audio.outputs.find((entry) => !entry.isVirtual)?.nodeName ?? null;
      next.audio.configuredDefaultOutput = next.audio.defaultOutput;
    }
  }

  function validateProfile(next: Snapshot, profile: AudioProfile): AudioNode {
    if (profile.schemaVersion !== 1 || !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(profile.id) || !profile.name.trim() || profile.name.length > 80) demoError("Profilo non valido: nome, UUID o versione non corretti.");
    if (!Number.isFinite(profile.volume) || profile.volume < 0 || profile.volume > 1) demoError("Il volume deve essere tra 0 e 100%.");
    const output = next.audio.outputs.find((entry) => entry.nodeName === profile.outputNodeName && !entry.isVirtual);
    if (!output) demoError("L'uscita fisica del profilo non e' disponibile.");
    if (profile.hrtf !== null && !hrtfs.get(profile.hrtf)?.readable) demoError("Riferimento HRTF non disponibile nella demo.");
    if (profile.surroundRequested && (!profile.hrtf || !hrtfs.get(profile.hrtf)?.readable || output.channels.length !== 2)) demoError("Il profilo 7.1 richiede una HRTF leggibile e un'uscita stereo.");
    return output;
  }

  return {
    demo: true,
    async getSnapshot() { return clone(state); },
    async refreshDevices() { return commit(() => {}); },
    async setDpi(deviceId, x, y) {
      return commit((next) => {
        const device = mouse(next, deviceId);
        const capability = device.capabilities.dpi;
        if (!capability?.writable || !isDpiValueAllowed(capability.values, x) || !isDpiValueAllowed(capability.values, y) || (!capability.separateAxes && x !== y)) demoError("DPI non validi per questo mouse.");
        device.values.dpi = { x, y };
      });
    },
    async setPollingRate(deviceId, rate) {
      return commit((next) => {
        const device = mouse(next, deviceId);
        if (!device.capabilities.polling?.writable || !device.capabilities.polling.values.includes(rate)) demoError("Polling non supportato da questo mouse.");
        device.values.pollingRate = rate;
      });
    },
    async setDefaultOutput(nodeName) {
      return commit((next) => {
        const output = next.audio.outputs.find((entry) => entry.nodeName === nodeName);
        if (!output) demoError("Uscita audio non disponibile.");
        if (output.isVirtual && next.audio.surroundState !== "on") demoError("Il nodo virtuale richiede il surround attivo.");
        if (!output.isVirtual) next.audio.selectedOutput = nodeName;
        next.audio.defaultOutput = next.audio.surroundState === "on" ? virtualName : nodeName;
        next.audio.configuredDefaultOutput = next.audio.defaultOutput;
      });
    },
    async setVolume(nodeName, volume) {
      return commit((next) => {
        const target = node(next, nodeName);
        if (target.volume === null || !Number.isFinite(volume) || volume < 0 || volume > 1) demoError("Volume non disponibile o fuori intervallo (0-100%).");
        target.volume = volume;
      });
    },
    async setMute(nodeName, muted) {
      return commit((next) => {
        const target = node(next, nodeName);
        if (target.muted === null || typeof muted !== "boolean") demoError("Stato mute non disponibile.");
        target.muted = muted;
      });
    },
    async setSurround(enabled) { return commit((next) => surround(next, enabled)); },
    async importHrtf() {
      return commit((next) => {
        if (next.audio.surroundState === "on") surround(next, false);
        next.audio.hrtf = { reference: "975c10a0-f67e-478e-94a6-bcad73bce14c.sofa", displayName: "SOFA importata (simulata)", readable: true };
        hrtfs.set(next.audio.hrtf.reference, clone(next.audio.hrtf));
      });
    },
    async saveAudioProfile(profile) {
      return commit((next) => {
        validateProfile(next, profile);
        const index = next.profiles.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === profile.id);
        if (index === -1) next.profiles.push(clone(profile));
        else next.profiles[index] = clone(profile);
      });
    },
    async applyAudioProfile(profileId) {
      return commit((next) => {
        const profile = next.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === profileId);
        if (!profile) demoError("Profilo non trovato.");
        const output = validateProfile(next, profile);
        next.audio.selectedOutput = output.nodeName;
        if (profile.hrtf !== null) next.audio.hrtf = clone(hrtfs.get(profile.hrtf)!);
        surround(next, profile.surroundRequested);
        const volumeTarget = profile.surroundRequested ? node(next, virtualName) : output;
        volumeTarget.volume = profile.volume;
        volumeTarget.muted = profile.mute;
      });
    },
    async subscribe(listener) {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => { listeners.delete(listener); };
    },
    async exitApp() {},
  };
}

export function createHubApi(): HubApi {
  if (import.meta.env.VITE_DEMO === "true") {
    const requested = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("devices");
    return createDemoApi(requested !== null && ["0", "1", "3"].includes(requested) ? Number(requested) : 3);
  }
  return createTauriApi();
}

import { asAppError, createHubApi } from "./api";

import MousePanel from "./views/MousePanel";
import AudioPanel from "./views/AudioPanel";

type Tab = "dashboard" | "mouse" | "audio";
const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string; number: string }[] = [
  { id: "dashboard", label: "Panoramica", number: "01" },
  { id: "mouse", label: "Mouse", number: "02" },
  { id: "audio", label: "Audio", number: "03" },
];

function Diagnostics({ diagnostics }: { diagnostics: Diagnostic[] }) {
  const providers = new Map<ProviderKind, Diagnostic>();
  const priority = { info: 0, warning: 1, error: 2 };
  for (const diagnostic of diagnostics) {
    const previous = providers.get(diagnostic.provider);
    if (!previous || priority[diagnostic.severity] > priority[previous.severity]) providers.set(diagnostic.provider, diagnostic);
  }
  return (
    <details className="diagnostics-panel">
      <summary aria-label="Servizi e diagnostica"><span className="diagnostics-title"><span className="diagnostic-symbol" aria-hidden="true">[+]</span>Servizi e diagnostica</span><span className="provider-summary">{[...providers.values()].map((diagnostic) => <span key={diagnostic.provider} className="provider-chip"><i className={`status-dot ${diagnostic.code === "available" ? "available" : diagnostic.severity !== "info" ? "partial" : "muted"}`} aria-hidden="true" />{providerNames[diagnostic.provider]}<span className="sr-only">: {availabilityNames[diagnostic.code]}</span></span>)}</span><span className="details-chevron" aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
      <div className="diagnostic-list">{diagnostics.length === 0 ? <p className="control-hint">Nessuna diagnostica disponibile.</p> : diagnostics.map((diagnostic) => <article key={diagnostic.id} className={`diagnostic-item severity-${diagnostic.severity}`}><div><strong>{providerNames[diagnostic.provider]}</strong><span className={`status-pill ${diagnostic.code === "available" ? "available" : "muted"}`}>{availabilityNames[diagnostic.code]}</span></div><p>{diagnostic.message}</p>{diagnostic.detail && <pre>{diagnostic.detail}</pre>}</article>)}</div>
    </details>
  );
}

export default function App({ api: injectedApi }: { api?: HubApi }) {
  const [defaultApi] = useState(createHubApi);
  const api = injectedApi ?? defaultApi;
  const [snapshot, setSnapshot] = useState<Snapshot | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("dashboard");
  const [selectedMouseId, setSelectedMouseId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [audioFocus, setAudioFocus] = useState<{ output: string | null; deviceId: string | null; token: number }>({ output: null, deviceId: null, token: 0 });
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, AppError>>({});
  const pendingRef = useRef(new Set<string>());
  const generationRef = useRef(0);
  const activeRef = useRef(false);
  const focusFromCardRef = useRef(false);

  function acceptSnapshot(incoming: Snapshot, generation: number) {
    if (!activeRef.current || generationRef.current !== generation) return;
    // Commands and events may arrive in a different order than their readbacks.
    setSnapshot((current) => current && incoming.revision < current.revision ? current : incoming);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const generation = ++generationRef.current;
    activeRef.current = true;
    let alive = true;
    let unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;
    setSnapshot(null);
    setLoading(true);
    setErrors({});
    setPending(new Set());
    pendingRef.current.clear();
    setSelectedMouseId(null);
    setAudioFocus((current) => ({ output: null, deviceId: null, token: current.token + 1 }));
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const stop = await api.subscribe((incoming) => { if (alive) acceptSnapshot(incoming, generation); });
        if (alive) unsubscribe = stop;
        else stop();
      } catch (error) {
        if (alive) setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, subscription: asAppError(error) }));
      }
    })();
    void (async () => {
      try {
        acceptSnapshot(await api.getSnapshot(), generation);
      } catch (error) {
        if (alive) setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, connection: asAppError(error) }));
      } finally {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      activeRef.current = false;
      generationRef.current += 1;
      unsubscribe?.();
    };
  }, [api]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!snapshot) return;
    setSelectedMouseId((current) => snapshot.devices.some((device) => device.id === current && device.kind === "mouse") ? current : snapshot.devices.find((device) => device.kind === "mouse")?.id ?? null);
  }, [snapshot]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (snapshot && focusFromCardRef.current) {
      document.getElementById(`panel-${tab}`)?.focus();
      focusFromCardRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [tab, snapshot]);

  async function runMutation(key: string, operation: () => Promise<Snapshot>): Promise<boolean> {
    const generation = generationRef.current;
    const group = key.startsWith("mouse/") ? key.slice(0, key.lastIndexOf("/") + 1) : key.startsWith("audio/") ? "audio/" : key;
    if (!activeRef.current || [...pendingRef.current].some((entry) => group === key ? entry === key : entry.startsWith(group))) return false;
    pendingRef.current.add(key);
    setPending(new Set(pendingRef.current));
    setErrors((current) => { const next = { ...current }; delete next[key]; return next; });
    try {
      acceptSnapshot(await operation(), generation);
      return activeRef.current && generationRef.current === generation;
    } catch (error) {
      if (!activeRef.current || generationRef.current !== generation) return false;
      const failure = asAppError(error);
      setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: failure }));
      try {
        acceptSnapshot(await api.getSnapshot(), generation);
      } catch (readbackError) {
        if (activeRef.current && generationRef.current === generation) {
          const readback = asAppError(readbackError);
          setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: { ...failure, detail: [failure.detail, `Rilettura non riuscita: ${readback.message}`].filter(Boolean).join("\n") } }));
        }
      }
      return false;
    } finally {
      if (activeRef.current && generationRef.current === generation) {
        pendingRef.current.delete(key);
        setPending(new Set(pendingRef.current));
      }
    }
  }

  function chooseTab(next: Tab) {
    setTab(next);
    if (next === "audio") setAudioFocus((current) => ({ output: null, deviceId: null, token: current.token + 1 }));
  }

  function tabKey(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, current: Tab) {
    const index = tabs.findIndex((item) => item.id === current);
    const nextIndex = event.key === "ArrowRight" ? (index + 1) % tabs.length : event.key === "ArrowLeft" ? (index + tabs.length - 1) % tabs.length : event.key === "Home" ? 0 : event.key === "End" ? tabs.length - 1 : null;
    if (nextIndex === null) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    chooseTab(tabs[nextIndex].id);
    document.getElementById(`tab-${tabs[nextIndex].id}`)?.focus();
  }

  function openDevice(device: Device) {
    focusFromCardRef.current = true;
    if (device.kind === "mouse") {
      setSelectedMouseId(device.id);
      setTab("mouse");
    } else if (device.kind === "headset") {
      const output = snapshot?.audio.outputs.find((node) => device.outputNodeNames.includes(node.nodeName));
      setAudioFocus((current) => ({ output: output?.nodeName ?? null, deviceId: device.id, token: current.token + 1 }));
      setTab("audio");
    }
  }

  const demo = api.demo || snapshot?.demo === true;
  const controls = { api, pending, runMutation };
  const connectionLabel = demo ? "Demo / nessun hardware collegato" : snapshot?.platform === "unsupportedPlatform" ? "Backend non disponibile" : snapshot ? "Sessione locale / Linux" : loading ? "Connessione al backend" : "Connessione non riuscita";

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <a className="skip-link" href="#main-content">Vai ai controlli</a>
      <header className="app-header"><div className="header-inner"><div className="brand-lockup"><svg className="hub-logo" viewBox="0 0 44 44" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 7H32L40 22L32 37H12L4 22L12 7Z" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" /><path d="M15 15H25L30 22L25 29H15L11 22L15 15Z" stroke="currentColor" /><path d="M25 15L32 7M30 22H40M25 29L32 37M15 15L12 7M15 29L12 37M11 22H4" stroke="currentColor" strokeOpacity=".55" /></svg><div><span className="brand-name">Peripheral <strong>Hub</strong></span><span className="brand-subtitle">Client non ufficiale / Razer e Logitech</span></div></div><div className="header-actions"><div className={`connection-badge${demo ? " demo-badge" : ""}`} role="status"><span className={`status-dot ${demo || snapshot?.platform === "linux" ? "available" : "muted"}`} aria-hidden="true" />{connectionLabel}</div>{!demo && isTauri() && snapshot && <button className="text-button exit-button" aria-label="Esci e ripristina l'audio" disabled={pending.has("exit")} onClick={() => { void runMutation("exit", async () => { await api.exitApp(); return snapshot; }); }}>Esci</button>}</div></div></header>
      <div className="navigation-wrap"><nav className="main-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Sezioni Peripheral Hub">{tabs.map((item) => <button key={item.id} type="button" role="tab" id={`tab-${item.id}`} aria-selected={tab === item.id} aria-controls={`panel-${item.id}`} tabIndex={tab === item.id ? 0 : -1} onClick={() => chooseTab(item.id)} onKeyDown={(event) => tabKey(event, item.id)}><span className="tab-number">{item.number}</span>{item.label}<span className="tab-active-mark" aria-hidden="true" /></button>)}</nav><span className="revision-label mono">{snapshot ? `LETTURA / ${String(snapshot.revision).padStart(4, "0")}` : "LETTURA / ----"}</span></div>
      <main id="main-content" className="main-content">
        {Object.entries(errors).map(([key, error]) => <div className="error-banner" role="alert" key={key}><div><strong>{key === "subscription" ? "Aggiornamenti live non disponibili" : key === "connection" ? "Connessione al backend non riuscita" : "Operazione non confermata"}</strong><p>{error.message}</p><small>I controlli mostrano soltanto l'ultima lettura confermata.</small>{error.detail && <details><summary>Dettaglio diagnostico</summary><pre>{error.detail}</pre></details>}</div><button className="text-button" aria-label={`Chiudi errore ${error.code}`} onClick={() => setErrors((current) => { const next = { ...current }; delete next[key]; return next; })}>Chiudi</button></div>)}
        {snapshot?.platform === "unsupportedPlatform" && !demo && <section className="platform-banner"><span className="note-mark" aria-hidden="true">[!]</span><div><h2>Piattaforma non supportata</h2><p>{snapshot.diagnostics.find((diagnostic) => diagnostic.code === "unsupportedPlatform")?.message ?? "I controlli hardware e PipeWire richiedono l'app desktop su Linux."}</p><small>Nessun passaggio automatico alla demo.</small></div></section>}
        {snapshot ? <><div role="tabpanel" id={`panel-${tab}`} aria-labelledby={`tab-${tab}`} tabIndex={0} className="view-panel">{tab === "dashboard" ? <Dashboard snapshot={snapshot} selectedMouseId={selectedMouseId} onSelectMouse={setSelectedMouseId} onOpenDevice={openDevice} {...controls} /> : tab === "mouse" ? <MousePanel devices={snapshot.devices} selectedId={selectedMouseId} onSelect={setSelectedMouseId} {...controls} /> : <AudioPanel key={audioFocus.token} snapshot={snapshot} initialOutput={audioFocus.output} initialDevice={snapshot.devices.find((device) => device.id === audioFocus.deviceId) ?? null} {...controls} />}</div><Diagnostics diagnostics={snapshot.diagnostics} /></> : <section className="panel startup-panel"><div className="startup-mark" aria-hidden="true">PH</div><span className="eyebrow">Peripheral Hub</span><h1>{loading ? "Connessione al tuo setup." : "Backend non raggiungibile."}</h1><p role="status">{loading ? "Caricamento dei dispositivi e dello stato audio..." : "Nessun dato simulato e nessuna operazione hardware. Riprova la lettura del backend."}</p>{!loading && <button className="button button-primary" disabled={pending.has("connection")} onClick={() => { void runMutation("connection", () => api.getSnapshot()); }}>Riprova connessione</button>}</section>}
      </main>
      <footer className="app-footer"><span>Peripheral Hub <span className="footer-divider">/</span> Non ufficiale</span><span>Controllo locale <span className="footer-dot" aria-hidden="true" /> Nessun account richiesto</span></footer>
    </div>
  );
}

export type ProviderKind = "openrazer" | "ratbag" | "upower" | "headsetcontrol" | "pipewire" | "usb" | "app";
export type AvailabilityCode = "available" | "absent" | "unsupported" | "providerMissing" | "permissionDenied" | "unknown" | "unsupportedPlatform";
export type Brand = "razer" | "logitech" | "generic";
export type DeviceKind = "mouse" | "headset" | "receiver" | "unknown";
export type DpiValues = { kind: "range"; min: number; max: number; step: number } | { kind: "discrete"; values: number[] } | { kind: "unknown" };
export interface DpiCapability { values: DpiValues; writable: boolean; separateAxes: boolean; reason: string | null }
export interface PollingCapability { values: number[]; writable: boolean; reason: string | null }
export interface Battery {
  percentage: number | null;
  level: "unknown" | "critical" | "low" | "normal" | "high" | "full" | null;
  charging: boolean | null;
  source: ProviderKind;
  // Unix epoch seconds, not JavaScript milliseconds.
  observedAt: number;
  sourceUpdatedAt: number | null;
  stale: boolean;
}
export interface DeviceIdentity {
  serial: string | null; physicalPath: string | null;
  vendorId: number | null; productId: number | null;
  transport: string | null; firmware: string | null; backendPath: string | null;
}
export interface Device {
  id: string; name: string; brand: Brand; kind: DeviceKind; provider: ProviderKind;
  identity: DeviceIdentity; availability: AvailabilityCode;
  support: "supported" | "partial" | "unsupported";
  capabilities: { dpi: DpiCapability | null; polling: PollingCapability | null };
  values: { dpi: { x: number; y: number } | null; pollingRate: number | null; battery: Battery | null };
  outputNodeNames: string[]; limitations: string[];
}
export interface AudioNode {
  id: number; objectSerial: number | null; nodeName: string; description: string; mediaClass: string;
  channels: string[]; volume: number | null; muted: boolean | null; isVirtual: boolean;
  deviceSerial: string | null; deviceBusPath: string | null;
  vendorId: number | null; productId: number | null; transport: string | null;
}
export type SurroundState = "off" | "starting" | "on" | "stopping" | "error";
export interface AudioSnapshot {
  available: boolean; outputs: AudioNode[]; inputs: AudioNode[];
  defaultOutput: string | null; configuredDefaultOutput: string | null;
  selectedOutput: string | null; defaultInput: string | null;
  surroundState: SurroundState; canEnableSurround: boolean; surroundReason: string | null;
  hrtf: { reference: string; displayName: string; readable: boolean } | null;
}
export interface AudioProfile {
  schemaVersion: 1; id: string; name: string; outputNodeName: string; hrtf: string | null;
  volume: number; mute: boolean; surroundRequested: boolean;
}
export interface Diagnostic {
  id: string; provider: ProviderKind; code: AvailabilityCode; severity: "info" | "warning" | "error";
  message: string; detail: string | null;
}

  revision: number; platform: "linux" | "unsupportedPlatform"; demo: boolean;
  devices: Device[]; audio: AudioSnapshot; profiles: AudioProfile[]; diagnostics: Diagnostic[];
}
