use crate::models::{AppResult, AvailabilityCode, Battery, Diagnostic, ProviderKind, Severity};
use crate::providers::{bounded_text, hex_id, invalid_data, MAX_DEVICES, MAX_PROVIDER_BYTES};
use serde_json::Value;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Reading {
    pub vendor_id: u16,
    pub product_id: u16,
    pub name: String,
    pub readable_battery: bool,
    pub battery: Battery,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Report {
    pub api_version: Option<String>,
    pub devices: Vec<Reading>,
    pub diagnostics: Vec<Diagnostic>,
}

pub fn parse_json(bytes: &[u8], observed_at: u64) -> AppResult<Report> {
    if bytes.len() > MAX_PROVIDER_BYTES {
        return Err(invalid_data("Output JSON HeadsetControl troppo grande."));
    }
    let json: Value = serde_json::from_slice(bytes).map_err(|error| {
        invalid_data("Output HeadsetControl non JSON.").detail(error.to_string())
    })?;
    let root = json
        .as_object()
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("Output HeadsetControl non e un oggetto JSON."))?;
    let api_version = root
        .get("api_version")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .filter(|s| s.len() <= 32)
        .map(str::to_owned);
    let supported_api = matches!(api_version.as_deref(), Some("1.8" | "1.9"));
    let devices = root
        .get("devices")
        .and_then(Value::as_array)
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("devices[] manca nell'output HeadsetControl."))?;
    if devices.len() > MAX_DEVICES {
        return Err(invalid_data("Troppi dispositivi HeadsetControl."));
    }
    let count_matches = root
        .get("device_count")
        .and_then(Value::as_u64)
        .is_none_or(|count| count == devices.len() as u64);
    let mut report = Report {
        api_version,
        ..Report::default()
    };
    if !supported_api || !count_matches {
        report.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new("headsetcontrol-api", ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol, AvailabilityCode::Unknown, Severity::Warning,
            "API HeadsetControl non verificata o conteggio incoerente: batteria lasciata non disponibile."));
    }
    for (index, device) in devices.iter().enumerate() {
        let Some(device) = device.as_object() else {
            report.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(
                format!("headsetcontrol-record-{index}"),
                ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
                AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
                Severity::Warning,
                "Record cuffie HeadsetControl incompleto.",
            ));
            continue;
        };
        let vendor_id = device
            .get("id_vendor")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .and_then(hex_id);
        let product_id = device
            .get("id_product")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .and_then(hex_id);
        let (Some(0x046d), Some(product_id)) = (vendor_id, product_id) else {
            continue;
        };
        let readable = device
            .get("readable_capabilities")
            .and_then(Value::as_array);
        let advertised = device.get("capabilities").and_then(Value::as_array);
        let has_battery = |caps: Option<&Vec<Value>>| {
            caps.is_some_and(|caps| {
                caps.len() <= 128
                    && caps.iter().all(Value::is_string)
                    && caps
                        .iter()
                        .any(|cap| cap.as_str() == Some("CAP_BATTERY_STATUS"))
            })
        };
        let readable_battery =
            supported_api && count_matches && has_battery(readable) && has_battery(advertised);
        let success = device.get("status").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some("success");
        let no_errors = device.get("errors").is_none_or(|errors| match errors {
            Value::Object(errors) => errors.is_empty(),
            Value::Array(errors) => errors.is_empty(),
            Value::Null => true,
            _ => false,
        });
        let battery = device.get("battery").and_then(Value::as_object);
        let status = battery
            .and_then(|b| b.get("status"))
            .and_then(Value::as_str);
        let valid_status = matches!(status, Some("BATTERY_AVAILABLE" | "BATTERY_CHARGING"));
        let level = battery
            .and_then(|b| b.get("level"))
            .and_then(Value::as_i64)
            .filter(|level| (0..=100).contains(level));
        let available = readable_battery && success && no_errors && valid_status;
        let percentage = if available {
            level.map(|value| value as f64)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let charging = if available {
            match status {
                Some("BATTERY_CHARGING") => Some(true),
                Some("BATTERY_AVAILABLE") if level.is_some() => Some(false),
                _ => None,
            }
        } else {
            None
        };
        let name = bounded_text(
            device
                .get("device")
                .and_then(Value::as_str)
                .unwrap_or("Cuffie Logitech"),
        );
        if !available || percentage.is_none() {
            report.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(
                format!("headsetcontrol-battery-{index}"),
                ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
                AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
                Severity::Info,
                format!("{name}: lettura batteria non disponibile o parziale."),
            ));
        }
        report.devices.push(Reading {
            vendor_id: 0x046d,
            product_id,
            name,
            readable_battery,
            battery: Battery {
                percentage,
                level: None,
                charging,
                source: ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
                observed_at,
                source_updated_at: None,
                stale: false,
            },
        });
    }
    Ok(report)
}

pub fn usb_fingerprint(usb: &[crate::devices::UsbMetadata]) -> String {
    let mut usb: Vec<_> = usb
        .iter()
        .filter(|device| device.identity.vendor_id == Some(0x046d) && device.is_audio)
        .collect();
    usb.sort_by(|a, b| a.instance.cmp(&b.instance));
    serde_json::to_string(
        &usb.iter()
            .map(|device| {
                (
                    &device.instance,
                    &device.identity.serial,
                    device.identity.vendor_id,
                    device.identity.product_id,
                )
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
    )
    .unwrap_or_default()
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) async fn discover(usb: &[crate::devices::UsbMetadata]) -> Report {
    use crate::models::now_secs;
    use crate::providers::diagnostic;
    use std::{
        sync::OnceLock,
        time::{Duration, Instant},
    };
    use tokio::sync::Mutex;

    // Cache the USB instance generation too, never a model-only anonymous battery sample.
    // Holding the mutex during the probe prevents duplicate event/30s child processes.
    static CACHE: OnceLock<Mutex<Option<(String, Instant, Report)>>> = OnceLock::new();
    let mut cache = CACHE.get_or_init(|| Mutex::new(None)).lock().await;
    let key = usb_fingerprint(usb);
    if key == "[]" {
        *cache = None;
        return Report::default();
    }
    if let Some((cached_key, observed, report)) = cache.as_ref() {
        if key == *cached_key && observed.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(30) {
            return report.clone();
        }
    }
    let mut report = match crate::process::run(
        "headsetcontrol",
        &["-b", "-o", "json"],
        Duration::from_secs(5),
    )
    .await
    {
        Ok(output) => {
            match parse_json(&output.stdout, now_secs()) {
                Ok(mut report) => {
                    if !output.stderr.trim().is_empty() {
                        let mut warning = Diagnostic::new("headsetcontrol-stderr", ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol, AvailabilityCode::Unknown, Severity::Warning,
                        "HeadsetControl ha segnalato un errore: campioni batteria non confermati.");
                        warning.detail = Some(
                            crate::models::AppError::new(
                                crate::models::ErrorCode::CommandFailed,
                                "HeadsetControl",
                            )
                            .detail(&output.stderr)
                            .detail
                            .unwrap_or_default(),
                        );
                        report.diagnostics.push(warning);
                        for device in &mut report.devices {
                            device.battery.percentage = None;
                            device.battery.charging = None;
                        }
                    }
                    report
                }
                Err(error) => Report {
                    diagnostics: vec![diagnostic(
                        ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
                        "headsetcontrol-json",
                        &error,
                    )],
                    ..Report::default()
                },
            }
        }
        Err(error) => Report {
            diagnostics: vec![diagnostic(
                ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
                "headsetcontrol",
                &error,
            )],
            ..Report::default()
        },
    };
    let current_usb = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(crate::devices::read_usb).await;
    if !matches!(current_usb, Ok(Ok(ref current)) if usb_fingerprint(current) == key) {
        report.devices.clear();
        report.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(
            "headsetcontrol-hotplug",
            ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
            AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
            Severity::Warning,
            "Istanze USB cambiate durante il probe: batteria anonima non associata.",
        ));
    }
    *cache = Some((key, Instant::now(), report.clone()));
    report
}


=== FILE: C:\Users\shady\Desktop\github\sycomopse\src-tauri\src\providers\mod.rs ===
pub mod headsetcontrol;
pub mod openrazer;
pub mod ratbag;
pub mod upower;

use crate::models::{
    AppError, AppResult, AvailabilityCode, DeviceIdentity, Diagnostic, ErrorCode, ProviderKind,
    Severity,
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub const MAX_PROVIDER_BYTES: usize = 256 * 1024;
pub const MAX_DEVICES: usize = 256;

pub fn invalid_data(message: &str) -> AppError {
    AppError::new(ErrorCode::InvalidData, message)
}

pub fn bounded_text(value: &str) -> String {
    value
        .chars()
        .filter(|c| !c.is_control())
        .take(256)
        .collect()
}

pub fn valid_serial(value: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let value = value.trim();
    if value.is_empty()
        || value.len() > 256
        || value.chars().any(char::is_control)
        || value.chars().all(|c| c == '0' || c == ':' || c == '-')
        || matches!(
            value.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str(),
            "unknown" | "none" | "n/a"
        )
    {
        None
    } else {
        Some(value.to_owned())
    }
}

pub fn hex_id(value: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let value = value.trim();
    let value = value
        .strip_prefix("0x")
        .or_else(|| value.strip_prefix("0X"))
        .unwrap_or(value);
    if value.is_empty() || value.len() > 4 || !value.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
        return None;
    }
    u16::from_str_radix(value, 16).ok()
}

pub fn identities_match(a: &DeviceIdentity, b: &DeviceIdentity) -> bool {
    if a.vendor_id.zip(b.vendor_id).is_some_and(|(a, b)| a != b)
        || a.product_id.zip(b.product_id).is_some_and(|(a, b)| a != b)
    {
        return false;
    }
    let a_serial = a.serial.as_deref().and_then(valid_serial);
    let b_serial = b.serial.as_deref().and_then(valid_serial);
    if a_serial
        .as_ref()
        .zip(b_serial.as_ref())
        .is_some_and(|(a, b)| a != b)
    {
        return false;
    }
    let physical_match = a
        .physical_path
        .as_deref()
        .zip(b.physical_path.as_deref())
        .map(|(a, b)| {
            a.starts_with("/sys/devices/")
                && a == b
                && !a.split('/').any(|p| matches!(p, "." | ".."))
        });
    match physical_match {
        Some(matches) => matches,
        None => a_serial.zip(b_serial).is_some_and(|(a, b)| a == b),
    }
}

pub fn unique_identity_match(
    identity: &DeviceIdentity,
    candidates: &[DeviceIdentity],
) -> Option<usize> {
    let mut matches = candidates
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter(|(_, candidate)| identities_match(identity, candidate));
    let (index, _) = matches.next()?;
    matches.next().is_none().then_some(index)
}

pub fn ordered_values(mut values: Vec<u32>, maximum_count: usize) -> AppResult<Vec<u32>> {
    if values.is_empty() || values.len() > maximum_count || values.contains(&0) {
        return Err(invalid_data("Lista di valori hardware vuota o non valida."));
    }
    values.sort_unstable();
    if values.windows(2).any(|pair| pair[0] == pair[1]) {
        return Err(invalid_data(
            "Il provider ha restituito valori hardware duplicati.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(values)
}

pub fn diagnostic(provider: ProviderKind, id: impl Into<String>, error: &AppError) -> Diagnostic {
    let code = match error.code {
        ErrorCode::ProviderMissing => AvailabilityCode::ProviderMissing,
        ErrorCode::PermissionDenied => AvailabilityCode::PermissionDenied,
        ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform => AvailabilityCode::UnsupportedPlatform,
        ErrorCode::Unsupported => AvailabilityCode::Unsupported,
        ErrorCode::DeviceNotFound => AvailabilityCode::Absent,
        _ => AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
    };
    let mut diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(id, provider, code, Severity::Warning, &error.message);
    diagnostic.detail = error.detail.clone();
    diagnostic
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct MethodSignature {
    pub input: String,
    pub output: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PropertySignature {
    pub signature: String,
    pub readable: bool,
    pub writable: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Interface {
    pub methods: BTreeMap<String, MethodSignature>,
    pub properties: BTreeMap<String, PropertySignature>,
    pub signals: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct Introspection {
    pub interfaces: BTreeMap<String, Interface>,
}

impl Introspection {
    pub fn method(&self, interface: &str, method: &str, input: &str, output: &str) -> bool {
        self.interfaces
            .get(interface)
            .and_then(|i| i.methods.get(method))
            .is_some_and(|m| m.input == input && m.output == output)
    }

    pub fn property(
        &self,
        interface: &str,
        property: &str,
        signature: &str,
        writable: bool,
    ) -> bool {
        self.interfaces
            .get(interface)
            .and_then(|i| i.properties.get(property))
            .is_some_and(|p| p.signature == signature && p.readable && (!writable || p.writable))
    }

    pub fn signal(&self, interface: &str, signal: &str, signature: &str) -> bool {
        self.interfaces
            .get(interface)
            .and_then(|i| i.signals.get(signal))
            .is_some_and(|s| s == signature)
    }
}

// A bounded parser for the DBus introspection grammar, without fetching a DTD. Keeping
// this small, strict grammar portable also lets the installed API contract be fixture-tested.
pub fn parse_introspection(xml: &str) -> AppResult<Introspection> {
    let malformed = || invalid_data("Introspection DBus non valida o troppo grande.");
    if xml.len() > MAX_PROVIDER_BYTES {
        return Err(malformed());
    }
    let mut result = Introspection::default();
    let mut stack: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut interface_name: Option<String> = None;
    let mut member_name: Option<String> = None;
    let mut cursor = 0;
    let mut root_seen = false;
    let mut elements = 0;
    while cursor < xml.len() {
        let rest = &xml[cursor..];
        let Some(start) = rest.find('<') else {
            if !rest.trim().is_empty() {
                return Err(malformed());
            }
            break;
        };
        if !rest[..start].trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
        cursor += start;
        let rest = &xml[cursor..];
        if rest.starts_with("<!--") {
            cursor += rest.find("-->").ok_or_else(malformed)? + 3;
            continue;
        }
        if rest.starts_with("<?") {
            cursor += rest.find("?>").ok_or_else(malformed)? + 2;
            continue;
        }
        let mut quote = None;
        let mut end = None;
        for (index, byte) in rest.bytes().enumerate().skip(1) {
            match (quote, byte) {
                (Some(q), b) if q == b => quote = None,
                (None, b'\'' | b'"') => quote = Some(byte),
                (None, b'>') => {
                    end = Some(index);
                    break;
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        let end = end.ok_or_else(malformed)?;
        let tag = rest[1..end].trim();
        cursor += end + 1;
        if tag.starts_with("!DOCTYPE ") {
            if root_seen || tag.contains('[') {
                return Err(malformed());
            }
            continue;
        }
        if tag.starts_with('!') {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
        if let Some(name) = tag.strip_prefix('/') {
            let name = name.trim();
            if stack.pop().as_deref() != Some(name) {
                return Err(malformed());
            }
            if matches!(name, "method" | "signal") {
                member_name = None;
            }
            if name == "interface" {
                interface_name = None;
            }
            continue;
        }
        elements += 1;
        if elements > 8192 || stack.len() >= 16 {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
        let self_closing = tag.ends_with('/');
        let tag = if self_closing {
            tag[..tag.len() - 1].trim_end()
        } else {
            tag
        };
        let split = tag.find(char::is_whitespace).unwrap_or(tag.len());
        let name = &tag[..split];
        let attrs = parse_attributes(&tag[split..])?;
        let attr = |key: &str| attrs.get(key).cloned().ok_or_else(malformed);
        let parent = stack.last().map(String::as_str);
        match name {
            "node" => {
                if parent.is_none() {
                    if root_seen {
                        return Err(malformed());
                    }
                    root_seen = true;
                } else if parent != Some("node") {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
            }
            "interface" => {
                if stack.len() != 1 || parent != Some("node") {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                let name = attr("name")?;
                if result
                    .interfaces
                    .insert(name.clone(), Interface::default())
                    .is_some()
                {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                if !self_closing {
                    interface_name = Some(name);
                }
            }
            "method" | "signal" => {
                if parent != Some("interface") {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                let interface = result
                    .interfaces
                    .get_mut(interface_name.as_deref().ok_or_else(malformed)?)
                    .ok_or_else(malformed)?;
                let member = attr("name")?;
                let duplicate = if name == "method" {
                    interface
                        .methods
                        .insert(member.clone(), MethodSignature::default())
                        .is_some()
                } else {
                    interface
                        .signals
                        .insert(member.clone(), String::new())
                        .is_some()
                };
                if duplicate {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                if !self_closing {
                    member_name = Some(member);
                }
            }
            "arg" => {
                let interface = result
                    .interfaces
                    .get_mut(interface_name.as_deref().ok_or_else(malformed)?)
                    .ok_or_else(malformed)?;
                let member = member_name.as_deref().ok_or_else(malformed)?;
                let signature = attr("type")?;
                if signature.is_empty() || signature.len() > 255 || !signature.is_ascii() {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                let direction = attrs.get("direction").map(String::as_str);
                match parent {
                    Some("method") => {
                        let method = interface.methods.get_mut(member).ok_or_else(malformed)?;
                        match direction.unwrap_or("in") {
                            "in" => method.input.push_str(&signature),
                            "out" => method.output.push_str(&signature),
                            _ => return Err(malformed()),
                        }
                        if method.input.len() > 255 || method.output.len() > 255 {
                            return Err(malformed());
                        }
                    }
                    Some("signal") if direction.is_none() || direction == Some("out") => {
                        let signal = interface.signals.get_mut(member).ok_or_else(malformed)?;
                        signal.push_str(&signature);
                        if signal.len() > 255 {
                            return Err(malformed());
                        }
                    }
                    _ => return Err(malformed()),
                }
            }
            "property" => {
                if parent != Some("interface") {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                let access = attr("access")?;
                if !matches!(access.as_str(), "read" | "write" | "readwrite") {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
                let property = PropertySignature {
                    signature: attr("type")?,
                    readable: access != "write",
                    writable: access != "read",
                };
                let interface = result
                    .interfaces
                    .get_mut(interface_name.as_deref().ok_or_else(malformed)?)
                    .ok_or_else(malformed)?;
                if interface
                    .properties
                    .insert(attr("name")?, property)
                    .is_some()
                {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
            }
            "annotation" => {
                if !matches!(
                    parent,
                    Some("interface" | "method" | "signal" | "property" | "arg")
                ) {
                    return Err(malformed());
                }
            }
            _ => return Err(malformed()),
        }
        if !self_closing {
            stack.push(name.to_owned());
        }
    }
    if !root_seen || !stack.is_empty() {
        return Err(malformed());
    }
    Ok(result)
}

fn parse_attributes(mut text: &str) -> AppResult<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    let malformed = || invalid_data("Attributi XML DBus non validi.");
    let mut result = BTreeMap::new();
    while !text.trim().is_empty() {
        text = text.trim_start();
        let end = text
            .find(|c: char| c == '=' || c.is_whitespace())
            .ok_or_else(malformed)?;
        let name = &text[..end];
        if name.is_empty()
            || !name
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'_' | b':' | b'-'))
        {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
        text = text[end..]
            .trim_start()
            .strip_prefix('=')
            .ok_or_else(malformed)?
            .trim_start();
        let quote = text
            .chars()
            .next()
            .filter(|c| matches!(c, '\'' | '"'))
            .ok_or_else(malformed)?;
        text = &text[1..];
        let end = text.find(quote).ok_or_else(malformed)?;
        let raw = &text[..end];
        if raw.contains('<') {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
        let mut value = String::new();
        let mut remaining = raw;
        while let Some(index) = remaining.find('&') {
            value.push_str(&remaining[..index]);
            let entity_end = remaining[index..].find(';').ok_or_else(malformed)? + index;
            value.push(match &remaining[index..=entity_end] {
                "&amp;" => '&',
                "&quot;" => '"',
                "&apos;" => '\'',
                "&lt;" => '<',
                "&gt;" => '>',
                _ => return Err(malformed()),
            });
            remaining = &remaining[entity_end + 1..];
        }
        value.push_str(remaining);
        if result.insert(name.to_owned(), value).is_some() || result.len() > 16 {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
        text = &text[end + 1..];
        if !text.is_empty() && !text.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) {
            return Err(malformed());
        }
    }
    Ok(result)
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) mod dbus {
    use super::*;
    use serde::Serialize;
    use std::{collections::HashMap, time::Duration};
    use zbus::{
        proxy::{CacheProperties, MethodFlags},
        zvariant::{DynamicDeserialize, DynamicType, OwnedValue},
        Connection,
    };

    pub const CALL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);
    pub const PROPERTIES: &str = "org.freedesktop.DBus.Properties";

    pub struct Bus {
        pub connection: Connection,
        pub owner: String,
    }

    pub fn error(error: zbus::Error) -> AppError {
        let detail = error.to_string();
        let code = if detail.contains("AccessDenied")
            || detail.contains("NotAuthorized")
            || detail.contains("AuthFailed")
        {
            ErrorCode::PermissionDenied
        } else if detail.contains("NameHasNoOwner") || detail.contains("ServiceUnknown") {
            ErrorCode::ProviderMissing
        } else if detail.contains("UnknownObject") {
            ErrorCode::DeviceNotFound
        } else if detail.contains("UnknownMethod")
            || detail.contains("UnknownProperty")
            || detail.contains("UnknownInterface")
        {
            ErrorCode::Unsupported
        } else {
            ErrorCode::CommandFailed
        };
        AppError::new(code, "Il provider DBus non ha completato la richiesta.").detail(detail)
    }

    pub async fn connection(session: bool) -> AppResult<Connection> {
        tokio::time::timeout(CALL_TIMEOUT, async {
            if session {
                Connection::session().await
            } else {
                Connection::system().await
            }
        })
        .await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Timeout, "Connessione al bus DBus scaduta."))?
        .map_err(error)
    }

    impl Bus {
        pub async fn active(service: &str, session: bool) -> AppResult<Self> {
            let connection = connection(session).await?;
            let bus = Self {
                connection,
                owner: "org.freedesktop.DBus".to_owned(),
            };
            let owner: String = bus
                .call(
                    "/org/freedesktop/DBus",
                    "org.freedesktop.DBus",
                    "GetNameOwner",
                    &(service,),
                )
                .await?;
            if !owner.starts_with(':') || owner.len() > 255 {
                return Err(invalid_data("Proprietario DBus non valido."));
            }
            Ok(Self {
                connection: bus.connection,
                owner,
            })
        }

        pub async fn call<B, R>(
            &self,
            path: &str,
            interface: &str,
            method: &str,
            body: &B,
        ) -> AppResult<R>
        where
            B: Serialize + DynamicType,
            R: for<'de> DynamicDeserialize<'de>,
        {
            let operation = async {
                let proxy: zbus::Proxy<'_> = zbus::proxy::Builder::new(&self.connection)
                    .destination(self.owner.as_str())
                    .map_err(error)?
                    .path(path)
                    .map_err(error)?
                    .interface(interface)
                    .map_err(error)?
                    .cache_properties(CacheProperties::No)
                    .build()
                    .await
                    .map_err(error)?;
                let reply: Option<R> = proxy
                    .call_with_flags(method, MethodFlags::NoAutoStart.into(), body)
                    .await
                    .map_err(error)?;
                reply.ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("Risposta DBus mancante."))
            };
            tokio::time::timeout(CALL_TIMEOUT, operation)
                .await
                .map_err(|_| {
                    AppError::new(ErrorCode::Timeout, "Richiesta al provider DBus scaduta.")
                })?
        }

        pub async fn introspect(&self, path: &str) -> AppResult<Introspection> {
            let xml: String = self
                .call(
                    path,
                    "org.freedesktop.DBus.Introspectable",
                    "Introspect",
                    &(),
                )
                .await?;
            parse_introspection(&xml)
        }

        pub async fn ensure_owner(&self, service: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
            let daemon = Self {
                connection: self.connection.clone(),
                owner: "org.freedesktop.DBus".to_owned(),
            };
            let owner: String = daemon
                .call(
                    "/org/freedesktop/DBus",
                    "org.freedesktop.DBus",
                    "GetNameOwner",
                    &(service,),
                )
                .await?;
            if owner != self.owner {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::DeviceNotFound,
                    "Proprietario del provider DBus cambiato; richiesta non ripetuta.",
                ));
            }
            Ok(())
        }

        pub async fn get<T>(&self, path: &str, interface: &str, property: &str) -> AppResult<T>
        where
            T: TryFrom<OwnedValue>,
            T::Error: std::fmt::Display,
        {
            let value: OwnedValue = self
                .call(path, PROPERTIES, "Get", &(interface, property))
                .await?;
            T::try_from(value).map_err(|error| {
                invalid_data("Tipo della proprieta DBus non valido.").detail(error.to_string())
            })
        }

        pub async fn get_all(
            &self,
            path: &str,
            interface: &str,
        ) -> AppResult<HashMap<String, OwnedValue>> {
            let values: HashMap<String, OwnedValue> =
                self.call(path, PROPERTIES, "GetAll", &(interface,)).await?;
            if values.len() > 128 {
                return Err(invalid_data("Troppe proprieta DBus."));
            }
            Ok(values)
        }
    }

    pub fn property<T>(properties: &HashMap<String, OwnedValue>, name: &str) -> AppResult<T>
    where
        T: TryFrom<OwnedValue>,
        T::Error: std::fmt::Display,
    {
        let value = properties
            .get(name)
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("Proprieta DBus obbligatoria mancante.").detail(name))?;
        let value = value.try_clone().map_err(|error| {
            invalid_data("Proprieta DBus non copiabile.").detail(error.to_string())
        })?;
        T::try_from(value).map_err(|error| {
            invalid_data("Tipo della proprieta DBus non valido.").detail(format!("{name}: {error}"))
        })
    }
}

use crate::models::*;
use crate::providers::{invalid_data, ordered_values, Introspection};

pub const DPI_INTERFACE: &str = "razer.device.dpi";
pub const MISC_INTERFACE: &str = "razer.device.misc";
pub const POWER_INTERFACE: &str = "razer.device.power";
pub const VIPER_PRODUCT: u16 = 0x00b8;

pub fn parse_vid_pid(values: &[i32]) -> AppResult<(u16, u16)> {
    if values.len() != 2 {
        return Err(invalid_data("getVidPid deve restituire due Int32."));
    }
    let vendor =
        u16::try_from(values[0]).map_err(|_| invalid_data("Vendor USB OpenRazer non valido."))?;
    let product =
        u16::try_from(values[1]).map_err(|_| invalid_data("Product USB OpenRazer non valido."))?;
    if vendor != 0x1532 {
        return Err(invalid_data("Vendor OpenRazer non Razer."));
    }
    Ok((vendor, product))
}

pub fn parse_dpi(values: &[i32], scalar: bool) -> AppResult<DpiValue> {
    if values.len() != 2 {
        return Err(invalid_data("getDPI deve restituire due Int32."));
    }
    let x = u32::try_from(values[0])
        .ok()
        .filter(|x| *x > 0 && *x <= u32::from(u16::MAX))
        .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("DPI X OpenRazer non valido."))?;
    let y = if scalar && values[1] == 0 {
        x
    } else {
        u32::try_from(values[1])
            .ok()
            .filter(|y| *y > 0 && *y <= u32::from(u16::MAX))
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("DPI Y OpenRazer non valido."))?
    };
    if scalar && y != x {
        return Err(invalid_data("Assi DPI incoerenti con availableDPI."));
    }
    Ok(DpiValue { x, y })
}

pub fn dpi_capability(
    api: &Introspection,
    product: u16,
    maximum: Option<u32>,
    discrete: Option<Vec<u32>>,
    current: Option<DpiValue>,
) -> Option<DpiCapability> {
    let getter = api.method(DPI_INTERFACE, "getDPI", "", "ai");
    let setter = api.method(DPI_INTERFACE, "setDPI", "qq", "");
    if !getter && !setter {
        return None;
    }
    let scalar = product != VIPER_PRODUCT && api.method(DPI_INTERFACE, "availableDPI", "", "ai");
    let values = if product == VIPER_PRODUCT && maximum == Some(30000) {
        DpiValues::Range {
            min: 100,
            max: 30000,
            step: 1,
        }
    } else if scalar {
        discrete
            .filter(|values| values.iter().all(|v| *v <= u32::from(u16::MAX)))
            .and_then(|values| ordered_values(values, 300).ok())
            .map(|values| DpiValues::Discrete { values })
            .unwrap_or(DpiValues::Unknown)
    } else {
        DpiValues::Unknown
    };
    let values_known = !matches!(values, DpiValues::Unknown);
    let current_valid =
        current.is_some_and(|current| values.contains(current.x) && values.contains(current.y));
    let writable = getter && setter && values_known;
    let reason = if !values_known {
        Some("Minimo/lista DPI del modello non verificati, oppure maxDPI runtime incoerente: sola lettura.".to_owned())
    } else if !getter || !setter {
        Some("Firme getDPI/setDPI non disponibili nell'introspection installata.".to_owned())
    } else if !current_valid {
        Some("DPI corrente non disponibile o fuori elenco: scegliere un valore verificato prima di Applica.".to_owned())
    } else {
        None
    };
    Some(DpiCapability {
        values,
        writable,
        separate_axes: !scalar,
        reason,
    })
}

pub fn polling_capability(
    api: &Introspection,
    product: u16,
    supported: Option<Vec<u32>>,
    current: Option<u32>,
) -> Option<PollingCapability> {
    let getter = api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getPollRate", "", "i");
    let setter = api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "setPollRate", "q", "");
    if !getter && !setter {
        return None;
    }
    let runtime_list = api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getSupportedPollRates", "", "aq");
    let values = if runtime_list {
        supported
            .and_then(|rates| ordered_values(rates, 32).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    } else if product == VIPER_PRODUCT {
        vec![125, 500, 1000]
    } else {
        Vec::new()
    };
    let documented =
        product != VIPER_PRODUCT || values.iter().all(|value| [125, 500, 1000].contains(value));
    let writable = getter
        && setter
        && !values.is_empty()
        && documented
        && current.is_some_and(|rate| values.contains(&rate));
    let reason = if values.is_empty() {
        Some("Rate supportati non verificati per il modello installato.".to_owned())
    } else if !documented {
        Some(
            "Rate runtime inattesi per Viper V3 HyperSpeed; non si presume HyperPolling."
                .to_owned(),
        )
    } else if !writable {
        Some("Getter/setter polling o stato corrente non validi.".to_owned())
    } else {
        None
    };
    Some(PollingCapability {
        values,
        writable,
        reason,
    })
}

pub fn battery(
    percentage: Option<f64>,
    charging: Option<bool>,
    product: u16,
    observed_at: u64,
) -> Battery {
    Battery {
        percentage: percentage.filter(|p| p.is_finite() && (0.0..=100.0).contains(p)),
        level: None,
        charging: if product == VIPER_PRODUCT {
            Some(false)
        } else {
            charging
        },
        source: ProviderKind::Openrazer,
        observed_at,
        source_updated_at: None,
        stale: false,
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) use linux::{discover, set_dpi, set_polling_rate};

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod linux {
    use super::*;
    use crate::devices::UsbMetadata;
    use crate::providers::{
        bounded_text, dbus::Bus, diagnostic, identities_match, unique_identity_match, valid_serial,
        MAX_DEVICES,
    };
    use std::collections::HashSet;

    const SERVICE: &str = "org.razer";
    const ROOT: &str = "/org/razer";
    const MANAGER: &str = "razer.devices";

    async fn manager() -> AppResult<Bus> {
        let bus = Bus::active(SERVICE, true).await.map_err(|error| {
            if error.code == ErrorCode::ProviderMissing {
                AppError::new(ErrorCode::ProviderMissing, "OpenRazer non attivo sul session bus. Verificare daemon, driver e permessi manualmente; l'app non avvia il servizio.")
            } else { error }
        })?;
        if !bus
            .introspect(ROOT)
            .await?
            .method(MANAGER, "getDevices", "", "as")
        {
            return Err(invalid_data("OpenRazer non espone getDevices() -> as."));
        }
        Ok(bus)
    }

    async fn serials(bus: &Bus) -> AppResult<Vec<String>> {
        let values: Vec<String> = bus.call(ROOT, MANAGER, "getDevices", &()).await?;
        if values.len() > MAX_DEVICES {
            return Err(invalid_data("Troppi dispositivi OpenRazer."));
        }
        if values.iter().any(|serial| {
            valid_serial(serial).as_deref() != Some(serial.as_str())
                || !serial
                    .bytes()
                    .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_')
        }) {
            return Err(invalid_data("Seriale/percorso OpenRazer non valido."));
        }
        Ok(values)
    }

    pub(crate) async fn discover(usb: &[UsbMetadata], include_batteries: bool) -> Discovery {
        let mut discovery = Discovery::default();
        let bus = match manager().await {
            Ok(bus) => bus,
            Err(error) => {
                discovery.diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    "openrazer",
                    &error,
                ));
                return discovery;
            }
        };
        let serials = match serials(&bus).await {
            Ok(serials) => serials,
            Err(error) => {
                discovery.diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    "openrazer-devices",
                    &error,
                ));
                return discovery;
            }
        };
        let mut seen = HashSet::new();
        for serial in &serials {
            if serials.iter().filter(|s| *s == serial).count() != 1 || !seen.insert(serial) {
                discovery.diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    "openrazer-serial",
                    &invalid_data("Seriale OpenRazer duplicato; istanza non identificabile."),
                ));
                continue;
            }
            match read_device(&bus, serial, usb, include_batteries).await {
                Ok((device, diagnostics)) => {
                    discovery.devices.push(device);
                    discovery.diagnostics.extend(diagnostics);
                }
                Err(error) => discovery.diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("openrazer:{serial}"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        discovery
    }

    async fn read_device(
        bus: &Bus,
        serial: &str,
        usb: &[UsbMetadata],
        include_batteries: bool,
    ) -> AppResult<(Device, Vec<Diagnostic>)> {
        let path = format!("/org/razer/device/{serial}");
        let api = bus.introspect(&path).await?;
        if !api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getVidPid", "", "ai") {
            return Err(invalid_data("OpenRazer getVidPid() -> ai non disponibile."));
        }
        let ids: Vec<i32> = bus.call(&path, MISC_INTERFACE, "getVidPid", &()).await?;
        let (vendor, product) = parse_vid_pid(&ids)?;
        let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
        let mut identity = DeviceIdentity {
            serial: Some(serial.to_owned()),
            vendor_id: Some(vendor),
            product_id: Some(product),
            backend_path: Some(path.clone()),
            ..DeviceIdentity::default()
        };
        let usb_identities: Vec<_> = usb.iter().map(|u| u.identity.clone()).collect();
        if let Some(index) = unique_identity_match(&identity, &usb_identities) {
            identity.physical_path = usb[index].identity.physical_path.clone();
            identity.transport = usb[index].identity.transport.clone();
        }
        let mut name = if product == VIPER_PRODUCT {
            "Razer Viper V3 HyperSpeed".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("Razer {vendor:04x}:{product:04x}")
        };
        if api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getDeviceName", "", "s") {
            match bus
                .call::<_, String>(&path, MISC_INTERFACE, "getDeviceName", &())
                .await
            {
                Ok(value) if !value.trim().is_empty() => name = bounded_text(value.trim()),
                Ok(_) => {}
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:name"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        if api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getFirmware", "", "s") {
            match bus
                .call::<_, String>(&path, MISC_INTERFACE, "getFirmware", &())
                .await
            {
                Ok(value) => identity.firmware = Some(bounded_text(value.trim())),
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:firmware"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let mut kind = if product == 0x00b3 {
            DeviceKind::Receiver
        } else if product == VIPER_PRODUCT {
            DeviceKind::Mouse
        } else {
            DeviceKind::Unknown
        };
        if product != 0x00b3 && api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getDeviceType", "", "s") {
            match bus
                .call::<_, String>(&path, MISC_INTERFACE, "getDeviceType", &())
                .await
            {
                Ok(value) => {
                    kind = match value.as_str() {
                        "mouse" => DeviceKind::Mouse,
                        "headset" => DeviceKind::Headset,
                        _ => DeviceKind::Unknown,
                    }
                }
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:type"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let mut maximum = None;
        if api.method(DPI_INTERFACE, "maxDPI", "", "i") {
            match bus
                .call::<_, i32>(&path, DPI_INTERFACE, "maxDPI", &())
                .await
            {
                Ok(value) => {
                    maximum = u32::try_from(value)
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|v| *v > 0 && *v <= u32::from(u16::MAX))
                }
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:maxDPI"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let scalar =
            product != VIPER_PRODUCT && api.method(DPI_INTERFACE, "availableDPI", "", "ai");
        let mut discrete = None;
        if scalar {
            let result: AppResult<Vec<i32>> =
                bus.call(&path, DPI_INTERFACE, "availableDPI", &()).await;
            match result.and_then(|values| {
                if values.len() > 300 {
                    return Err(invalid_data("Lista DPI OpenRazer troppo grande."));
                }
                values
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|value| {
                        u32::try_from(value)
                            .map_err(|_| invalid_data("availableDPI contiene valori negativi."))
                    })
                    .collect::<AppResult<Vec<_>>>()
                    .and_then(|values| ordered_values(values, 300))
            }) {
                Ok(values) => discrete = Some(values),
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:availableDPI"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let mut current_dpi = None;
        if api.method(DPI_INTERFACE, "getDPI", "", "ai") {
            let result: AppResult<Vec<i32>> = bus.call(&path, DPI_INTERFACE, "getDPI", &()).await;
            match result.and_then(|values| parse_dpi(&values, scalar)) {
                Ok(value) => current_dpi = Some(value),
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:getDPI"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let mut supported_rates = None;
        if api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getSupportedPollRates", "", "aq") {
            match bus
                .call::<_, Vec<u16>>(&path, MISC_INTERFACE, "getSupportedPollRates", &())
                .await
            {
                Ok(values) => match ordered_values(values.into_iter().map(u32::from).collect(), 32)
                {
                    Ok(values) => supported_rates = Some(values),
                    Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                        ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                        format!("{path}:rates"),
                        &error,
                    )),
                },
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:rates"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let mut current_rate = None;
        if api.method(MISC_INTERFACE, "getPollRate", "", "i") {
            match bus
                .call::<_, i32>(&path, MISC_INTERFACE, "getPollRate", &())
                .await
            {
                Ok(value) => {
                    current_rate = u32::try_from(value)
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|v| *v > 0 && *v <= u32::from(u16::MAX))
                }
                Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                    ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                    format!("{path}:polling"),
                    &error,
                )),
            }
        }
        let mut battery_value = None;
        if include_batteries
            && (api.method(POWER_INTERFACE, "getBattery", "", "d")
                || api.method(POWER_INTERFACE, "isCharging", "", "b"))
        {
            let mut percentage = None;
            let mut charging = None;
            if api.method(POWER_INTERFACE, "getBattery", "", "d") {
                match bus
                    .call::<_, f64>(&path, POWER_INTERFACE, "getBattery", &())
                    .await
                {
                    Ok(value) => percentage = Some(value),
                    Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                        ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                        format!("{path}:battery"),
                        &error,
                    )),
                }
            }
            if product != VIPER_PRODUCT && api.method(POWER_INTERFACE, "isCharging", "", "b") {
                match bus
                    .call::<_, bool>(&path, POWER_INTERFACE, "isCharging", &())
                    .await
                {
                    Ok(value) => charging = Some(value),
                    Err(error) => diagnostics.push(diagnostic(
                        ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                        format!("{path}:charging"),
                        &error,
                    )),
                }
            }
            battery_value = Some(battery(percentage, charging, product, now_secs()));
        }
        let mut capabilities = DeviceCapabilities {
            dpi: dpi_capability(&api, product, maximum, discrete, current_dpi),
            polling: polling_capability(&api, product, supported_rates, current_rate),
        };
        let ambiguous_usb = usb_identities
            .iter()
            .filter(|candidate| identities_match(&identity, candidate))
            .count()
            > 1;
        if kind != DeviceKind::Mouse || ambiguous_usb {
            let reason = "Istanza mouse non verificata o seriale USB ambiguo.".to_owned();
            if let Some(cap) = &mut capabilities.dpi {
                cap.writable = false;
                cap.reason = Some(reason.clone());
            }
            if let Some(cap) = &mut capabilities.polling {
                cap.writable = false;
                cap.reason = Some(reason);
            }
        }
        let support = if capabilities.dpi.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| c.writable)
            && capabilities.polling.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| c.writable)
        {
            SupportLevel::Supported
        } else {
            SupportLevel::Partial
        };
        let mut limitations = vec!["Unico proprietario delle scritture Razer: OpenRazer gia attivo, senza auto-activation.".to_owned(),
            "Nessun remapping, RGB, LOD, calibrazione o selettore chimica batteria in questa versione.".to_owned(),
            "getPollRate e uno stato del daemon, non una misura fisica della frequenza.".to_owned()];
        if product == VIPER_PRODUCT {
            limitations.push("Batteria AA del Viper V3 HyperSpeed: non ricaricabile nel mouse, charging sempre false.".to_owned());
        }
        Ok((
            Device {
                id: format!("openrazer:{serial}"),
                name,
                brand: Brand::Razer,
                kind,
                provider: ProviderKind::Openrazer,
                identity,
                availability: AvailabilityCode::Available,
                support,
                capabilities,
                values: DeviceValues {
                    dpi: current_dpi,
                    polling_rate: current_rate,
                    battery: battery_value,
                    staged: false,
                },
                output_node_names: Vec::new(),
                limitations,
            },
            diagnostics,
        ))
    }

    async fn authoritative(bus: &Bus, previous: &Device) -> AppResult<Device> {
        bus.ensure_owner(SERVICE).await?;
        let serial = previous
            .identity
            .serial
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(valid_serial)
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("Seriale OpenRazer non verificato."))?;
        if serials(bus).await?.iter().filter(|s| *s == &serial).count() != 1 {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::DeviceNotFound,
                "Istanza OpenRazer scollegata o ambigua.",
            ));
        }
        let usb = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(crate::devices::read_usb)
            .await
            .map_err(|error| invalid_data("Worker USB interrotto.").detail(error.to_string()))??;
        let (device, _) = read_device(bus, &serial, &usb, false).await?;
        if !identities_match(&previous.identity, &device.identity)
            || device.identity.backend_path != previous.identity.backend_path
            || previous
                .identity
                .physical_path
                .as_ref()
                .is_some_and(|path| device.identity.physical_path.as_ref() != Some(path))
            || device.kind != DeviceKind::Mouse
            || device.brand != Brand::Razer
        {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::DeviceNotFound,
                "Identita OpenRazer cambiata; aggiornare la discovery.",
            ));
        }
        Ok(device)
    }

    pub(crate) async fn set_dpi(previous: &Device, x: u32, y: u32) -> AppResult<()> {
        let bus = manager().await?;
        let device = authoritative(&bus, previous).await?;
        let capability = device
            .capabilities
            .dpi
            .as_ref()
            .filter(|c| c.writable)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Unsupported,
                    "DPI OpenRazer attualmente di sola lettura.",
                )
            })?;
        if !capability.values.contains(x)
            || !capability.values.contains(y)
            || (!capability.separate_axes && x != y)
        {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::Validation,
                "DPI non ammessi dal modello OpenRazer attivo.",
            ));
        }
        let x_wire = u16::try_from(x)
            .map_err(|_| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Validation, "DPI oltre UInt16."))?;
        let y_wire = if capability.separate_axes {
            u16::try_from(y)
                .map_err(|_| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Validation, "DPI oltre UInt16."))?
        } else {
            0
        };
        let path = device
            .identity
            .backend_path
            .as_deref()
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("Percorso OpenRazer assente."))?;
        bus.call::<_, ()>(path, DPI_INTERFACE, "setDPI", &(x_wire, y_wire))
            .await?;
        let readback = authoritative(&bus, &device).await.map_err(|error| {
            AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::NotConfirmed,
                "DPI inviati ma dispositivo/readback non confermati.",
            )
            .detail(error.to_string())
        })?;
        if readback.values.dpi != Some(DpiValue { x, y }) {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::NotConfirmed,
                "OpenRazer non ha confermato i DPI richiesti.",
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(crate) async fn set_polling_rate(previous: &Device, rate: u32) -> AppResult<()> {
        let bus = manager().await?;
        let device = authoritative(&bus, previous).await?;
        let capability = device
            .capabilities
            .polling
            .as_ref()
            .filter(|c| c.writable)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Unsupported,
                    "Polling OpenRazer attualmente di sola lettura.",
                )
            })?;
        if !capability.values.contains(&rate) {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::Validation,
                "Rate non ammesso da OpenRazer per il modello attivo.",
            ));
        }
        let rate = u16::try_from(rate)
            .map_err(|_| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Validation, "Rate oltre UInt16."))?;
        let path = device
            .identity
            .backend_path
            .as_deref()
            .ok_or_else(|| invalid_data("Percorso OpenRazer assente."))?;
        bus.call::<_, ()>(path, MISC_INTERFACE, "setPollRate", &(rate,))
            .await?;
        let readback = authoritative(&bus, &device).await.map_err(|error| {
            AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::NotConfirmed,
                "Polling inviato ma readback non confermato.",
            )
            .detail(error.to_string())
        })?;
        if readback.values.polling_rate != Some(u32::from(rate)) {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::NotConfirmed,
                "OpenRazer non ha confermato il polling richiesto.",
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}
