use crate::models::*;
use crate::providers::{identities_match, unique_identity_match, valid_serial};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct UsbMetadata {
    pub identity: DeviceIdentity,
    pub name: String,
    pub is_audio: bool,
    pub bus_alias: Option<String>,
    pub instance: String,
}

pub fn usb_device(usb: &UsbMetadata) -> Option<Device> {
    let brand = match usb.identity.vendor_id? {
        0x1532 => Brand::Razer,
        0x046d => Brand::Logitech,
        _ => return None,
    };
    let product = usb.identity.product_id?;
    let mut limitations =
        vec!["Discovery USB di soli metadata; nessun pacchetto HID inviato.".to_owned()];
    let (kind, fallback_name) = match (brand, product) {
        (Brand::Razer, 0x00b8) => {
            limitations.push("DPI, polling e batteria richiedono OpenRazer gia attivo.".to_owned());
            (DeviceKind::Mouse, "Razer Viper V3 HyperSpeed")
        }
        (Brand::Razer, 0x057c | 0x057d) => {
            limitations.push("ID BlackShark V3 X HyperSpeed proposto nella PR OpenRazer #2899 non integrata; modello e trasporto da verificare.".to_owned());
            limitations.push("OpenRazer stock non supporta questa cuffia. Solo audio PipeWire; batteria e controlli onboard non disponibili.".to_owned());
            if product == 0x057d {
                limitations.push(
                    "ID del dongle audio, non prova dell'identita/firmware della cuffia associata."
                        .to_owned(),
                );
            }
            (
                DeviceKind::Headset,
                "Audio Razer, possibile BlackShark V3 X HyperSpeed",
            )
        }
        (Brand::Razer, 0x00b3) => {
            limitations.push("HyperPolling receiver separato: non identifica il mouse associato e non prova il supporto 8000 Hz.".to_owned());
            (DeviceKind::Receiver, "Razer HyperPolling receiver")
        }
        (Brand::Logitech, 0xc547) => {
            limitations.push("Receiver SUPERLIGHT: non e il mouse HID++ 4093, ne prova il supporto SUPERLIGHT 2.".to_owned());
            (DeviceKind::Receiver, "Logitech SUPERLIGHT receiver")
        }
        (Brand::Logitech, 0xc08b | 0xc094) => (DeviceKind::Mouse, "Mouse Logitech USB"),
        (_, _) if usb.is_audio => {
            limitations.push("Audio standard disponibile dove esposto da PipeWire; controlli proprietari non presunti.".to_owned());
            (DeviceKind::Headset, "Audio USB")
        }
        _ => (DeviceKind::Unknown, "Periferica USB"),
    };
    let name = if usb.name.trim().is_empty() {
        fallback_name.to_owned()
    } else {
        usb.name.clone()
    };
    Some(Device {
        id: format!("usb:{}", usb.identity.physical_path.as_deref()?),
        name,
        brand,
        kind,
        provider: ProviderKind::Usb,
        identity: usb.identity.clone(),
        availability: AvailabilityCode::Available,
        support: if kind == DeviceKind::Headset {
            SupportLevel::Partial
        } else {
            SupportLevel::Unsupported
        },
        capabilities: DeviceCapabilities::default(),
        values: DeviceValues::default(),
        output_node_names: Vec::new(),
        limitations,
    })
}

pub fn usb_physical_path(value: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let value = value.strip_prefix("/sys").unwrap_or(value);
    if !value.starts_with("/devices/")
        || value.len() > 4096
        || value.contains('\\')
        || value.chars().any(char::is_control)
    {
        return None;
    }
    let mut components = Vec::new();
    let mut usb_root = None;
    for component in value.split('/').skip(1) {
        if component.is_empty() || matches!(component, "." | "..") {
            return None;
        }
        components.push(component);
        if let Some((bus, ports)) = component.split_once('-') {
            if !bus.is_empty()
                && bus.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
                && !ports.is_empty()
                && ports
                    .split('.')
                    .all(|p| !p.is_empty() && p.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
            {
                usb_root = Some(format!("/sys/{}", components.join("/")));
            }
        }
    }
    usb_root
}

pub fn usb_bus_alias(physical_path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let root = usb_physical_path(physical_path)?;
    let sysname = root.rsplit('/').next()?;
    let (_, ports) = sysname.split_once('-')?;
    let pci = root.split('/').rev().find(|part| {
        part.len() == 12
            && part.as_bytes()[4] == b':'
            && part.as_bytes()[7] == b':'
            && part.as_bytes()[10] == b'.'
            && part
                .bytes()
                .enumerate()
                .all(|(i, b)| matches!(i, 4 | 7 | 10) || b.is_ascii_hexdigit())
    })?;
    Some(format!("pci-{pci}-usb-0:{ports}"))
}

pub fn verified_usb_path(value: &str, devices: &[UsbMetadata]) -> Option<usize> {
    let physical = usb_physical_path(value);
    let mut matches = devices.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, usb)| {
        if physical
            .as_deref()
            .is_some_and(|path| Some(path) == usb.identity.physical_path.as_deref())
        {
            return true;
        }
        usb.bus_alias.as_deref().is_some_and(|alias| {
            value == alias
                || value
                    .strip_prefix(alias)
                    .and_then(|suffix| suffix.strip_prefix(':'))
                    .is_some_and(|suffix| {
                        let Some((configuration, interface)) = suffix.split_once('.') else {
                            return false;
                        };
                        !configuration.is_empty()
                            && configuration.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
                            && !interface.is_empty()
                            && interface.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
                    })
        })
    });
    let (index, _) = matches.next()?;
    matches.next().is_none().then_some(index)
}

pub fn merge_usb(discovery: &mut Discovery, usb: &[UsbMetadata]) {
    for metadata in usb {
        let Some(mut device) = usb_device(metadata) else {
            continue;
        };
        let candidates: Vec<_> = discovery
            .devices
            .iter()
            .filter(|candidate| {
                candidate.brand == device.brand
                    && (candidate.kind == device.kind
                        || candidate.kind == DeviceKind::Unknown
                        || device.kind == DeviceKind::Unknown)
                    && !(candidate.kind == DeviceKind::Receiver && device.kind == DeviceKind::Mouse)
            })
            .map(usb_match_identity)
            .collect();
        if unique_identity_match(&device.identity, &candidates).is_some() {
            let index = discovery.devices.iter().position(|candidate| {
                candidate.brand == device.brand
                    && identities_match(&usb_match_identity(candidate), &device.identity)
                    && (candidate.kind == device.kind
                        || candidate.kind == DeviceKind::Unknown
                        || device.kind == DeviceKind::Unknown)
            });
            if let Some(index) = index {
                let usb_identities: Vec<_> = usb.iter().map(|usb| usb.identity.clone()).collect();
                if unique_identity_match(
                    &usb_match_identity(&discovery.devices[index]),
                    &usb_identities,
                )
                .is_none()
                {
                    discovery.devices.push(device);
                    continue;
                }
                let candidate = &mut discovery.devices[index];
                if candidate.identity.physical_path.is_none() {
                    candidate.identity.physical_path = device.identity.physical_path.take();
                }
                if candidate.identity.serial.is_none() {
                    candidate.identity.serial = device.identity.serial.take();
                }
                if candidate.identity.transport.is_none() {
                    candidate.identity.transport = device.identity.transport.take();
                }
                for limitation in device.limitations {
                    if !candidate.limitations.contains(&limitation) {
                        candidate.limitations.push(limitation);
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }
        }
        discovery.devices.push(device);
    }
}

fn usb_match_identity(device: &Device) -> DeviceIdentity {
    let mut identity = device.identity.clone();
    if device.provider == ProviderKind::Ratbag && device.kind == DeviceKind::Mouse {
        // Collapse a verified HID child only for USB metadata association. Matching
        // VID/PID still prevents a logical HID++ mouse being merged with its receiver.
        if let Some(path) = identity
            .physical_path
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(usb_physical_path)
        {
            identity.physical_path = Some(path);
        }
    }
    identity
}

pub fn associate_audio(discovery: &mut Discovery, audio_nodes: &[AudioNode], usb: &[UsbMetadata]) {
    for node in audio_nodes
        .iter()
        .filter(|node| !node.is_virtual && node.media_class == "Audio/Sink")
    {
        let mut identity = DeviceIdentity {
            serial: node.device_serial.as_deref().and_then(valid_serial),
            vendor_id: node.vendor_id,
            product_id: node.product_id,
            ..DeviceIdentity::default()
        };
        if let Some(index) = node
            .device_bus_path
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(|path| verified_usb_path(path, usb))
        {
            let physical = &usb[index].identity;
            if !identity
                .vendor_id
                .zip(physical.vendor_id)
                .is_some_and(|(a, b)| a != b)
                && !identity
                    .product_id
                    .zip(physical.product_id)
                    .is_some_and(|(a, b)| a != b)
                && !identity
                    .serial
                    .as_deref()
                    .zip(physical.serial.as_deref())
                    .is_some_and(|(a, b)| a != b)
            {
                identity.physical_path = physical.physical_path.clone();
                if identity.serial.is_none() {
                    identity.serial = physical.serial.clone();
                }
            }
        }
        let indices: Vec<_> = discovery
            .devices
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter(|(_, device)| {
                device.kind == DeviceKind::Headset
                    && device.brand != Brand::Generic
                    && identities_match(&identity, &device.identity)
            })
            .map(|(index, _)| index)
            .collect();
        if indices.len() == 1 {
            discovery.devices[indices[0]]
                .output_node_names
                .push(node.node_name.clone());
        } else {
            discovery.devices.push(Device {
                id: format!("pipewire:{}", node.node_name), name: node.description.clone(), brand: Brand::Generic,
                kind: DeviceKind::Headset, provider: ProviderKind::Pipewire,
                identity: DeviceIdentity { transport: node.transport.clone(), ..identity },
                availability: AvailabilityCode::Available, support: SupportLevel::Partial,
                capabilities: DeviceCapabilities::default(), values: DeviceValues::default(),
                output_node_names: vec![node.node_name.clone()],
                limitations: vec!["Uscita audio PipeWire; identita vendor non associata con certezza. Nessuna batteria o funzione onboard presunta.".to_owned()],
            });
        }
    }
}

pub fn associate_headset_batteries(
    discovery: &mut Discovery,
    usb: &[UsbMetadata],
    report: &crate::providers::headsetcontrol::Report,
) {
    for reading in &report.devices {
        let json_count = report
            .devices
            .iter()
            .filter(|r| r.vendor_id == reading.vendor_id && r.product_id == reading.product_id)
            .count();
        let matching_usb: Vec<_> = usb
            .iter()
            .filter(|usb| {
                usb.identity.vendor_id == Some(reading.vendor_id)
                    && usb.identity.product_id == Some(reading.product_id)
            })
            .collect();
        if json_count != 1 || matching_usb.len() != 1 {
            discovery.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(
                "headsetcontrol-ambiguous",
                ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol,
                AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
                Severity::Warning,
                "Batteria cuffie non associata: JSON senza seriale o piu istanze USB identiche.",
            ));
            continue;
        }
        let metadata = matching_usb[0];
        let candidates: Vec<_> = discovery
            .devices
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter(|(_, device)| {
                device.brand == Brand::Logitech
                    && device.kind == DeviceKind::Headset
                    && identities_match(&metadata.identity, &device.identity)
            })
            .map(|(index, _)| index)
            .collect();
        if candidates.len() == 1 {
            let device = &mut discovery.devices[candidates[0]];
            device.provider = ProviderKind::Headsetcontrol;
            device.values.battery = Some(reading.battery.clone());
            device.support = SupportLevel::Partial;
            device
                .limitations
                .push("HeadsetControl usato solo per -b -o json; nessun setter vendor.".to_owned());
        }
    }
}

pub async fn discover(audio_nodes: &[AudioNode], include_batteries: bool) -> Discovery {
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        linux::discover(audio_nodes, include_batteries).await
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    {
        let _ = (audio_nodes, include_batteries);
        Discovery {
            devices: Vec::new(),
            diagnostics: vec![Diagnostic::new(
                "hardware-platform",
                ProviderKind::App,
                AvailabilityCode::UnsupportedPlatform,
                Severity::Info,
                AppError::unsupported_platform().message,
            )],
        }
    }
}

pub async fn set_dpi(device: &Device, x: u32, y: u32) -> AppResult<()> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        validate_owner(device)?;
        match device.provider {
            ProviderKind::Openrazer => crate::providers::openrazer::set_dpi(device, x, y).await,
            ProviderKind::Ratbag => crate::providers::ratbag::set_dpi(device, x, y).await,
            _ => Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::Unsupported,
                "Nessun proprietario dei controlli DPI.",
            )),
        }
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    {
        let _ = (device, x, y);
        Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
    }
}

pub async fn set_polling_rate(device: &Device, rate: u32) -> AppResult<()> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        validate_owner(device)?;
        match device.provider {
            ProviderKind::Openrazer => {
                crate::providers::openrazer::set_polling_rate(device, rate).await
            }
            ProviderKind::Ratbag => crate::providers::ratbag::set_polling_rate(device, rate).await,
            _ => Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::Unsupported,
                "Nessun proprietario del polling hardware.",
            )),
        }
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    {
        let _ = (device, rate);
        Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn validate_owner(device: &Device) -> AppResult<()> {
    if device.kind != DeviceKind::Mouse
        || device.availability != AvailabilityCode::Available
        || !matches!(
            (device.brand, device.provider),
            (Brand::Razer, ProviderKind::Openrazer) | (Brand::Logitech, ProviderKind::Ratbag)
        )
    {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Unsupported,
            "Dispositivo senza proprietario hardware scrivibile.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn watch_changes(sender: tokio::sync::mpsc::UnboundedSender<()>) {
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        tokio::join!(
            linux::watch_bus(sender.clone(), true, &[("org.razer", "/org/razer")]),
            linux::watch_bus(
                sender,
                false,
                &[
                    ("org.freedesktop.ratbag1", "/org/freedesktop/ratbag1"),
                    ("org.freedesktop.UPower", "/org/freedesktop/UPower")
                ]
            )
        );
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    {
        let _ = sender;
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) use linux::{ratbag_identity, read_usb};

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod linux {
    use super::*;
    use crate::providers::{
        self, bounded_text, dbus, diagnostic, hex_id, invalid_data, MAX_DEVICES,
    };
    use std::{
        collections::HashSet,
        fs,
        io::Read,
        path::{Path, PathBuf},
        time::Duration,
    };
    use tokio::sync::mpsc::UnboundedSender;
    use zbus::{MatchRule, MessageStream};

    fn text(path: &Path) -> AppResult<String> {
        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        fs::File::open(path)?.take(4097).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
        if bytes.len() > 4096 {
            return Err(invalid_data("Metadata sysfs troppo grandi."));
        }
        String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| invalid_data("Metadata sysfs non UTF-8."))
    }

    pub(crate) fn read_usb() -> AppResult<Vec<UsbMetadata>> {
        let root = Path::new("/sys/bus/usb/devices");
        let mut devices = Vec::new();
        let mut seen = HashSet::new();
        for (index, entry) in fs::read_dir(root)?.enumerate() {
            if index >= 2048 {
                return Err(invalid_data("Troppi oggetti USB sysfs."));
            }
            let entry = entry?;
            let path = entry.path();
            let Ok(vendor_text) = text(&path.join("idVendor")) else {
                continue;
            };
            let Some(vendor_id) = hex_id(&vendor_text) else {
                continue;
            };
            if !matches!(vendor_id, 0x1532 | 0x046d) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(product_id) = text(&path.join("idProduct"))
                .ok()
                .as_deref()
                .and_then(hex_id)
            else {
                continue;
            };
            let canonical = fs::canonicalize(&path)?;
            let Some(physical_path) = canonical
                .to_str()
                .filter(|p| p.starts_with("/sys/devices/"))
                .map(str::to_owned)
            else {
                continue;
            };
            if !seen.insert(physical_path.clone()) {
                continue;
            }
            let sysname = entry.file_name();
            let sysname = sysname.to_string_lossy();
            let mut is_audio = false;
            for interface in fs::read_dir(&canonical)?.take(128).flatten() {
                if interface
                    .file_name()
                    .to_string_lossy()
                    .starts_with(&format!("{sysname}:"))
                    && text(&interface.path().join("bInterfaceClass"))
                        .ok()
                        .as_deref()
                        .and_then(hex_id)
                        == Some(1)
                {
                    is_audio = true;
                }
            }
            let serial = text(&path.join("serial"))
                .ok()
                .as_deref()
                .and_then(valid_serial);
            let name = text(&path.join("product"))
                .ok()
                .map(|s| bounded_text(s.trim()))
                .unwrap_or_default();
            let instance = format!(
                "{}:{}:{}",
                physical_path,
                text(&path.join("busnum")).unwrap_or_default().trim(),
                text(&path.join("devnum")).unwrap_or_default().trim()
            );
            let bus_alias = usb_bus_alias(&physical_path);
            devices.push(UsbMetadata {
                identity: DeviceIdentity {
                    serial,
                    physical_path: Some(physical_path),
                    vendor_id: Some(vendor_id),
                    product_id: Some(product_id),
                    transport: Some("usb".to_owned()),
                    ..DeviceIdentity::default()
                },
                name,
                is_audio,
                bus_alias,
                instance,
            });
            if devices.len() > MAX_DEVICES {
                return Err(invalid_data("Troppi dispositivi USB vendor."));
            }
        }
        devices.sort_by(|a, b| a.instance.cmp(&b.instance));
        Ok(devices)
    }

    pub(crate) fn ratbag_identity(
        backend_path: &str,
        vendor_id: u16,
        product_id: u16,
    ) -> DeviceIdentity {
        let mut identity = DeviceIdentity {
            vendor_id: Some(vendor_id),
            product_id: Some(product_id),
            backend_path: Some(backend_path.to_owned()),
            ..DeviceIdentity::default()
        };
        let Some(sysname) = backend_path.strip_prefix("/org/freedesktop/ratbag1/device/") else {
            return identity;
        };
        let Some(number) = sysname.strip_prefix("hidraw") else {
            return identity;
        };
        if number.is_empty() || !number.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
            return identity;
        }
        // The sysname comes from Manager.Devices, never from an assumed hidraw index.
        let Ok(path) =
            fs::canonicalize(Path::new("/sys/class/hidraw").join(sysname).join("device"))
        else {
            return identity;
        };
        let Some(physical_path) = path.to_str().filter(|p| p.starts_with("/sys/devices/")) else {
            return identity;
        };
        let Ok(uevent) = text(&path.join("uevent")) else {
            return identity;
        };
        let Some(hid_id) = uevent.lines().find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("HID_ID=")) else {
            return identity;
        };
        let parts: Vec<_> = hid_id.split(':').collect();
        if parts.len() != 3
            || u32::from_str_radix(parts[1], 16).ok() != Some(u32::from(vendor_id))
            || u32::from_str_radix(parts[2], 16).ok() != Some(u32::from(product_id))
        {
            return identity;
        }
        identity.serial = uevent
            .lines()
            .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("HID_UNIQ="))
            .and_then(valid_serial);
        identity.physical_path = Some(physical_path.to_owned());
        identity
    }

    fn native_identity_path(value: &str, usb: &[UsbMetadata], device_type: u32) -> Option<String> {
        if matches!(device_type, 17 | 19) {
            if let Some(index) = verified_usb_path(value, usb).filter(|index| usb[*index].is_audio)
            {
                return usb[index].identity.physical_path.clone();
            }
        }
        if value.len() > 4096
            || value.chars().any(char::is_control)
            || value.split('/').any(|p| matches!(p, "." | ".."))
        {
            return None;
        }
        let path = if value.starts_with("/sys/") {
            PathBuf::from(value)
        } else if !value.is_empty()
            && value
                .bytes()
                .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'_' | b'-' | b':'))
        {
            Path::new("/sys/class/power_supply").join(value)
        } else {
            return None;
        };
        let canonical = fs::canonicalize(path.join("device"))
            .or_else(|_| fs::canonicalize(&path))
            .ok()?;
        let path = canonical.to_str()?.strip_prefix("/sys/devices/")?;
        let path = format!("/sys/devices/{path}");
        Some(path.split("/power_supply/").next()?.to_owned())
    }

    fn associate_upower(
        discovery: &mut Discovery,
        usb: &[UsbMetadata],
        readings: &[providers::upower::Reading],
    ) {
        let mut assignments: Vec<(usize, &providers::upower::Reading)> = Vec::new();
        for reading in readings {
            let identity = DeviceIdentity {
                serial: reading.serial.clone(),
                physical_path: reading
                    .native_path
                    .as_deref()
                    .and_then(|p| native_identity_path(p, usb, reading.device_type)),
                ..DeviceIdentity::default()
            };
            let matches: Vec<_> = discovery
                .devices
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .filter(|(_, device)| {
                    device.brand != Brand::Generic
                        && providers::upower::compatible_type(reading.device_type, device.kind)
                        && identities_match(&identity, &device.identity)
                })
                .map(|(index, _)| index)
                .collect();
            if matches.len() == 1 {
                assignments.push((matches[0], reading));
            } else if !matches.is_empty() {
                discovery.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(
                    "upower-ambiguous",
                    ProviderKind::Upower,
                    AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
                    Severity::Warning,
                    "Batteria UPower non associata: identita periferica ambigua.",
                ));
            }
        }
        for (index, reading) in &assignments {
            if assignments
                .iter()
                .filter(|(candidate, _)| candidate == index)
                .count()
                != 1
            {
                continue;
            }
            let device = &mut discovery.devices[*index];
            let vendor_sample = device
                .values
                .battery
                .as_ref()
                .is_some_and(|b| b.percentage.is_some() || b.level.is_some());
            if !vendor_sample {
                device.values.battery = Some(reading.battery.clone());
            }
            if device.brand == Brand::Razer && device.identity.product_id == Some(0x00b8) {
                if let Some(battery) = &mut device.values.battery {
                    battery.charging = Some(false);
                }
            }
            if reading.battery.stale {
                device.limitations.push("Campione UPower vecchio o timestamp sorgente sconosciuto; non e una nuova misura hardware.".to_owned());
            }
        }
    }

    pub(super) async fn discover(audio_nodes: &[AudioNode], include_batteries: bool) -> Discovery {
        let (usb, usb_error) = match tokio::task::spawn_blocking(read_usb).await {
            Ok(Ok(usb)) => (usb, None),
            Ok(Err(error)) => (Vec::new(), Some(error)),
            Err(error) => (
                Vec::new(),
                Some(invalid_data("Worker USB interrotto.").detail(error.to_string())),
            ),
        };
        let (razer, ratbag, (power, power_diagnostics), headset) = tokio::join!(
            providers::openrazer::discover(&usb, include_batteries),
            providers::ratbag::discover(),
            async {
                if include_batteries {
                    providers::upower::discover().await
                } else {
                    (Vec::new(), Vec::new())
                }
            },
            async {
                if include_batteries {
                    providers::headsetcontrol::discover(&usb).await
                } else {
                    providers::headsetcontrol::Report::default()
                }
            }
        );
        let mut discovery = razer;
        discovery.devices.extend(ratbag.devices);
        discovery.diagnostics.extend(ratbag.diagnostics);
        discovery.diagnostics.extend(power_diagnostics);
        discovery.diagnostics.extend(headset.diagnostics.clone());
        if let Some(error) = usb_error {
            discovery
                .diagnostics
                .push(diagnostic(ProviderKind::Usb, "usb", &error));
        }
        merge_usb(&mut discovery, &usb);
        associate_headset_batteries(&mut discovery, &usb, &headset);
        associate_upower(&mut discovery, &usb, &power);
        associate_audio(&mut discovery, audio_nodes, &usb);
        discovery
    }

    pub(super) async fn watch_bus(
        sender: UnboundedSender<()>,
        session: bool,
        services: &[(&str, &str)],
    ) {
        let mut retry = Duration::from_secs(1);
        loop {
            if sender.is_closed() {
                return;
            }
            if let Ok(connection) = dbus::connection(session).await {
                let mut streams = Vec::new();
                for (service, root) in services {
                    let signal_rule = MatchRule::builder()
                        .msg_type(zbus::message::Type::Signal)
                        .sender(*service)
                        .and_then(|rule| rule.path_namespace(*root))
                        .map(|rule| rule.build().to_owned());
                    let owner_rule = MatchRule::builder()
                        .msg_type(zbus::message::Type::Signal)
                        .sender("org.freedesktop.DBus")
                        .and_then(|rule| rule.interface("org.freedesktop.DBus"))
                        .and_then(|rule| rule.member("NameOwnerChanged"))
                        .and_then(|rule| rule.arg(0, *service))
                        .map(|rule| rule.build().to_owned());
                    for rule in [signal_rule, owner_rule].into_iter().flatten() {
                        if let Ok(Ok(stream)) = tokio::time::timeout(
                            dbus::CALL_TIMEOUT,
                            MessageStream::for_match_rule(rule, &connection, Some(64)),
                        )
                        .await
                        {
                            streams.push(stream);
                        }
                    }
                }
                if streams.len() == services.len() * 2 {
                    retry = Duration::from_secs(1);
                    if sender.send(()).is_err() {
                        return;
                    }
                    let mut dirty = false;
                    let mut deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now();
                    loop {
                        let next = std::future::poll_fn(|cx| {
                            for stream in &mut streams {
                                let result = zbus::export::futures_core::Stream::poll_next(
                                    std::pin::Pin::new(stream),
                                    cx,
                                );
                                if result.is_ready() {
                                    return result;
                                }
                            }
                            std::task::Poll::Pending
                        });
                        tokio::select! {
                            _ = sender.closed() => return,
                            message = next => match message {
                                Some(Ok(_)) => { if !dirty { deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(150); } dirty = true; }
                                _ => { let _ = sender.send(()); break; },
                            },
                            _ = tokio::time::sleep_until(deadline), if dirty => {
                                if sender.send(()).is_err() { return; }
                                dirty = false;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            tokio::select! { _ = sender.closed() => return, _ = tokio::time::sleep(retry) => {} }
            retry = (retry * 2).min(Duration::from_secs(30));
        }
    }
}

pub mod audio;
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
mod commands;
pub mod devices;
pub mod models;
pub mod process;
pub mod profiles;
pub mod providers;
pub mod surround;

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use models::*;
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use std::path::PathBuf;
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex as StdMutex};
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use tauri::menu::{Menu, MenuEvent, MenuItem, PredefinedMenuItem};
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use tauri::tray::{MouseButton, MouseButtonState, TrayIconBuilder, TrayIconEvent};
#[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", target_os = "linux"))]
use tauri::Emitter;
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use tauri::{AppHandle, Manager};

use tokio::sync::Mutex;
#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
use tokio::sync::RwLock;

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
pub(crate) enum AudioMutation {
    DefaultOutput(String),
    Volume(String, f64),
    Mute(String, bool),
    Surround(bool),
    Import(PathBuf),
    Apply(AudioProfile),
}

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
pub(crate) struct AppState {
    snapshot: RwLock<Snapshot>,
    tasks: StdMutex<Vec<tauri::async_runtime::JoinHandle<()>>>,
    monitor_task: StdMutex<Option<tauri::async_runtime::JoinHandle<()>>>,
    shutdown_started: AtomicBool,
    exit_ready: AtomicBool,
    tray_created: AtomicBool,
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    hardware: Mutex<()>,
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    audio: Mutex<audio::AudioController>,
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    profiles: Mutex<profiles::ProfileStore>,
}

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
impl AppState {
    fn new() -> AppResult<Self> {
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        let profiles = profiles::ProfileStore::new()?;
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        let audio = audio::AudioController::new(profiles.paths().clone());
        Ok(Self {
            snapshot: RwLock::new(Snapshot::default()),
            tasks: StdMutex::new(Vec::new()),
            monitor_task: StdMutex::new(None),
            shutdown_started: AtomicBool::new(false),
            exit_ready: AtomicBool::new(false),
            tray_created: AtomicBool::new(false),
            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
            hardware: Mutex::new(()),
            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
            audio: Mutex::new(audio),
            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
            profiles: Mutex::new(profiles),
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn require_platform(&self) -> AppResult<()> {
        if !cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
            return Err(AppError::unsupported_platform());
        }
        if self.shutdown_started.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::Busy,
                "Chiusura in corso: nessuna nuova operazione consentita.",
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(crate) async fn get_snapshot(&self) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        Ok(self.snapshot.read().await.clone())
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn publish(&self, app: &AppHandle, update: impl FnOnce(&mut Snapshot)) -> Snapshot {
        let mut snapshot = self.snapshot.write().await;
        update(&mut snapshot);
        snapshot.revision = snapshot.revision.saturating_add(1);
        let confirmed = snapshot.clone();
        drop(snapshot);
        let _ = app.emit("snapshot-changed", &confirmed);
        confirmed
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn publish_audio(
        &self,
        app: &AppHandle,
        audio: AudioSnapshot,
        error: Option<&AppError>,
    ) -> Snapshot {
        self.publish(app, |snapshot| {
            snapshot.audio = audio;
            snapshot
                .diagnostics
                .retain(|diagnostic| diagnostic.id != "audio-runtime");
            let diagnostic = match error {
                Some(error) => {
                    diagnostic_from_error("audio-runtime", ProviderKind::Pipewire, error)
                }
                None => Diagnostic::new(
                    "audio-runtime",
                    ProviderKind::Pipewire,
                    if snapshot.audio.available {
                        AvailabilityCode::Available
                    } else {
                        AvailabilityCode::Absent
                    },
                    Severity::Info,
                    if snapshot.audio.available {
                        "PipeWire connesso. Routing per nuovi stream auto-collegati."
                    } else {
                        "Nessuna uscita PipeWire disponibile."
                    },
                ),
            };
            snapshot.diagnostics.push(diagnostic);
        })
        .await
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn refresh_audio(&self, app: &AppHandle) {
        let mut audio = self.audio.lock().await;
        let result = audio.refresh().await;
        self.publish_audio(app, audio.snapshot(), result.as_ref().err())
            .await;
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn refresh_hardware_locked(
        &self,
        app: &AppHandle,
        include_batteries: bool,
        preserve: Option<(Device, bool)>,
    ) -> Snapshot {
        let previous = self.snapshot.read().await.clone();
        let nodes: Vec<_> = previous
            .audio
            .outputs
            .iter()
            .chain(&previous.audio.inputs)
            .cloned()
            .collect();
        let mut discovery = devices::discover(&nodes, include_batteries).await;
        for device in &mut discovery.devices {
            if device.values.staged {
                let old = previous
                    .devices
                    .iter()
                    .find(|old| old.id == device.id && old.identity == device.identity);
                device.values.dpi = old.and_then(|old| old.values.dpi);
                device.values.polling_rate = old.and_then(|old| old.values.polling_rate);
            }
            if !include_batteries {
                if let Some(old) = previous
                    .devices
                    .iter()
                    .find(|old| old.id == device.id && old.identity == device.identity)
                {
                    if device.values.battery.is_none() {
                        device.values.battery = old.values.battery.clone().map(|mut battery| {
                            battery.stale |= now_secs().saturating_sub(battery.observed_at) > 90;
                            battery
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            // An unconfirmed ratbag staging value must not replace the last confirmed UI value.
            if let Some((old, is_dpi)) = &preserve {
                if old.id == device.id && old.identity == device.identity {
                    if *is_dpi {
                        device.values.dpi = old.values.dpi;
                    } else {
                        device.values.polling_rate = old.values.polling_rate;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        self.publish(app, |snapshot| {
            snapshot.devices = discovery.devices;
            snapshot.diagnostics.retain(|diagnostic| {
                matches!(
                    diagnostic.provider,
                    ProviderKind::Pipewire | ProviderKind::App
                )
            });
            snapshot.diagnostics.extend(discovery.diagnostics);
        })
        .await
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn refresh_hardware(&self, app: &AppHandle, include_batteries: bool) -> Snapshot {
        let _guard = self.hardware.lock().await;
        self.refresh_hardware_locked(app, include_batteries, None)
            .await
    }

    pub(crate) async fn refresh_all(&self, app: &AppHandle) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            self.refresh_audio(app).await;
            Ok(self.refresh_hardware(app, true).await)
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        {
            let _ = app;
            Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
        }
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn device(&self, id: &str) -> AppResult<Device> {
        self.snapshot
            .read()
            .await
            .devices
            .iter()
            .find(|device| device.id == id)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::DeviceNotFound,
                    "Dispositivo non piu' presente. Aggiorna la discovery.",
                )
            })
    }

    pub(crate) async fn set_dpi(
        &self,
        app: &AppHandle,
        id: &str,
        x: u32,
        y: u32,
    ) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            let _guard = self.hardware.lock().await;
            self.require_platform()?;
            let device = self.device(id).await?;
            let capability = device.capabilities.dpi.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Unsupported,
                    "DPI hardware non disponibili per questo dispositivo.",
                )
            })?;
            if !capability.writable
                || !capability.values.contains(x)
                || !capability.values.contains(y)
                || (!capability.separate_axes && x != y)
            {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Validation,
                    capability
                        .reason
                        .as_deref()
                        .unwrap_or("DPI o assi non ammessi dal provider proprietario."),
                ));
            }
            let result = devices::set_dpi(&device, x, y).await;
            let confirmed = self
                .refresh_hardware_locked(app, false, result.as_ref().err().map(|_| (device, true)))
                .await;
            result?;
            Ok(confirmed)
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        {
            let _ = (app, id, x, y);
            Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
        }
    }

    pub(crate) async fn set_polling_rate(
        &self,
        app: &AppHandle,
        id: &str,
        rate: u32,
    ) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            let _guard = self.hardware.lock().await;
            self.require_platform()?;
            let device = self.device(id).await?;
            let capability = device.capabilities.polling.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Unsupported,
                    "Polling hardware non disponibile per questo dispositivo.",
                )
            })?;
            if !capability.writable || !capability.values.contains(&rate) {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Validation,
                    capability
                        .reason
                        .as_deref()
                        .unwrap_or("Polling non ammesso dal provider proprietario."),
                ));
            }
            let result = devices::set_polling_rate(&device, rate).await;
            let confirmed = self
                .refresh_hardware_locked(app, false, result.as_ref().err().map(|_| (device, false)))
                .await;
            result?;
            Ok(confirmed)
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        {
            let _ = (app, id, rate);
            Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
        }
    }

    pub(crate) async fn mutate_audio(
        &self,
        app: &AppHandle,
        mutation: AudioMutation,
    ) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            let mut audio = self.audio.lock().await;
            self.require_platform()?;
            let requested = match &mutation {
                AudioMutation::Surround(enabled) => Some(*enabled),
                AudioMutation::Apply(profile) => Some(profile.surround_requested),
                AudioMutation::DefaultOutput(_)
                    if audio.snapshot().surround_state == SurroundState::On =>
                {
                    Some(true)
                }
                _ => None,
            };
            if let Some(enabled) = requested {
                let mut pending = audio.snapshot();
                if (enabled && pending.surround_state != SurroundState::On)
                    || (!enabled && pending.surround_state != SurroundState::Off)
                {
                    pending.surround_state = if enabled {
                        SurroundState::Starting
                    } else {
                        SurroundState::Stopping
                    };
                    self.publish(app, |snapshot| snapshot.audio = pending).await;
                }
            }
            let result = match mutation {
                AudioMutation::DefaultOutput(name) => audio.set_default_output(&name).await,
                AudioMutation::Volume(name, volume) => audio.set_volume(&name, volume).await,
                AudioMutation::Mute(name, muted) => audio.set_mute(&name, muted).await,
                AudioMutation::Surround(enabled) => audio.set_surround(enabled).await,
                AudioMutation::Import(path) => audio.import_hrtf(path).await,
                AudioMutation::Apply(profile) => audio.apply_profile(&profile).await,
            };
            let confirmed = self
                .publish_audio(app, audio.snapshot(), result.as_ref().err())
                .await;
            result?;
            Ok(confirmed)
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        {
            // Consume typed fields too: they never reach a CLI or a DBus bus on other OSes.
            match mutation {
                AudioMutation::DefaultOutput(name) => drop(name),
                AudioMutation::Volume(name, volume) => {
                    let _ = (name, volume);
                }
                AudioMutation::Mute(name, muted) => {
                    let _ = (name, muted);
                }
                AudioMutation::Surround(enabled) => {
                    let _ = enabled;
                }
                AudioMutation::Import(path) => drop(path),
                AudioMutation::Apply(profile) => drop(profile),
            }
            let _ = app;
            Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
        }
    }

    pub(crate) async fn save_profile(
        &self,
        app: &AppHandle,
        profile: AudioProfile,
    ) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            let mut store = self.profiles.lock().await;
            self.require_platform()?;
            let result = store.save(profile);
            drop(store);
            let confirmed = self
                .publish(app, |snapshot| {
                    snapshot
                        .diagnostics
                        .retain(|diagnostic| diagnostic.id != "profiles-write");
                    match &result {
                        Ok(profiles) => snapshot.profiles = profiles.clone(),
                        Err(error) => snapshot.diagnostics.push(diagnostic_from_error(
                            "profiles-write",
                            ProviderKind::App,
                            error,
                        )),
                    }
                })
                .await;
            result?;
            Ok(confirmed)
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        {
            let _ = (app, profile);
            Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
        }
    }

    pub(crate) async fn apply_profile(&self, app: &AppHandle, id: &str) -> AppResult<Snapshot> {
        self.require_platform()?;
        let profile = self
            .snapshot
            .read()
            .await
            .profiles
            .iter()
            .find(|profile| profile.id == id)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(ErrorCode::Validation, "Profilo non trovato o non valido.")
            })?;
        self.mutate_audio(app, AudioMutation::Apply(profile)).await
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    async fn handle_audio_event(&self, app: &AppHandle, event: audio::AudioEvent) {
        let mut audio = self.audio.lock().await;
        let topology = |snapshot: AudioSnapshot| -> Vec<_> {
            snapshot
                .outputs
                .into_iter()
                .chain(snapshot.inputs)
                .map(|node| (node.node_name, node.id, node.object_serial))
                .collect()
        };
        let before = topology(audio.snapshot());
        let result = audio.handle_event(event).await;
        let after = audio.snapshot();
        let changed = before != topology(after.clone());
        self.publish_audio(app, after, result.as_ref().err()).await;
        drop(audio);
        if changed {
            self.refresh_hardware(app, false).await;
        }
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    fn track(&self, task: tauri::async_runtime::JoinHandle<()>) {
        self.tasks
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner())
            .push(task);
    }
}

#[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", target_os = "linux"))]
fn diagnostic_from_error(id: &str, provider: ProviderKind, error: &AppError) -> Diagnostic {
    let code = match error.code {
        ErrorCode::ProviderMissing => AvailabilityCode::ProviderMissing,
        ErrorCode::PermissionDenied => AvailabilityCode::PermissionDenied,
        ErrorCode::Unsupported | ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform => AvailabilityCode::Unsupported,
        _ => AvailabilityCode::Unknown,
    };
    let mut diagnostic =
        Diagnostic::new(id, provider, code, Severity::Warning, error.message.clone());
    diagnostic.detail = error.detail.clone();
    diagnostic
}

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
fn show_window(app: &AppHandle) {
    if let Some(window) = app.get_webview_window("main") {
        let _ = window.show();
        let _ = window.unminimize();
        let _ = window.set_focus();
    }
}

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
pub(crate) fn request_exit(app: AppHandle) {
    let state = app.state::<Arc<AppState>>().inner().clone();
    if state.shutdown_started.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst) {
        return;
    }
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
        let tasks = std::mem::take(
            &mut *state
                .tasks
                .lock()
                .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner()),
        );
        for task in &tasks {
            task.abort();
        }
        for task in tasks {
            let _ = task.await;
        }
        let monitor = state
            .monitor_task
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner())
            .take();
        if let Some(mut monitor) = monitor {
            if tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3), &mut monitor)
                .await
                .is_err()
            {
                monitor.abort();
                let _ = monitor.await;
            }
        }
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        if let Err(error) = state.audio.lock().await.shutdown().await {
            eprintln!("Ripristino audio alla chiusura: {}", error.message);
        }
        state.exit_ready.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        app.exit(0);
    });
}

#[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", test, not(target_os = "linux")))]
mod platform_tests {
    use super::*;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn native_non_linux_snapshot_is_never_a_demo() {
        let state = AppState::new().unwrap();
        assert!(!state.snapshot.read().await.demo);
        assert_eq!(
            state.get_snapshot().await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform
        );
    }
}

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
fn menu_event(app: &AppHandle, event: MenuEvent) {
    match event.id().as_ref() {
        "open" => show_window(app),
        "surround-off" => {
            let app = app.clone();
            let state = app.state::<Arc<AppState>>().inner().clone();
            tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
                let _ = state
                    .mutate_audio(&app, AudioMutation::Surround(false))
                    .await;
            });
        }
        "exit" => request_exit(app.clone()),
        _ => {}
    }
}

#[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", target_os = "linux"))]
async fn tray_host_available() -> bool {
    let check = async {
        let connection = zbus::Connection::session().await.ok()?;
        let bus = zbus::fdo::DBusProxy::new(&connection).await.ok()?;
        let name = zbus::names::BusName::try_from("org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher").ok()?;
        if !bus.name_has_owner(name).await.ok()? {
            return Some(false);
        }
        let watcher = zbus::Proxy::new(
            &connection,
            "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
            "/StatusNotifierWatcher",
            "org.kde.StatusNotifierWatcher",
        )
        .await
        .ok()?;
        Some(
            watcher
                .get_property::<bool>("IsStatusNotifierHostRegistered")
                .await
                .unwrap_or(false),
        )
    };
    tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1), check)
        .await
        .ok()
        .flatten()
        .unwrap_or(false)
}

#[cfg(all(feature = "desktop", target_os = "linux"))]
fn start_workers(app: &AppHandle, state: &Arc<AppState>) {
    let (audio_sender, mut audio_receiver) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel();
    let (device_sender, mut device_receiver) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel();
    *state
        .monitor_task
        .lock()
        .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner()) =
        Some(tauri::async_runtime::spawn(audio::monitor(audio_sender)));
    state.track(tauri::async_runtime::spawn(devices::watch_changes(
        device_sender,
    )));
    let worker_app = app.clone();
    let worker_state = state.clone();
    state.track(tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
        let profiles = worker_state.profiles.lock().await.load();
        worker_state.publish(&worker_app, |snapshot| match profiles {
            Ok(profiles) => snapshot.profiles = profiles,
            Err(error) => snapshot.diagnostics.push(diagnostic_from_error("profiles-load", ProviderKind::App, &error)),
        }).await;
        worker_state.refresh_audio(&worker_app).await;
        worker_state.refresh_hardware(&worker_app, true).await;
        let mut battery_tick = tokio::time::interval(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30));
        battery_tick.set_missed_tick_behavior(tokio::time::MissedTickBehavior::Skip);
        battery_tick.tick().await;
        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                Some(event) = audio_receiver.recv() => worker_state.handle_audio_event(&worker_app, event).await,
                Some(()) = device_receiver.recv() => {
                    tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(150)).await;
                    while device_receiver.try_recv().is_ok() {}
                    worker_state.refresh_hardware(&worker_app, false).await;
                }
                _ = battery_tick.tick() => { worker_state.refresh_hardware(&worker_app, true).await; }
            }
        }
    }));
    let signal_app = app.clone();
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
        use tokio::signal::unix::{signal, SignalKind};
        let Ok(mut term) = signal(SignalKind::terminate()) else {
            return;
        };
        let Ok(mut interrupt) = signal(SignalKind::interrupt()) else {
            return;
        };
        tokio::select! { _ = term.recv() => {}, _ = interrupt.recv() => {} }
        request_exit(signal_app);
    });
}

#[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
pub fn run() {
    let builder = tauri::Builder::default()
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_single_instance::init(|app, _args, _cwd| show_window(app)))
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_dialog::init())
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            commands::get_snapshot, commands::refresh_devices, commands::set_dpi,
            commands::set_polling_rate, commands::set_default_output, commands::set_volume,
            commands::set_mute, commands::set_surround, commands::import_hrtf,
            commands::save_audio_profile, commands::apply_audio_profile, commands::exit_app,
        ])
        .on_menu_event(menu_event)
        .setup(|app| {
            let state = Arc::new(AppState::new()?);
            app.manage(state.clone());
            let open = MenuItem::with_id(app, "open", "Apri", true, None::<&str>)?;
            let off = MenuItem::with_id(app, "surround-off", "Disattiva 7.1", cfg!(target_os = "linux"), None::<&str>)?;
            let separator = PredefinedMenuItem::separator(app)?;
            let exit = MenuItem::with_id(app, "exit", "Esci", true, None::<&str>)?;
            let menu = Menu::with_items(app, &[&open, &off, &separator, &exit])?;
            app.set_menu(menu.clone())?;
            if let Some(icon) = app.default_window_icon().cloned() {
                let tray = TrayIconBuilder::with_id("peripheral-hub")
                    .icon(icon).tooltip("Peripheral Hub").menu(&menu).show_menu_on_left_click(false)
                    .on_menu_event(menu_event)
                    .on_tray_icon_event(|tray, event| {
                        if matches!(event, TrayIconEvent::Click { button: MouseButton::Left, button_state: MouseButtonState::Up, .. }) {
                            show_window(tray.app_handle());
                        }
                    }).build(app);
                state.tray_created.store(tray.is_ok(), Ordering::SeqCst);
            }
            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
            start_workers(app.handle(), &state);
            Ok(())
        })
        .on_window_event(|window, event| {
            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
            if let tauri::WindowEvent::CloseRequested { api, .. } = event {
                api.prevent_close();
                let window = window.clone();
                let app = window.app_handle().clone();
                let state = app.state::<Arc<AppState>>().inner().clone();
                tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
                    if state.tray_created.load(Ordering::SeqCst) && tray_host_available().await {
                        let _ = window.hide();
                    } else {
                        let _ = window.show();
                        let _ = window.set_focus();
                        state.publish(&app, |snapshot| {
                            snapshot.diagnostics.retain(|diagnostic| diagnostic.id != "tray-unavailable");
                            snapshot.diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new("tray-unavailable", ProviderKind::App, AvailabilityCode::Unsupported, Severity::Warning,
                                "Tray non utilizzabile: la finestra resta aperta. Usa Esci per ripristinare l'audio e chiudere."));
                        }).await;
                    }
                });
            }
            #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
            let _ = (window, event);
        });
    let app = builder
        .build(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("Impossibile avviare Peripheral Hub");
    app.run(|app, event| {
        if let tauri::RunEvent::ExitRequested { api, .. } = event {
            let state = app.state::<Arc<AppState>>();
            if !state.exit_ready.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
                api.prevent_exit();
                request_exit(app.clone());
            }
        }
    });
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

pub type AppResult<T> = Result<T, AppError>;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum ErrorCode {
    UnsupportedPlatform,
    Validation,
    DeviceNotFound,
    ProviderMissing,
    PermissionDenied,
    Unsupported,
    Busy,
    Timeout,
    CommandFailed,
    NotConfirmed,
    InvalidData,
    Io,
    AudioUnavailable,
    HrtfUnavailable,
    TargetUnavailable,
    Cancelled,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("{message}")]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct AppError {
    pub code: ErrorCode,
    pub message: String,
    pub detail: Option<String>,
}

impl AppError {
    pub fn new(code: ErrorCode, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            code,
            message: message.into(),
            detail: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn detail(mut self, detail: impl AsRef<str>) -> Self {
        self.detail = Some(
            detail
                .as_ref()
                .chars()
                .filter(|c| !c.is_control() || *c == '\n')
                .take(2048)
                .collect(),
        );
        self
    }

    pub fn unsupported_platform() -> Self {
        Self::new(
            ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform,
            "Il backend hardware e audio richiede Linux. La demo si avvia solo con npm run demo.",
        )
    }
}

impl From<std::io::Error> for AppError {
    fn from(error: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::new(ErrorCode::Io, "Operazione su file o processo non riuscita.")
            .detail(error.to_string())
    }
}

    UnsupportedPlatform,

    Available,
    Absent,
    Unsupported,
    ProviderMissing,

    Info,

    Error,

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Diagnostic {
    pub id: String,
    pub provider: ProviderKind,
    pub code: AvailabilityCode,
    pub severity: Severity,
    pub message: String,
    pub detail: Option<String>,
}

impl Diagnostic {
    pub fn new(
        id: impl Into<String>,
        provider: ProviderKind,
        code: AvailabilityCode,
        severity: Severity,
        message: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: id.into(),
            provider,
            code,
            severity,
            message: message.into(),
            detail: None,
        }
    }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct DeviceIdentity {
    pub serial: Option<String>,
    pub physical_path: Option<String>,
    pub vendor_id: Option<u16>,
    pub product_id: Option<u16>,
    pub transport: Option<String>,
    pub firmware: Option<String>,
    pub backend_path: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum DpiValues {
    Range { min: u32, max: u32, step: u32 },
    Discrete { values: Vec<u32> },
    Unknown,
}

impl DpiValues {
    pub fn contains(&self, value: u32) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Range { min, max, step } => {
                *step > 0 && value >= *min && value <= *max && (value - min) % step == 0
            }
            Self::Discrete { values } => values.contains(&value),
            Self::Unknown => false,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct DpiCapability {
    pub values: DpiValues,
    pub writable: bool,
    pub separate_axes: bool,
    pub reason: Option<String>,
}

    pub reason: Option<String>,
}

}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
