=== FILE: C:\Users\shady\Desktop\github\sycomopse\src-tauri\src\process.rs ===
use crate::models::{AppError, AppResult, ErrorCode};
#[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", test))]
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::{AsyncRead, AsyncReadExt};
use tokio::process::{Child, Command};

const STDOUT_LIMIT: u64 = 16 * 1024 * 1024;
const STDERR_LIMIT: u64 = 64 * 1024;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct ProcessOutput {
    pub stdout: Vec<u8>,
    pub stderr: String,
}

fn allowed(executable: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(
        executable,
        "pw-dump" | "wpctl" | "pipewire" | "headsetcontrol" | "ratbagd"
    )
}

pub fn owned_command(executable: &str) -> AppResult<Command> {
    if !allowed(executable) {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Validation,
            "Eseguibile non consentito.",
        ));
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    {
        Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        let mut command = Command::new(executable);
        command
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped())
            .kill_on_drop(true);
        let parent = std::process::id() as libc::pid_t;
        // The child must not outlive this app, including a crash between fork and exec.
        unsafe {
            command.pre_exec(move || {
                if libc::prctl(libc::PR_SET_PDEATHSIG, libc::SIGTERM) == -1 {
                    return Err(std::io::Error::last_os_error());
                }
                if libc::getppid() != parent {
                    return Err(std::io::Error::new(
                        std::io::ErrorKind::Interrupted,
                        "Parent exited before exec",
                    ));
                }
                Ok(())
            });
        }
        Ok(command)
    }
}

async fn read_bounded(reader: impl AsyncRead + Unpin, limit: u64) -> AppResult<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    reader.take(limit + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes).await?;
    if bytes.len() as u64 > limit {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::InvalidData,
            "Output del processo troppo grande.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

async fn capture(mut child: Child, timeout: Duration) -> AppResult<ProcessOutput> {
    let stdout = child
        .stdout
        .take()
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Io, "stdout non disponibile."))?;
    let stderr = child
        .stderr
        .take()
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Io, "stderr non disponibile."))?;
    let mut stdout_task = tokio::spawn(read_bounded(stdout, STDOUT_LIMIT));
    let mut stderr_task = tokio::spawn(read_bounded(stderr, STDERR_LIMIT));
    let result = tokio::time::timeout(timeout, async {
        let status = child.wait().await?;
        let stdout = (&mut stdout_task).await.map_err(|error| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::Io, "Lettura stdout interrotta.").detail(error.to_string())
        })??;
        let stderr = (&mut stderr_task).await.map_err(|error| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::Io, "Lettura stderr interrotta.").detail(error.to_string())
        })??;
        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&stderr).into_owned();
        if !status.success() {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::CommandFailed,
                "Il comando del sistema non e' riuscito.",
            )
            .detail(format!("exit={status}; {stderr}")));
        }
        Ok(ProcessOutput { stdout, stderr })
    })
    .await;
    match result {
        Ok(Ok(output)) => Ok(output),
        other => {
            let _ = child.kill().await;
            let _ = child.wait().await;
            stdout_task.abort();
            stderr_task.abort();
            match other {
                Ok(Err(error)) => Err(error),
                Err(_) => Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Timeout,
                    "Il comando del sistema ha superato il tempo massimo.",
                )),
                Ok(Ok(_)) => unreachable!(),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub async fn run(executable: &str, args: &[&str], timeout: Duration) -> AppResult<ProcessOutput> {
    let mut command = owned_command(executable)?;
    command.args(args);
    let child = command.spawn().map_err(|error| {
        let code = match error.kind() {
            std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => ErrorCode::ProviderMissing,
            std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => ErrorCode::PermissionDenied,
            _ => ErrorCode::Io,
        };
        AppError::new(code, format!("Impossibile eseguire {executable}.")).detail(error.to_string())
    })?;
    capture(child, timeout).await
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn rejects_shells_and_arbitrary_executables() {
        assert!(!allowed("sh"));
        assert!(!allowed("sudo"));
        assert!(!allowed("wpctl --help"));
        assert!(allowed("wpctl"));
        assert_eq!(
            owned_command("powershell").unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::Validation
        );
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn bounded_reader_rejects_excess_data() {
        assert_eq!(
            read_bounded(&b"12345"[..], 4).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::InvalidData
        );
        assert_eq!(read_bounded(&b"1234"[..], 4).await.unwrap(), b"1234");
    }

    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn no_cli_execution_on_unsupported_platform() {
        let error = run("wpctl", &["set-volume", "999", "1"], Duration::from_secs(1))
            .await
            .unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform);
    }

    #[test]
    #[ignore = "child fixture invoked only by the timeout test"]
    fn timeout_child() {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(30));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn timeout_kills_and_reaps_owned_child() {
        let mut command = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap());
        command
            .args(["--ignored", "--exact", "process::tests::timeout_child"])
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped())
            .kill_on_drop(true);
        let error = capture(command.spawn().unwrap(), Duration::from_millis(80))
            .await
            .unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, ErrorCode::Timeout);
    }
}


=== FILE: C:\Users\shady\Desktop\github\sycomopse\src-tauri\src\profiles.rs ===
use crate::models::{AppError, AppResult, AudioProfile, ErrorCode};
use directories::BaseDirs;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashSet;
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::fs::File;
use std::fs::{self, OpenOptions};
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use uuid::Uuid;

const MAX_PROFILE_BYTES: usize = 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_PROFILES: usize = 128;
pub(crate) const SYSTEM_HRTF: &str = "system";
pub(crate) const APP_NODE_PREFIX: &str = "peripheral-hub.surround.";

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct AppPaths {
    pub config_dir: PathBuf,
    pub data_dir: PathBuf,
    pub state_dir: PathBuf,
    pub runtime_dir: PathBuf,
}

impl AppPaths {
    fn discover() -> AppResult<Self> {
        let base = BaseDirs::new()
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Io, "Cartella personale non disponibile."))?;
        let xdg = |key: &str, fallback: PathBuf| -> AppResult<PathBuf> {
            match std::env::var_os(key) {
                Some(value) if !value.is_empty() => {
                    let path = PathBuf::from(value);
                    if !path.is_absolute() {
                        return Err(AppError::new(
                            ErrorCode::Validation,
                            format!("{key} deve essere un percorso assoluto."),
                        ));
                    }
                    Ok(path)
                }
                _ => Ok(fallback),
            }
        };
        let config_dir =
            xdg("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", base.home_dir().join(".config"))?.join("peripheral-hub");
        let data_dir =
            xdg("XDG_DATA_HOME", base.home_dir().join(".local/share"))?.join("peripheral-hub");
        let state_dir =
            xdg("XDG_STATE_HOME", base.home_dir().join(".local/state"))?.join("peripheral-hub");
        let runtime_dir = match std::env::var_os("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR") {
            Some(value) if !value.is_empty() => {
                let root = PathBuf::from(value);
                if !root.is_absolute() {
                    return Err(AppError::new(
                        ErrorCode::Validation,
                        "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR deve essere un percorso assoluto.",
                    ));
                }
                root.join("peripheral-hub")
            }
            _ => state_dir.join("runtime"),
        };
        Ok(Self {
            config_dir,
            data_dir,
            state_dir,
            runtime_dir,
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", deny_unknown_fields)]
struct ProfileDocument {
    schema_version: u32,
    profiles: Vec<AudioProfile>,
}

pub struct ProfileStore {
    paths: AppPaths,
    blocked: Option<AppError>,
}

impl ProfileStore {
    pub fn new() -> AppResult<Self> {
        Ok(Self::at(AppPaths::discover()?))
    }

    pub fn at(paths: AppPaths) -> Self {
        Self {
            paths,
            blocked: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn paths(&self) -> &AppPaths {
        &self.paths
    }

    pub fn load(&mut self) -> AppResult<Vec<AudioProfile>> {
        let path = self.paths.config_dir.join("profiles.json");
        let result = match read_limited(&path, MAX_PROFILE_BYTES) {
            Ok(bytes) => parse_profiles(&bytes),
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
                // A failed load must not silently turn into an empty writable store.
                if let Some(error) = &self.blocked {
                    return Err(error.clone());
                }
                return Ok(Vec::new());
            }
            Err(error) => Err(AppError::from(error)),
        };
        match result {
            Ok(profiles) => {
                self.blocked = None;
                Ok(profiles)
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.blocked = Some(error.clone());
                Err(error)
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn save(&mut self, profile: AudioProfile) -> AppResult<Vec<AudioProfile>> {
        validate_profile(&profile)?;
        // Reload before every write, including the first write, to preserve foreign data.
        let mut profiles = self.load()?;
        match profiles.iter().position(|saved| saved.id == profile.id) {
            Some(index) => profiles[index] = profile,
            None if profiles.len() < MAX_PROFILES => profiles.push(profile),
            None => {
                return Err(AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::Validation,
                    "Numero massimo di profili raggiunto (128).",
                ));
            }
        }
        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&ProfileDocument {
            schema_version: 1,
            profiles: profiles.clone(),
        })
        .map_err(|error| invalid_profiles().detail(error.to_string()))?;
        if bytes.len() > MAX_PROFILE_BYTES {
            return Err(invalid_profiles());
        }
        atomic_write(&self.paths.config_dir.join("profiles.json"), &bytes)?;
        Ok(profiles)
    }
}

fn invalid_profiles() -> AppError {
    AppError::new(
        ErrorCode::InvalidData,
        "Profili non validi: il file originale viene conservato e non sovrascritto.",
    )
}

fn parse_profiles(bytes: &[u8]) -> AppResult<Vec<AudioProfile>> {
    if bytes.len() > MAX_PROFILE_BYTES {
        return Err(invalid_profiles());
    }
    let document: ProfileDocument = serde_json::from_slice(bytes)
        .map_err(|error| invalid_profiles().detail(error.to_string()))?;
    if document.schema_version != 1 {
        return Err(invalid_profiles().detail(format!(
            "schemaVersion {} non supportata; nessuna migrazione automatica.",
            document.schema_version
        )));
    }
    if document.profiles.len() > MAX_PROFILES {
        return Err(invalid_profiles());
    }
    let mut ids = HashSet::new();
    for profile in &document.profiles {
        validate_profile(profile).map_err(|error| invalid_profiles().detail(error.message))?;
        if !ids.insert(&profile.id) {
            return Err(invalid_profiles().detail("UUID duplicato."));
        }
    }
    Ok(document.profiles)
}

pub(crate) fn validate_node_name(name: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
    if name.is_empty() || name.len() > 512 || name.chars().any(char::is_control) {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Validation,
            "Nome del nodo audio non valido.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn validate_hrtf_reference(reference: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
    if reference == SYSTEM_HRTF {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let valid = reference
        .strip_suffix(".sofa")
        .is_some_and(|id| Uuid::parse_str(id).is_ok_and(|uuid| uuid.to_string() == id));
    if !valid {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Validation,
            "Riferimento HRTF non valido: sono ammessi solo system o UUID.sofa importati.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn validate_profile(profile: &AudioProfile) -> AppResult<()> {
    let valid_id = Uuid::parse_str(&profile.id).is_ok_and(|uuid| uuid.to_string() == profile.id);
    if profile.schema_version != 1
        || !valid_id
        || profile.name.is_empty()
        || profile.name.len() > 120
        || profile.name.trim() != profile.name
        || profile.name.chars().any(char::is_control)
        || !profile.volume.is_finite()
        || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&profile.volume)
        || profile.output_node_name.starts_with(APP_NODE_PREFIX)
    {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Validation,
            "Profilo acustico non valido.",
        ));
    }
    validate_node_name(&profile.output_node_name)?;
    if let Some(reference) = &profile.hrtf {
        validate_hrtf_reference(reference)?;
    } else if profile.surround_requested {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Validation,
            "Un profilo 7.1 deve indicare un HRTF disponibile.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn read_limited(path: &Path, maximum: usize) -> std::io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    if !fs::metadata(path)?.is_file() {
        return Err(std::io::Error::new(
            std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "Il percorso non e' un file regolare.",
        ));
    }
    let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
    options.read(true);
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        options.custom_flags(libc::O_NONBLOCK);
    }
    let file = options.open(path)?;
    let metadata = file.metadata()?;
    if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.len() > maximum as u64 {
        return Err(std::io::Error::new(
            std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "File non regolare o troppo grande.",
        ));
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity((metadata.len() as usize).min(maximum));
    file.take(maximum as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
    if bytes.len() > maximum {
        return Err(std::io::Error::new(
            std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "File troppo grande.",
        ));
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

pub(crate) fn private_dir(path: &Path) -> AppResult<()> {
    if !path.is_absolute() {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Validation,
            "Cartella privata non assoluta.",
        ));
    }
    let mut builder = fs::DirBuilder::new();
    builder.recursive(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::DirBuilderExt;
        builder.mode(0o700);
    }
    builder.create(path)?;
    let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(path)?;
    if !metadata.is_dir() || metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::PermissionDenied,
            "La cartella privata non deve essere un collegamento simbolico.",
        ));
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::{MetadataExt, PermissionsExt};
        // Only app-owned subdirectories are passed here, never an XDG root.
        if metadata.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() } {
            return Err(AppError::new(
                ErrorCode::PermissionDenied,
                "Cartella di un altro utente.",
            ));
        }
        if metadata.mode() & 0o077 != 0 {
            fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn write_new_private(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> AppResult<()> {
    let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
    options.write(true).create_new(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        options.mode(0o600);
    }
    let mut file = options.open(path)?;
    file.write_all(bytes)?;
    file.sync_all()?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn atomic_write(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> AppResult<()> {
    let parent = path
        .parent()
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::new(ErrorCode::Validation, "Destinazione del file non valida."))?;
    private_dir(parent)?;
    if fs::symlink_metadata(path).is_ok_and(|metadata| metadata.file_type().is_symlink()) {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::PermissionDenied,
            "Il file non deve essere un link.",
        ));
    }
    let temporary = parent.join(format!(".{}.tmp", Uuid::new_v4()));
    let result = (|| {
        write_new_private(&temporary, bytes)?;
        fs::rename(&temporary, path)?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        File::open(parent)?.sync_all()?;
        Ok(())
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&temporary);
    }
    result
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn profile() -> AudioProfile {
        AudioProfile {
            schema_version: 1,
            id: "1f8bfe67-6807-4be7-a4e3-54c1d3bbf002".into(),
            name: "Cuffie".into(),
            output_node_name: "alsa_output.usb-headset.stereo".into(),
            hrtf: Some(SYSTEM_HRTF.into()),
            volume: 0.4,
            mute: false,
            surround_requested: true,
        }
    }

    fn paths(root: &Path) -> AppPaths {
        AppPaths {
            config_dir: root.join("config"),
            data_dir: root.join("data"),
            state_dir: root.join("state"),
            runtime_dir: root.join("runtime"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn validates_schema_volume_and_controlled_references() {
        let mut value = profile();
        assert!(validate_profile(&value).is_ok());
        for reference in ["../hrtf.sofa", "/tmp/arbitrary.sofa", "test.conf", "SYSTEM"] {
            value.hrtf = Some(reference.into());
            assert!(validate_profile(&value).is_err());
        }
        value.hrtf = Some(format!("{}.sofa", Uuid::new_v4()));
        assert!(validate_profile(&value).is_ok());
        for volume in [f64::NAN, f64::INFINITY, -0.1, 1.001] {
            value.volume = volume;
            assert!(validate_profile(&value).is_err());
        }
        value.volume = 0.5;
        value.schema_version = 2;
        assert!(validate_profile(&value).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn initial_load_is_read_only_and_save_is_atomic() {
        let directory = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let app_paths = paths(directory.path());
        let mut store = ProfileStore::at(app_paths.clone());
        assert!(store.load().unwrap().is_empty());
        assert!(!app_paths.config_dir.exists());
        assert_eq!(store.save(profile()).unwrap().len(), 1);
        let mut changed = profile();
        changed.volume = 0.2;
        assert_eq!(store.save(changed.clone()).unwrap(), vec![changed.clone()]);
        assert_eq!(store.load().unwrap(), vec![changed]);
        assert_eq!(fs::read_dir(&app_paths.config_dir).unwrap().count(), 1);
        assert!(!app_paths.state_dir.exists());
    }

    #[test]
    fn corrupt_and_unknown_documents_are_not_overwritten() {
        for bytes in [
            include_bytes!("../tests/profiles_corrupt.json").as_slice(),
            include_bytes!("../tests/profiles_unknown_version.json").as_slice(),
            b"{\"schemaVersion\":1,\"profiles\":[],\"foreign\":true}".as_slice(),
        ] {
            let directory = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
            let app_paths = paths(directory.path());
            fs::create_dir(&app_paths.config_dir).unwrap();
            let file = app_paths.config_dir.join("profiles.json");
            fs::write(&file, bytes).unwrap();
            let mut store = ProfileStore::at(app_paths);
            assert!(store.load().is_err());
            assert!(store.save(profile()).is_err());
            assert_eq!(fs::read(file).unwrap(), bytes);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn duplicate_ids_and_unknown_profile_fields_are_rejected() {
        let value = profile();
        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&ProfileDocument {
            schema_version: 1,
            profiles: vec![value.clone(), value],
        })
        .unwrap();
        assert!(parse_profiles(&bytes).is_err());
        let mut json = serde_json::to_value(profile()).unwrap();
        json["runtimeId"] = serde_json::json!(42);
        assert!(serde_json::from_value::<AudioProfile>(json).is_err());
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    #[test]
    fn config_state_and_runtime_are_private_and_symlinks_rejected() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::{symlink, PermissionsExt};
        let directory = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let app_paths = paths(directory.path());
        for path in [
            &app_paths.config_dir,
            &app_paths.state_dir,
            &app_paths.runtime_dir,
        ] {
            private_dir(path).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(
                fs::metadata(path).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777,
                0o700
            );
        }
        let file = app_paths.config_dir.join("profiles.json");
        symlink(directory.path().join("foreign"), &file).unwrap();
        assert!(atomic_write(&file, b"{}").is_err());
    }
}

#![cfg_attr(not(target_os = "linux"), allow(dead_code))]

use crate::models::{AppError, AppResult, ErrorCode, HrtfStatus};
use crate::profiles::{
    atomic_write, private_dir, read_limited, validate_hrtf_reference, validate_node_name,
    write_new_private, AppPaths, APP_NODE_PREFIX, SYSTEM_HRTF,
};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use uuid::Uuid;

pub(crate) const MAX_SOFA_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
const RESTORE_FILE: &str = "audio-restore.json";
const SYSTEM_SOFA_PATH: &str = "/usr/share/libmysofa/default.sofa";

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub(crate) struct HrtfSelection {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub status: HrtfStatus,
}

impl HrtfSelection {
    pub fn readable(&self) -> bool {
        if !fs::metadata(&self.path).is_ok_and(|metadata| metadata.is_file()) {
            return false;
        }
        let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
        options.read(true);
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
            options.custom_flags(libc::O_NONBLOCK);
        }
        options.open(&self.path).is_ok_and(|file| {
            file.metadata().is_ok_and(|metadata| {
                metadata.is_file() && (128..=MAX_SOFA_BYTES as u64).contains(&metadata.len())
            })
        })
    }
}

fn hrtf_error() -> AppError {
    AppError::new(
        ErrorCode::HrtfUnavailable,
        "HRTF SOFA non disponibile o non valido. Installare libmysofa o importare un file .sofa valido.",
    )
}

fn validate_sofa_container(bytes: &[u8]) -> AppResult<()> {
    let signature = b"\x89HDF\r\n\x1a\n";
    let valid_signature = bytes.starts_with(signature)
        || (9..=20).any(|power| {
            bytes
                .get((1 << power)..)
                .is_some_and(|data| data.starts_with(signature))
        });
    // This is an envelope check, not a substitute for libmysofa's semantic check.
    if bytes.len() < 128
        || bytes.len() > MAX_SOFA_BYTES
        || !valid_signature
        || !bytes
            .windows(b"SOFAConventions".len())
            .any(|word| word == b"SOFAConventions")
        || !bytes.windows(b"SOFA".len()).any(|word| word == b"SOFA")
    {
        return Err(
            hrtf_error().detail("Firma HDF5/SOFA mancante o file troppo grande (massimo 64 MiB).")
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn validate_native_sofa(path: &Path) -> AppResult<()> {
    use std::ffi::CString;
    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;

    struct Library(*mut libc::c_void);
    impl Drop for Library {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            unsafe { libc::dlclose(self.0) };
        }
    }

    let filename = CString::new(path.as_os_str().as_bytes()).map_err(|_| hrtf_error())?;
    // Use the system dependency already required by PipeWire's SOFA plugin.
    let handle = unsafe {
        let first = libc::dlopen(
            c"libmysofa.so.1".as_ptr(),
            libc::RTLD_NOW | libc::RTLD_LOCAL,
        );
        if first.is_null() {
            libc::dlopen(c"libmysofa.so".as_ptr(), libc::RTLD_NOW | libc::RTLD_LOCAL)
        } else {
            first
        }
    };
    if handle.is_null() {
        return Err(hrtf_error().detail("La libreria di sistema libmysofa non e' installata."));
    }
    let library = Library(handle);
    type Load = unsafe extern "C" fn(*const libc::c_char, *mut libc::c_int) -> *mut libc::c_void;
    type Check = unsafe extern "C" fn(*mut libc::c_void) -> libc::c_int;
    type Free = unsafe extern "C" fn(*mut libc::c_void);
    let (load, check, free) = unsafe {
        (
            libc::dlsym(library.0, c"mysofa_load".as_ptr()),
            libc::dlsym(library.0, c"mysofa_check".as_ptr()),
            libc::dlsym(library.0, c"mysofa_free".as_ptr()),
        )
    };
    if load.is_null() || check.is_null() || free.is_null() {
        return Err(hrtf_error().detail("API libmysofa non disponibile."));
    }
    let load: Load = unsafe { std::mem::transmute(load) };
    let check: Check = unsafe { std::mem::transmute(check) };
    let free: Free = unsafe { std::mem::transmute(free) };
    let mut error = 0;
    let hrtf = unsafe { load(filename.as_ptr(), &mut error) };
    if hrtf.is_null() {
        return Err(hrtf_error().detail(format!("mysofa_load: {error}")));
    }
    let validation = unsafe { check(hrtf) };
    unsafe { free(hrtf) };
    if validation != 0 {
        return Err(hrtf_error().detail(format!("mysofa_check: {validation}")));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
fn validate_native_sofa(_path: &Path) -> AppResult<()> {
    Err(AppError::unsupported_platform())
}

pub(crate) fn resolve_hrtf(paths: &AppPaths, reference: &str) -> AppResult<HrtfSelection> {
    validate_hrtf_reference(reference)?;
    let path = if reference == SYSTEM_HRTF {
        PathBuf::from(SYSTEM_SOFA_PATH)
    } else {
        let root = paths.data_dir.join("hrtf");
        let path = root.join(reference);
        let metadata =
            fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|error| hrtf_error().detail(error.to_string()))?;
        if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
            return Err(hrtf_error().detail("Il riferimento importato non deve essere un link."));
        }
        let canonical_root =
            fs::canonicalize(root).map_err(|error| hrtf_error().detail(error.to_string()))?;
        let canonical_path =
            fs::canonicalize(&path).map_err(|error| hrtf_error().detail(error.to_string()))?;
        if canonical_path.parent() != Some(canonical_root.as_path()) {
            return Err(
                hrtf_error().detail("Il file HRTF deve restare nella cartella dati dell'app.")
            );
        }
        canonical_path
    };
    let bytes = read_limited(&path, MAX_SOFA_BYTES)
        .map_err(|error| hrtf_error().detail(error.to_string()))?;
    validate_sofa_container(&bytes)?;
    validate_native_sofa(&path)?;
    Ok(HrtfSelection {
        path,
        status: HrtfStatus {
            reference: reference.into(),
            display_name: if reference == SYSTEM_HRTF {
                "HRTF di sistema".into()
            } else {
                reference.into()
            },
            readable: true,
        },
    })
}

pub(crate) fn import_hrtf(paths: &AppPaths, source: &Path) -> AppResult<HrtfSelection> {
    if !source.is_absolute()
        || !source
            .extension()
            .and_then(|value| value.to_str())
            .is_some_and(|extension| extension.eq_ignore_ascii_case("sofa"))
    {
        return Err(hrtf_error()
            .detail("Selezionare un file .sofa dal dialogo, non una configurazione PipeWire."));
    }
    let bytes = read_limited(source, MAX_SOFA_BYTES)
        .map_err(|error| hrtf_error().detail(error.to_string()))?;
    validate_sofa_container(&bytes)?;
    let root = paths.data_dir.join("hrtf");
    private_dir(&root)?;
    let reference = format!("{}.sofa", Uuid::new_v4());
    let destination = root.join(&reference);
    let result = (|| {
        write_new_private(&destination, &bytes)?;
        // Validate the controlled copy, so a changed source cannot race the import.
        let mut selection = resolve_hrtf(paths, &reference)?;
        selection.status.display_name = source
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|name| name.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("HRTF importato")
            .chars()
            .filter(|character| !character.is_control())
            .take(120)
            .collect();
        Ok(selection)
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(destination);
    }
    result
}

pub(crate) fn render_config(
    target: &str,
    hrtf_path: &Path,
    input_name: &str,
    playback_name: &str,
    session: &str,
) -> AppResult<String> {
    validate_node_name(target)?;
    validate_node_name(input_name)?;
    validate_node_name(playback_name)?;
    let path = hrtf_path.to_str().ok_or_else(hrtf_error)?;
    let encoded = |value: &str| {
        serde_json::to_string(value).map_err(|error| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::InvalidData, "Configurazione non valida.")
                .detail(error.to_string())
        })
    };
    let values = [
        ("TARGET_OBJECT", encoded(target)?),
        ("SOFA_PATH", encoded(path)?),
        ("INPUT_NAME", encoded(input_name)?),
        ("PLAYBACK_NAME", encoded(playback_name)?),
        ("SESSION", encoded(session)?),
    ];
    let template = include_str!("../resources/spatializer-7.1.conf.template");
    let mut sections = template.split("{{");
    let mut config = sections.next().unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
    // Single-pass expansion: inserted user data cannot become another placeholder.
    for section in sections {
        let (key, remainder) = section.split_once("}}").ok_or_else(|| {
            AppError::new(ErrorCode::InvalidData, "Template surround incompleto.")
        })?;
        let value = values
            .iter()
            .find(|(name, _)| *name == key)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::InvalidData,
                    "Campo del template surround sconosciuto.",
                )
            })?;
        config.push_str(&value.1);
        config.push_str(remainder);
    }
    Ok(config)
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub(crate) struct RestoreRecord {
    pub schema_version: u32,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub previous_configured_default: Option<String>,
    pub previous_effective_default: Option<String>,
    pub selected_physical_target: String,
    pub target_serial: u64,
    pub virtual_node_name: String,
    pub playback_node_name: String,
    pub app_process_id: u32,
    pub filter_process_id: Option<u32>,
    pub virtual_object_serial: Option<u64>,
    pub playback_object_serial: Option<u64>,
    pub core_cookie: Option<u64>,
}

impl RestoreRecord {
    pub fn validate(&self) -> AppResult<()> {
        let uuid = Uuid::parse_str(&self.session_id).map_err(|_| invalid_restore())?;
        if self.schema_version != 1
            || uuid.to_string() != self.session_id
            || self.virtual_node_name != format!("{APP_NODE_PREFIX}input.{}", self.session_id)
            || self.playback_node_name != format!("{APP_NODE_PREFIX}playback.{}", self.session_id)
            || self.selected_physical_target.starts_with(APP_NODE_PREFIX)
            || self.app_process_id == 0
        {
            return Err(invalid_restore());
        }
        validate_node_name(&self.selected_physical_target)?;
        for name in [
            &self.previous_configured_default,
            &self.previous_effective_default,
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        {
            validate_node_name(name)?;
            if name.starts_with(APP_NODE_PREFIX) {
                return Err(invalid_restore());
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn invalid_restore() -> AppError {
    AppError::new(
        ErrorCode::InvalidData,
        "Stato di ripristino non valido: conservato, attivazione surround bloccata.",
    )
}

pub(crate) fn read_restore(paths: &AppPaths) -> AppResult<Option<RestoreRecord>> {
    let bytes = match read_limited(&paths.state_dir.join(RESTORE_FILE), 16 * 1024) {
        Ok(bytes) => bytes,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(error) => return Err(invalid_restore().detail(error.to_string())),
    };
    let record: RestoreRecord = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes)
        .map_err(|error| invalid_restore().detail(error.to_string()))?;
    record.validate()?;
    Ok(Some(record))
}

pub(crate) fn write_restore(paths: &AppPaths, record: &RestoreRecord) -> AppResult<()> {
    record.validate()?;
    // Never replace a corrupt/foreign-version recovery record.
    if read_restore(paths)?.is_some_and(|existing| existing.session_id != record.session_id) {
        return Err(AppError::new(
            ErrorCode::Busy,
            "Una sessione surround precedente deve essere recuperata prima dell'attivazione.",
        ));
    }
    let bytes =
        serde_json::to_vec(record).map_err(|error| invalid_restore().detail(error.to_string()))?;
    atomic_write(&paths.state_dir.join(RESTORE_FILE), &bytes)
}

pub(crate) fn clear_restore(paths: &AppPaths, session: &str) -> AppResult<()> {
    if read_restore(paths)?.is_some_and(|record| record.session_id == session) {
        fs::remove_file(paths.state_dir.join(RESTORE_FILE))?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        fs::File::open(&paths.state_dir)?.sync_all()?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum RestoreIntent {
    Bypass,
    Rollback,
    TargetLost,
    Recovery,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum RestoreAction {
    KeepExternal,
    SetOutput(String),
    ClearOutputDefault,
}

pub(crate) fn restore_decision(
    record: &RestoreRecord,
    configured: Option<&str>,
    target_present: bool,
    previous_present: bool,
    safe_physical_fallback: Option<&str>,
    intent: RestoreIntent,
) -> RestoreAction {
    if configured != Some(record.virtual_node_name.as_str()) {
        return RestoreAction::KeepExternal;
    }
    if intent == RestoreIntent::Rollback {
        return match &record.previous_configured_default {
            Some(name) if previous_present => RestoreAction::SetOutput(name.clone()),
            _ => RestoreAction::ClearOutputDefault,
        };
    }
    if record.previous_configured_default.is_none() && intent != RestoreIntent::TargetLost {
        return RestoreAction::ClearOutputDefault;
    }
    if intent == RestoreIntent::TargetLost || !target_present {
        return match safe_physical_fallback {
            Some(name) => RestoreAction::SetOutput(name.into()),
            None => RestoreAction::ClearOutputDefault,
        };
    }
    RestoreAction::SetOutput(record.selected_physical_target.clone())
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) mod runtime {
    use super::*;
    use crate::models::AudioNode;
    use crate::process;
    use std::collections::VecDeque;
    use std::process::Stdio;
    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
    use std::time::Duration;
    use tokio::io::AsyncReadExt;
    use tokio::process::{Child, ChildStdout, Command};
    use tokio::task::JoinHandle;

    const STDERR_BYTES: usize = 8192;

    pub(crate) struct OwnedChild {
        child: Option<Child>,
        stderr: Arc<Mutex<VecDeque<u8>>>,
        stderr_reader: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
    }

    impl OwnedChild {
        pub fn spawn(mut command: Command) -> AppResult<Self> {
            let mut child = command.spawn().map_err(|error| {
                let code = match error.kind() {
                    std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => ErrorCode::ProviderMissing,
                    std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => ErrorCode::PermissionDenied,
                    _ => ErrorCode::Io,
                };
                AppError::new(code, "Impossibile avviare il client PipeWire.")
                    .detail(error.to_string())
            })?;
            let stderr = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VecDeque::new()));
            let retained = stderr.clone();
            let stderr_reader = child.stderr.take().map(|mut reader| {
                tokio::spawn(async move {
                    let mut buffer = [0; 4096];
                    while let Ok(length) = reader.read(&mut buffer).await {
                        if length == 0 {
                            break;
                        }
                        if let Ok(mut tail) = retained.lock() {
                            for byte in &buffer[..length] {
                                if tail.len() == STDERR_BYTES {
                                    tail.pop_front();
                                }
                                tail.push_back(*byte);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                })
            });
            Ok(Self {
                child: Some(child),
                stderr,
                stderr_reader,
            })
        }

        pub fn id(&self) -> Option<u32> {
            self.child.as_ref().and_then(Child::id)
        }

        pub fn stdout(&mut self) -> Option<ChildStdout> {
            self.child.as_mut().and_then(|child| child.stdout.take())
        }

        pub fn has_exited(&mut self) -> AppResult<bool> {
            match self.child.as_mut() {
                Some(child) => Ok(child.try_wait()?.is_some()),
                None => Ok(true),
            }
        }

        pub fn stderr(&self) -> String {
            self.stderr
                .lock()
                .map(|tail| {
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&tail.iter().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>()).into_owned()
                })
                .unwrap_or_default()
        }

        pub async fn terminate(&mut self) -> AppResult<()> {
            let result = if let Some(mut child) = self.child.take() {
                let _ = child.start_kill();
                match tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(3), child.wait()).await {
                    Ok(result) => result.map(|_| ()).map_err(AppError::from),
                    Err(_) => {
                        tokio::spawn(async move {
                            let _ = child.wait().await;
                        });
                        Err(AppError::new(
                            ErrorCode::Timeout,
                            "Attesa di chiusura del client PipeWire scaduta.",
                        ))
                    }
                }
            } else {
                Ok(())
            };
            if let Some(task) = self.stderr_reader.take() {
                task.abort();
                let _ = task.await;
            }
            result
        }
    }

    impl Drop for OwnedChild {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            if let Some(task) = self.stderr_reader.take() {
                task.abort();
            }
            if let Some(mut child) = self.child.take() {
                let _ = child.start_kill();
                if let Ok(runtime) = tokio::runtime::Handle::try_current() {
                    runtime.spawn(async move {
                        let _ = child.wait().await;
                    });
                }
                // Without a live runtime, Tokio's kill_on_drop/reaper remains the backstop.
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) struct ActiveFilter {
        pub process: OwnedChild,
        pub record: RestoreRecord,
        pub directory: PathBuf,
        pub hrtf_reference: String,
    }

    impl ActiveFilter {
        pub async fn spawn(
            paths: &AppPaths,
            target: &AudioNode,
            hrtf: &HrtfSelection,
            previous_configured_default: Option<String>,
            previous_effective_default: Option<String>,
            core_cookie: Option<u64>,
        ) -> AppResult<Self> {
            let serial = target.object_serial.ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(
                    ErrorCode::TargetUnavailable,
                    "Il target non espone un'identita PipeWire stabile.",
                )
            })?;
            let session_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
            let mut record = RestoreRecord {
                schema_version: 1,
                virtual_node_name: format!("{APP_NODE_PREFIX}input.{session_id}"),
                playback_node_name: format!("{APP_NODE_PREFIX}playback.{session_id}"),
                session_id,
                previous_configured_default,
                previous_effective_default,
                selected_physical_target: target.node_name.clone(),
                target_serial: serial,
                app_process_id: std::process::id(),
                filter_process_id: None,
                virtual_object_serial: None,
                playback_object_serial: None,
                core_cookie,
            };
            private_dir(&paths.runtime_dir)?;
            let directory = paths.runtime_dir.join(&record.session_id);
            private_dir(&directory)?;
            let config_path = directory.join("spatializer.conf");
            let result = (|| {
                let config = render_config(
                    &target.node_name,
                    &hrtf.path,
                    &record.virtual_node_name,
                    &record.playback_node_name,
                    &record.session_id,
                )?;
                write_new_private(&config_path, config.as_bytes())?;
                write_restore(paths, &record)?;
                let mut command = process::owned_command("pipewire")?;
                command.arg("-c").arg(&config_path).stdout(Stdio::null());
                command
                    .env("PIPEWIRE_CONFIG_DIR", &directory)
                    .env("PIPEWIRE_CONFIG_PREFIX", "")
                    .env("PIPEWIRE_CONFIG_NAME", &config_path)
                    .env("PIPEWIRE_DAEMON", "false")
                    .env_remove("PIPEWIRE_NO_CONFIG")
                    .env_remove("PIPEWIRE_PROPS")
                    .env_remove("PIPEWIRE_NODE")
                    .env_remove("PIPEWIRE_AUTOCONNECT");
                let process = OwnedChild::spawn(command)?;
                record.filter_process_id = process.id();
                write_restore(paths, &record)?;
                Ok(Self {
                    process,
                    record: record.clone(),
                    directory: directory.clone(),
                    hrtf_reference: hrtf.status.reference.clone(),
                })
            })();
            if result.is_err() {
                let _ = fs::remove_file(config_path);
                let _ = fs::remove_dir(&directory);
                let _ = clear_restore(paths, &record.session_id);
            }
            result
        }

        pub async fn stop(&mut self) -> AppResult<()> {
            let result = self.process.terminate().await;
            let _ = fs::remove_file(self.directory.join("spatializer.conf"));
            let _ = fs::remove_dir(&self.directory);
            result
        }
    }

    impl Drop for ActiveFilter {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(self.directory.join("spatializer.conf"));
            let _ = fs::remove_dir(&self.directory);
            // Keep the independent restore record on abnormal drop/crash.
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::{json, Value};

    #[test]
    fn template_is_isolated_escaped_eight_to_two_and_conservative() {
        let target = "alsa_output.\"\\{{SESSION}}.usb";
        let path = Path::new("/data/quoted \"hrtf\"\\sample.sofa");
        let config = render_config(target, path, "input.test", "playback.test", "session").unwrap();
        let json_start = config.find("\n{\n").unwrap();
        let config: Value = serde_json::from_str(&config[json_start..]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(config["context.properties"]["core.daemon"], false);
        let modules = config["context.modules"].as_array().unwrap();
        for required in ["protocol-native", "client-node", "adapter"] {
            assert!(modules
                .iter()
                .any(|module| module["name"] == format!("libpipewire-module-{required}")));
        }
        let filter = &modules.last().unwrap()["args"];
        assert!(modules.last().unwrap().get("flags").is_none());
        let graph = &filter["filter.graph"];
        let nodes = graph["nodes"].as_array().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(nodes.len(), 10);
        assert_eq!(
            nodes.iter().filter(|node| node["type"] == "sofa").count(),
            8
        );
        for node in nodes.iter().filter(|node| node["type"] == "sofa") {
            assert_eq!(node["config"]["filename"], path.to_str().unwrap());
        }
        for mixer in nodes.iter().filter(|node| node["label"] == "mixer") {
            assert_eq!(mixer["control"].as_object().unwrap().len(), 8);
            for channel in 1..=8 {
                assert_eq!(mixer["control"][format!("Gain {channel}")], 0.125);
            }
        }
        assert_eq!(graph["links"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 16);
        assert_eq!(graph["inputs"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 8);
        assert_eq!(graph["outputs"], json!(["mixL:Out", "mixR:Out"]));
        assert_eq!(
            filter["capture.props"]["audio.position"],
            json!(["FL", "FR", "FC", "LFE", "RL", "RR", "SL", "SR"])
        );
        let playback = &filter["playback.props"];
        assert_eq!(playback["target.object"], target);
        for property in [
            "node.passive",
            "node.dont-fallback",
            "node.dont-reconnect",
            "stream.dont-remix",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(playback[property], true);
        }
        assert_eq!(playback["audio.position"], json!(["FL", "FR"]));
        assert_eq!(config["context.exec"], json!([]));
    }

    fn record() -> RestoreRecord {
        let id = "4ccf058b-1487-4cda-9b5a-11a6033a5ad0";
        RestoreRecord {
            schema_version: 1,
            session_id: id.into(),
            previous_configured_default: Some("old.headset".into()),
            previous_effective_default: Some("old.headset".into()),
            selected_physical_target: "chosen.headset".into(),
            target_serial: 50,
            virtual_node_name: format!("{APP_NODE_PREFIX}input.{id}"),
            playback_node_name: format!("{APP_NODE_PREFIX}playback.{id}"),
            app_process_id: 2,
            filter_process_id: Some(3),
            virtual_object_serial: Some(51),
            playback_object_serial: Some(52),
            core_cookie: Some(1),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn restore_respects_external_choices_automatic_defaults_and_unplug() {
        let mut record = record();
        for external in [Some("external.choice"), None] {
            assert_eq!(
                restore_decision(
                    &record,
                    external,
                    true,
                    true,
                    Some("speakers"),
                    RestoreIntent::Bypass
                ),
                RestoreAction::KeepExternal
            );
        }
        let owned = Some(record.virtual_node_name.as_str());
        assert_eq!(
            restore_decision(
                &record,
                owned,
                true,
                true,
                Some("speakers"),
                RestoreIntent::Bypass
            ),
            RestoreAction::SetOutput("chosen.headset".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            restore_decision(
                &record,
                owned,
                true,
                true,
                Some("speakers"),
                RestoreIntent::Rollback
            ),
            RestoreAction::SetOutput("old.headset".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            restore_decision(
                &record,
                owned,
                false,
                false,
                Some("speakers"),
                RestoreIntent::TargetLost
            ),
            RestoreAction::SetOutput("speakers".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            restore_decision(&record, owned, false, false, None, RestoreIntent::Recovery),
            RestoreAction::ClearOutputDefault
        );
        record.previous_configured_default = None;
        assert_eq!(
            restore_decision(
                &record,
                Some(&record.virtual_node_name),
                true,
                false,
                None,
                RestoreIntent::Bypass
            ),
            RestoreAction::ClearOutputDefault
        );
        assert_eq!(
            restore_decision(
                &record,
                Some(&record.virtual_node_name),
                true,
                false,
                None,
                RestoreIntent::Recovery
            ),
            RestoreAction::ClearOutputDefault
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_sofa_and_arbitrary_config_references_fail() {
        assert!(validate_sofa_container(b"not a sofa file").is_err());
        let mut bytes = vec![0; 256];
        bytes[..8].copy_from_slice(b"\x89HDF\r\n\x1a\n");
        assert!(validate_sofa_container(&bytes).is_err());
        bytes[32..47].copy_from_slice(b"SOFAConventions");
        assert!(validate_sofa_container(&bytes).is_ok());
        let mut record = record();
        record.virtual_node_name = "external.default".into();
        assert!(record.validate().is_err());
        assert!(validate_hrtf_reference("../../pipewire.conf").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn recovery_record_is_separate_and_unknown_versions_are_preserved() {
        let directory = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let paths = AppPaths {
            config_dir: directory.path().join("config"),
            data_dir: directory.path().join("data"),
            state_dir: directory.path().join("state"),
            runtime_dir: directory.path().join("runtime"),
        };
        let record = record();
        write_restore(&paths, &record).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            read_restore(&paths).unwrap().unwrap().session_id,
            record.session_id
        );
        assert!(!paths.config_dir.exists());
        let file = paths.state_dir.join(RESTORE_FILE);
        let bytes = b"{\"schemaVersion\":999}";
        fs::write(&file, bytes).unwrap();
        assert!(write_restore(&paths, &record).is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read(file).unwrap(), bytes);
    }
}

use peripheral_hub_lib::{
    devices::{self, UsbMetadata},
    models::*,
    providers::{self, headsetcontrol, openrazer, ratbag, upower},
};
use serde::Deserialize;

#[test]
fn usb_association_does_not_choose_first_duplicate_serial_and_verified_wired_hid_merges() {
    let first = usb(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-2",
        Some("SAME"),
        0x1532,
        0x00b8,
        false,
    );
    let second = usb(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-3",
        Some("SAME"),
        0x1532,
        0x00b8,
        false,
    );
    let mut provider = devices::usb_device(&first).unwrap();
    provider.provider = ProviderKind::Openrazer;
    provider.id = "openrazer:SAME".to_owned();
    provider.identity.physical_path = None;
    let mut discovery = Discovery {
        devices: vec![provider],
        diagnostics: Vec::new(),
    };
    devices::merge_usb(&mut discovery, &[first, second]);
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices[0].identity.physical_path, None);
    let wired = usb(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-4",
        None,
        0x046d,
        0xc094,
        false,
    );
    let mut provider = devices::usb_device(&wired).unwrap();
    provider.provider = ProviderKind::Ratbag;
    provider.identity.physical_path = Some(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-4/1-4:1.0/0003:046D:C094.0009".to_owned(),
    );
    let mut discovery = Discovery {
        devices: vec![provider],
        diagnostics: Vec::new(),
    };
    devices::merge_usb(&mut discovery, &[wired]);
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices[0].provider, ProviderKind::Ratbag);
    assert!(discovery.devices[0]
        .identity
        .physical_path
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap()
        .ends_with("0003:046D:C094.0009"));
}

#[test]
fn anonymous_battery_cache_key_changes_on_usb_generation_and_not_enumeration_order() {
    let first = usb(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-2",
        None,
        0x046d,
        0x0aff,
        true,
    );
    let mut changed = first.clone();
    changed.instance.push_str(":replacement");
    assert_ne!(
        headsetcontrol::usb_fingerprint(std::slice::from_ref(&first)),
        headsetcontrol::usb_fingerprint(&[changed])
    );
    let second = usb(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-3",
        None,
        0x046d,
        0x0aff,
        true,
    );
    assert_eq!(
        headsetcontrol::usb_fingerprint(&[first.clone(), second.clone()]),
        headsetcontrol::usb_fingerprint(&[second, first])
    );
}

#[test]
fn empty_introspection_interface_is_valid_but_does_not_invent_capabilities() {
    let api = providers::parse_introspection("<node><interface name='empty'/></node>").unwrap();
    assert!(api.interfaces["empty"].methods.is_empty());
    assert!(!api.method("empty", "getCapabilities", "", "as"));
}

fn identity(serial: Option<&str>, path: Option<&str>, vendor: u16, product: u16) -> DeviceIdentity {
    DeviceIdentity {
        serial: serial.map(str::to_owned),
        physical_path: path.map(str::to_owned),
        vendor_id: Some(vendor),
        product_id: Some(product),
        ..DeviceIdentity::default()
    }
}

fn usb(path: &str, serial: Option<&str>, vendor: u16, product: u16, audio: bool) -> UsbMetadata {
    UsbMetadata {
        identity: identity(serial, Some(path), vendor, product),
        name: "Fixture USB device".to_owned(),
        is_audio: audio,
        bus_alias: devices::usb_bus_alias(path),
        instance: format!("{path}:1:7"),
    }
}

fn audio(
    name: &str,
    serial: Option<&str>,
    path: Option<&str>,
    vendor: u16,
    product: u16,
) -> AudioNode {
    AudioNode {
        id: 42,
        object_serial: Some(900),
        node_name: name.to_owned(),
        description: "Fixture audio".to_owned(),
        media_class: "Audio/Sink".to_owned(),
        channels: vec!["FL".to_owned(), "FR".to_owned()],
        volume: None,
        muted: None,
        is_virtual: false,
        device_serial: serial.map(str::to_owned),
        device_bus_path: path.map(str::to_owned),
        vendor_id: Some(vendor),
        product_id: Some(product),
        transport: Some("usb".to_owned()),
    }
}

#[test]
fn introspection_requires_exact_openrazer_signatures_without_invented_capabilities() {
    let api = providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_openrazer.xml")).unwrap();
    assert!(api.method(openrazer::MISC_INTERFACE, "getVidPid", "", "ai"));
    assert!(api.method(openrazer::DPI_INTERFACE, "setDPI", "qq", ""));
    assert!(!api.method(openrazer::DPI_INTERFACE, "setDPI", "ii", ""));
    assert!(api.method(openrazer::POWER_INTERFACE, "getBattery", "", "d"));
    assert!(!api.method("razer.device.misc", "getCapabilities", "", "as"));
    let manager =
        providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_openrazer_manager.xml")).unwrap();
    assert!(manager.method("razer.devices", "getDevices", "", "as"));
    assert_eq!(
        openrazer::parse_vid_pid(&[0x1532, 0x00b8]).unwrap(),
        (0x1532, 0x00b8)
    );
    assert!(openrazer::parse_vid_pid(&[0x1532, -1]).is_err());
    assert!(openrazer::parse_vid_pid(&[0x046d, 0x4074]).is_err());
    assert!(openrazer::parse_vid_pid(&[0x1532]).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn malformed_or_ambiguous_xml_is_rejected_without_external_entity_expansion() {
    for xml in ["<node><interface name='x'></node>", "<node/><node/>",
        "<!DOCTYPE node [<!ENTITY x SYSTEM 'file:///etc/passwd'>]><node/>",
        "<node><interface name='x'><method name='m'/><method name='m'/></interface></node>",
        "<node><interface name='x'><property name='p' type='u' access='read'/><property name='p' type='u' access='read'/></interface></node>"] {
        assert!(providers::parse_introspection(xml).is_err(), "accepted {xml}");
    }
    assert!(
        providers::parse_introspection(&"x".repeat(providers::MAX_PROVIDER_BYTES + 1)).is_err()
    );
}

#[test]
fn viper_bounds_and_capability_failures_are_independent() {
    let api = providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_openrazer.xml")).unwrap();
    let current = Some(DpiValue { x: 800, y: 1200 });
    let dpi = openrazer::dpi_capability(&api, 0x00b8, Some(30000), None, current).unwrap();
    assert!(dpi.writable && dpi.separate_axes);
    assert!(dpi.values.contains(100) && dpi.values.contains(30000));
    assert!(!dpi.values.contains(99) && !dpi.values.contains(30001));
    let dpi_error = openrazer::dpi_capability(&api, 0x00b8, None, None, None).unwrap();
    let polling =
        openrazer::polling_capability(&api, 0x00b8, Some(vec![125, 500, 1000]), Some(500)).unwrap();
    assert!(!dpi_error.writable && polling.writable);
    assert!(!polling.values.contains(&250));
    assert!(
        !openrazer::polling_capability(&api, 0x00b8, Some(vec![125, 1000, 8000]), Some(1000))
            .unwrap()
            .writable
    );
    assert!(
        !openrazer::dpi_capability(&api, 0x9999, Some(20000), None, current)
            .unwrap()
            .writable
    );
    assert!(
        !openrazer::polling_capability(&api, 0x9999, None, Some(500))
            .unwrap()
            .writable
    );
    assert!(
        openrazer::polling_capability(&api, 0x9999, Some(vec![125, 250, 500]), Some(250))
            .unwrap()
            .writable
    );
    assert_eq!(
        openrazer::parse_dpi(&[800, 1200], false).unwrap(),
        DpiValue { x: 800, y: 1200 }
    );
    assert_eq!(
        openrazer::parse_dpi(&[800, 0], true).unwrap(),
        DpiValue { x: 800, y: 800 }
    );
    assert!(openrazer::parse_dpi(&[800, -1], false).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn razer_double_battery_is_bounded_and_viper_aa_is_never_charging() {
    for value in [-1.0, 100.1, f64::NAN, f64::INFINITY] {
        let battery = openrazer::battery(Some(value), Some(true), 0x00b8, 1000);
        assert_eq!(battery.percentage, None);
        assert_eq!(battery.charging, Some(false));
    }
    assert_eq!(
        openrazer::battery(Some(0.0), None, 0x00b8, 1000).percentage,
        Some(0.0)
    );
    assert_eq!(
        openrazer::battery(Some(64.5), None, 0x9999, 1000).charging,
        None
    );
}

#[test]
fn ratbag_contract_uses_properties_and_exact_api_two() {
    let manager =
        providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_ratbag_manager.xml")).unwrap();
    assert!(manager.property(ratbag::MANAGER_INTERFACE, "APIVersion", "i", false));
    assert!(manager.property(ratbag::MANAGER_INTERFACE, "Devices", "ao", false));
    for invalid in [-1, 0, 1, 3] {
        assert!(ratbag::validate_api_version(invalid).is_err());
    }
    ratbag::validate_api_version(2).unwrap();
    let device =
        providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_ratbag_device.xml")).unwrap();
    assert!(device.method(ratbag::DEVICE_INTERFACE, "Commit", "", "u"));
    assert!(device.signal(ratbag::DEVICE_INTERFACE, "Resync", ""));
    assert!(!device.method(ratbag::DEVICE_INTERFACE, "Refresh", "", ""));
    let profile =
        providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_ratbag_profile.xml")).unwrap();
    assert!(profile.property(ratbag::PROFILE_INTERFACE, "ReportRate", "u", true));
    assert!(profile.property(ratbag::PROFILE_INTERFACE, "ReportRates", "au", false));
    assert!(!profile.property(ratbag::PROFILE_INTERFACE, "ReportRates", "au", true));
    assert!(!profile.property(ratbag::PROFILE_INTERFACE, "Enabled", "b", false));
    let resolution =
        providers::parse_introspection(include_str!("hardware_ratbag_resolution.xml")).unwrap();
    assert!(resolution.property(ratbag::RESOLUTION_INTERFACE, "Resolution", "v", true));
    assert!(!resolution.method(ratbag::RESOLUTION_INTERFACE, "SetDPI", "u", ""));
    assert!(ratbag::parse_model("usb:1532:00b8:1").is_err());
    assert!(ratbag::parse_model("Logitech G305").is_err());
    assert!(ratbag::unsafe_profile_build(
        &ratbag::parse_model("usb:046d:c08b:1").unwrap()
    ));
    assert!(!ratbag::unsafe_profile_build(
        &ratbag::parse_model("usb:046d:4074:1").unwrap()
    ));
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Wire {
    property_signature: String,
    inner_signature: String,
    values: Vec<u32>,
}
impl Wire {
    fn decode(&self) -> ratbag::ResolutionValue {
        ratbag::ResolutionValue::from_wire(
            &self.property_signature,
            &self.inner_signature,
            &self.values,
        )
        .unwrap()
    }
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct RatbagFixture {
    model: String,
    api_version: i32,
    profiles: Vec<ratbag::ProfileFlags>,
    resolutions: Vec<ratbag::ResolutionFlags>,
    dpi_values: Vec<u32>,
    report_rates: Vec<u32>,
    scalar: Wire,
    xy: Wire,
}
fn ratbag_fixture() -> RatbagFixture {
    serde_json::from_str(include_str!("hardware_ratbag_state.json")).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn scalar_and_xy_preserve_inner_shape_and_nonuniform_discrete_values() {
    let fixture = ratbag_fixture();
    ratbag::validate_api_version(fixture.api_version).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        ratbag::parse_model(&fixture.model).unwrap().product_id,
        0x4074
    );
    let allowed = providers::ordered_values(fixture.dpi_values, 300).unwrap();
    let scalar = fixture.scalar.decode();
    let xy = fixture.xy.decode();
    assert_eq!(scalar.inner_signature(), "u");
    assert_eq!(xy.inner_signature(), "(uu)");
    assert_eq!(
        scalar.with_dpi(1200, 1200, &allowed, &[]).unwrap(),
        ratbag::ResolutionValue::Scalar(1200)
    );
    assert!(scalar.with_dpi(800, 1200, &allowed, &[]).is_err());
    assert!(scalar.with_dpi(1600, 1600, &allowed, &[]).is_err());
    assert_eq!(
        xy.with_dpi(1200, 3200, &allowed, &[1]).unwrap(),
        ratbag::ResolutionValue::Xy(1200, 3200)
    );
    assert!(xy.with_dpi(800, 1600, &allowed, &[1]).is_err());
    assert!(xy.with_dpi(800, 800, &allowed, &[]).is_err());
    assert!(ratbag::ResolutionValue::from_wire("u", "u", &[800]).is_err());
    assert!(ratbag::ResolutionValue::from_wire("v", "i", &[800]).is_err());
    assert!(ratbag::ResolutionValue::from_wire("v", "(uu)", &[800]).is_err());
    assert!(providers::ordered_values(vec![400, 400, 800], 300).is_err());
    assert!(providers::ordered_values(vec![0, 800], 300).is_err());
    assert_eq!(
        providers::ordered_values(vec![3200, 400, 1200, 800], 300).unwrap(),
        allowed
    );
    assert!(fixture.report_rates.contains(&250));
}

#[test]
fn active_flags_never_assume_zero_and_dirty_inactive_profile_blocks_writes() {
    let mut fixture = ratbag_fixture();
    assert_eq!(ratbag::active_profile(&fixture.profiles).unwrap(), 1);
    assert_eq!(ratbag::active_resolution(&fixture.resolutions).unwrap(), 1);
    ratbag::clean_profiles(&fixture.profiles).unwrap();
    fixture.profiles[0].is_dirty = Some(true);
    assert_eq!(
        ratbag::clean_profiles(&fixture.profiles).unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::Busy
    );
    fixture.profiles[0].is_dirty = None;
    assert!(ratbag::clean_profiles(&fixture.profiles).is_err());
    fixture.profiles[0].is_active = Some(true);
    assert!(ratbag::active_profile(&fixture.profiles).is_err());
    fixture.resolutions[0].is_active = Some(true);
    assert!(ratbag::active_resolution(&fixture.resolutions).is_err());
    fixture.resolutions[0].is_active = Some(false);
    fixture.resolutions[1].disabled = Some(true);
    assert!(ratbag::active_resolution(&fixture.resolutions).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn queued_commit_and_cached_desired_value_are_never_success_without_completion_signal() {
    let mut evidence = ratbag::CommitEvidence::default();
    evidence.stage_readback(true, true, false);
    evidence.begin_commit();
    evidence.commit_reply(0);
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, true, true));
    evidence.dirty_signal(true, false);
    assert!(evidence.can_confirm(true, true, true));
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(false, true, true));
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, false, true));
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, true, false));
    evidence.resync = true;
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, true, true));
}

#[test]
fn ignored_staging_concurrent_dirty_and_resync_cannot_be_confirmed() {
    for (matches, dirty, other) in [
        (false, true, false),
        (true, false, false),
        (true, true, true),
    ] {
        let mut evidence = ratbag::CommitEvidence::default();
        evidence.stage_readback(matches, dirty, other);
        evidence.begin_commit();
        evidence.commit_reply(0);
        evidence.dirty_signal(true, false);
        assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, true, true));
    }
    let mut evidence = ratbag::CommitEvidence::default();
    evidence.stage_readback(true, true, false);
    evidence.dirty_signal(true, false);
    evidence.begin_commit();
    evidence.commit_reply(0);
    evidence.dirty_signal(true, false);
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, true, true));
    let mut evidence = ratbag::CommitEvidence::default();
    evidence.stage_readback(true, true, false);
    evidence.begin_commit();
    evidence.commit_reply(0);
    evidence.dirty_signal(false, true);
    evidence.dirty_signal(true, false);
    assert!(!evidence.can_confirm(true, true, true));
}

#[test]
fn upower_coarse_stale_unknown_and_zero_percent_remain_distinct() {
    let fixtures: Vec<upower::BatteryProperties> =
        serde_json::from_str(include_str!("hardware_upower.json")).unwrap();
    let samples: Vec<_> = fixtures.iter().map(|p| upower::battery(p, 1000)).collect();
    assert_eq!(samples[0].percentage, Some(64.2));
    assert_eq!(samples[0].source_updated_at, Some(990));
    assert_eq!(samples[0].observed_at, 1000);
    assert!(!samples[0].stale);
    assert_eq!(samples[1].percentage, None);
    assert_eq!(samples[1].level, Some(BatteryLevel::Normal));
    assert_eq!(samples[2].level, Some(BatteryLevel::Full));
    assert!(samples[2].stale);
    assert_eq!(samples[3].percentage, None);
    assert_eq!(samples[3].charging, None);
    assert_eq!(samples[3].source_updated_at, None);
    assert!(samples[3].stale);
    assert_eq!(samples[4].percentage, None);
    assert_eq!(samples[5].percentage, None);
    assert!(samples[5].stale);
    assert_eq!(samples[6].level, Some(BatteryLevel::Critical));
    assert_eq!(samples[7].percentage, Some(0.0));
    assert!(upower::compatible_type(5, DeviceKind::Mouse));
    assert!(!upower::compatible_type(2, DeviceKind::Mouse));
    assert!(!upower::compatible_type(17, DeviceKind::Mouse));
}

#[test]
fn headsetcontrol_json_validates_api_readability_status_and_partial_records() {
    let report =
        headsetcontrol::parse_json(include_bytes!("hardware_headsetcontrol.json"), 1000).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(report.api_version.as_deref(), Some("1.9"));
    assert_eq!(report.devices[0].battery.percentage, Some(64.0));
    assert_eq!(report.devices[0].battery.charging, Some(false));
    assert_eq!(report.devices[1].battery.percentage, Some(72.0));
    assert_eq!(report.devices[1].battery.charging, Some(true));
    for sample in &report.devices[2..] {
        assert_eq!(sample.battery.percentage, None);
        assert_eq!(sample.battery.charging, None);
    }
    let unknown = include_str!("hardware_headsetcontrol.json").replace("1.9", "99.0");
    assert!(headsetcontrol::parse_json(unknown.as_bytes(), 1000)
        .unwrap()
        .devices
        .iter()
        .all(|d| d.battery.percentage.is_none()));
    assert!(headsetcontrol::parse_json(b"not JSON", 1000).is_err());
    assert!(
        headsetcontrol::parse_json(&vec![b' '; providers::MAX_PROVIDER_BYTES + 1], 1000).is_err()
    );
}

#[test]
fn identity_never_uses_name_or_vid_pid_and_duplicate_serial_is_ambiguous() {
    let a = identity(None, None, 0x046d, 0x0aff);
    assert!(!providers::identities_match(&a, &a));
    let a = identity(Some("SERIAL_A"), None, 0x046d, 0x0aff);
    assert_eq!(
        providers::unique_identity_match(&a, &[a.clone(), a.clone()]),
        None
    );
    let first = identity(
        Some("SERIAL_A"),
        Some("/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-2"),
        0x046d,
        0x0aff,
    );
    let second = identity(
        Some("SERIAL_A"),
        Some("/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-3"),
        0x046d,
        0x0aff,
    );
    assert!(!providers::identities_match(&first, &second));
    assert_eq!(
        providers::unique_identity_match(&first, &[first.clone(), second]),
        Some(0)
    );
    let changed = identity(
        Some("SERIAL_B"),
        first.physical_path.as_deref(),
        0x046d,
        0x0aff,
    );
    assert!(!providers::identities_match(&first, &changed));
}

#[test]
fn duplicate_headsets_do_not_receive_arbitrary_battery_or_vendor_audio_mapping() {
    let usb = vec![
        usb(
            "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-2",
            None,
            0x046d,
            0x0aff,
            true,
        ),
        usb(
            "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-3",
            None,
            0x046d,
            0x0aff,
            true,
        ),
    ];
    let mut discovery = Discovery::default();
    devices::merge_usb(&mut discovery, &usb);
    let report =
        headsetcontrol::parse_json(include_bytes!("hardware_headsetcontrol.json"), 1000).unwrap();
    devices::associate_headset_batteries(&mut discovery, &usb, &report);
    assert!(discovery.devices.iter().all(|d| d.values.battery.is_none()));
    devices::associate_audio(
        &mut discovery,
        &[
            audio("alsa_output.a", None, None, 0x046d, 0x0aff),
            audio("alsa_output.b", None, None, 0x046d, 0x0aff),
        ],
        &usb,
    );
    assert_eq!(
        discovery
            .devices
            .iter()
            .filter(|d| d.brand == Brand::Generic)
            .count(),
        2
    );
}

#[test]
fn verified_physical_audio_mapping_avoids_generic_duplicate_and_single_usb_battery_associates() {
    let usb = vec![usb(
        "/sys/devices/pci0000:00/0000:00:14.0/usb1/1-2",
        Some("HEADSET_A"),
        0x046d,
        0x0aff,
        true,
    )];
    let mut discovery = Discovery::default();
    devices::merge_usb(&mut discovery, &usb);
    let id = discovery.devices[0].id.clone();
    let report =
        headsetcontrol::parse_json(include_bytes!("hardware_headsetcontrol.json"), 1000).unwrap();
    devices::associate_headset_batteries(&mut discovery, &usb, &report);
    devices::associate_audio(
        &mut discovery,
        &[audio(
            "alsa_output.a",
            None,
            Some("pci-0000:00:14.0-usb-0:2:1.0"),
            0x046d,
            0x0aff,
        )],
        &usb,
    );
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices[0].id, id);
    assert_eq!(discovery.devices[0].output_node_names, ["alsa_output.a"]);
    assert_eq!(
        discovery.devices[0]
            .values
            .battery
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .percentage,
        Some(64.0)
    );
    assert!(devices::verified_usb_path("pci-0000:00:14.0-usb-0:20:1.0", &usb).is_none());
    assert!(devices::usb_physical_path("/sys/devices/../usb1/1-2").is_none());
}

#[test]
fn receivers_and_proposed_blackshark_ids_are_metadata_only_not_mouse_write_owners() {
    let receiver =
        devices::usb_device(&usb("/sys/devices/usb1/1-2", None, 0x046d, 0xc547, false)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(receiver.kind, DeviceKind::Receiver);
    assert!(receiver.capabilities.dpi.is_none());
    let hyper =
        devices::usb_device(&usb("/sys/devices/usb1/1-3", None, 0x1532, 0x00b3, false)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(hyper.kind, DeviceKind::Receiver);
    let blackshark =
        devices::usb_device(&usb("/sys/devices/usb1/1-4", None, 0x1532, 0x057d, true)).unwrap();
    assert!(blackshark.values.battery.is_none());
    assert!(blackshark.capabilities.dpi.is_none());
    assert!(blackshark.limitations.iter().any(|l| l.contains("#2899")));
    assert!(blackshark.limitations.iter().any(|l| l.contains("dongle")));
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
#[tokio::test]
async fn unsupported_platform_never_falls_back_to_simulated_hardware() {
    let discovery = devices::discover(&[], true).await;
    assert!(discovery.devices.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(
        discovery.diagnostics[0].code,
        AvailabilityCode::UnsupportedPlatform
    );
    let device = devices::usb_device(&usb(
        "/sys/devices/usb1/1-2",
        Some("A"),
        0x1532,
        0x00b8,
        false,
    ))
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        devices::set_dpi(&device, 800, 800).await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform
    );
    assert_eq!(
        devices::set_polling_rate(&device, 500)
            .await
            .unwrap_err()
            .code,
        ErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform
    );
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
fn properties_set_serialization_keeps_outer_property_variant_and_scalar_xy() {
    use zbus::zvariant::{serialized::Context, to_bytes, OwnedValue, LE};
    for resolution in [
        ratbag::ResolutionValue::Scalar(800),
        ratbag::ResolutionValue::Xy(800, 1200),
    ] {
        let body = (
            ratbag::RESOLUTION_INTERFACE,
            "Resolution",
            ratbag::resolution_set_value(resolution),
        );
        let bytes = to_bytes(Context::new_dbus(LE, 0), &body).unwrap();
        let ((interface, name, value), _): ((String, String, OwnedValue), _) =
            bytes.deserialize().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(interface, ratbag::RESOLUTION_INTERFACE);
        assert_eq!(name, "Resolution");
        assert_eq!(ratbag::decode_resolution(&value).unwrap(), resolution);
    }
    assert!(ratbag::decode_resolution(&OwnedValue::from(800u32)).is_err());
}
